    <content type="application/xml">
      <RuleDescription xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/netservices/2010/10/servicebus/connect" xmlns:i="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance">
        <Filter i:type="CorrelationFilter">
          <CorrelationId>batch-7</CorrelationId>
          <Label>priority</Label>
          <Properties>
            <KeyValueOfstringanyType><Key>region</Key><Value xmlns:d6p1="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema" i:type="d6p1:string">eu</Value></KeyValueOfstringanyType>
            <KeyValueOfstringanyType><Key>tier</Key><Value xmlns:d6p1="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema" i:type="d6p1:int">2</Value></KeyValueOfstringanyType>
          </Properties>
        </Filter>
        <Action i:type="EmptyRuleAction"/>
        <Name>priority</Name>
      </RuleDescription>
    </content>
  </entry>
</feed>

`crates/dispatched-core/src/mgmt/details.rs`:

```rust
//! One entity's description: every property the service reports, for an Overview.

use super::models::{EntityCounts, counts};
use super::xml::Node;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Queue, topic or subscription.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum EntityKind {
    /// A queue.
    Queue,
    /// A topic.
    Topic,
    /// A topic subscription.
    Subscription,
}

impl EntityKind {
    fn element(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            EntityKind::Queue => "QueueDescription",
            EntityKind::Topic => "TopicDescription",
            EntityKind::Subscription => "SubscriptionDescription",
        }
    }

    /// `queue`, `topic`, `subscription`.
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            EntityKind::Queue => "queue",
            EntityKind::Topic => "topic",
            EntityKind::Subscription => "subscription",
        }
    }
}

/// A property as the service reports it (element name and text).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct EntityProperty {
    /// Element name, e.g. `LockDuration`.
    pub name: String,
    /// Text, e.g. `PT1M`.
    pub value: String,
}

/// An entity's description.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct EntityDetails {
    /// What it is.
    pub kind: EntityKind,
    /// Its name (the subscription name for a subscription).
    pub name: String,
    /// The topic of a subscription.
    pub topic: Option<String>,
    /// `Active`, `Disabled`, `SendDisabled`, `ReceiveDisabled`.
    pub status: Option<String>,
    /// Counts; `None` when not reported (topics, the emulator).
    pub counts: Option<EntityCounts>,
    /// Size in bytes.
    pub size_in_bytes: Option<u64>,
    /// Every other non-empty leaf, in document order.
    pub properties: Vec<EntityProperty>,
}

/// Nested parts that are not plain properties (counts are their own field; rules hold key names).
const SKIPPED: &[&str] = &["CountDetails", "AuthorizationRules"];

pub(crate) fn details_from_entry(kind: EntityKind, topic: Option<&str>, entry: &Node) -> Option<EntityDetails> {
    let name = entry.text_of("title")?.to_string();
    let d = entry.path(&["content", kind.element()])?;
    let properties = d
        .children
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.children.is_empty() && !c.text.is_empty() && !SKIPPED.contains(&c.name.as_str()))
        .map(|c| EntityProperty { name: c.name.clone(), value: c.text.clone() })
        .collect();
    Some(EntityDetails {
        kind,
        name,
        topic: topic.map(str::to_string),
        status: d.text_of("Status").map(str::to_string),
        counts: counts(d),
        size_in_bytes: d.text_of("SizeInBytes").and_then(|t| t.parse().ok()),
        properties,
    })
}
```

`crates/dispatched-core/src/mgmt/rules.rs`:

```rust
//! Subscription rules (read-only in M1c).

use super::xml::Node;
use serde::Serialize;

/// A user property a correlation filter matches.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct CorrelationProperty {
    /// Property name.
    pub name: String,
    /// Expected value as text.
    pub value: String,
}

/// A rule's filter. `TrueFilter`/`FalseFilter` arrive as SQL `1=1` / `1=0`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum RuleFilter {
    /// SQL filter expression.
    Sql {
        /// The expression.
        expression: String,
    },
    /// Correlation filter: every set field must equal the message's.
    Correlation {
        correlation_id: Option<String>,
        message_id: Option<String>,
        to: Option<String>,
        reply_to: Option<String>,
        /// `Label` on the wire.
        subject: Option<String>,
        session_id: Option<String>,
        reply_to_session_id: Option<String>,
        content_type: Option<String>,
        properties: Vec<CorrelationProperty>,
    },
}

/// A rule of a subscription.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct RuleInfo {
    /// Rule name.
    pub name: String,
    /// Its filter.
    pub filter: RuleFilter,
    /// SQL action, if any.
    pub action: Option<String>,
    /// Creation time as the service wrote it.
    pub created_at: Option<String>,
}

fn text(n: &Node, name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    n.text_of(name).map(str::to_string)
}

pub(crate) fn rule_from_entry(entry: &Node) -> Option<RuleInfo> {
    let name = entry.text_of("title")?.to_string();
    let d = entry.path(&["content", "RuleDescription"])?;
    // The element's i:type attribute is not kept by the XML reader; SQL-shaped filters
    // (SqlFilter, TrueFilter, FalseFilter) carry SqlExpression, correlation filters do not.
    let filter = match d.child("Filter") {
        Some(f) => match f.text_of("SqlExpression") {
            Some(expression) => RuleFilter::Sql { expression: expression.to_string() },
            None => RuleFilter::Correlation {
                correlation_id: text(f, "CorrelationId"),
                message_id: text(f, "MessageId"),
                to: text(f, "To"),
                reply_to: text(f, "ReplyTo"),
                subject: text(f, "Label"),
                session_id: text(f, "SessionId"),
                reply_to_session_id: text(f, "ReplyToSessionId"),
                content_type: text(f, "ContentType"),
                properties: f
                    .child("Properties")
                    .map(|p| {
                        p.children_named("KeyValueOfstringanyType")
                            .filter_map(|kv| {
                                Some(CorrelationProperty {
                                    name: kv.text_of("Key")?.to_string(),
                                    value: kv.child("Value").map(|v| v.text.clone()).unwrap_or_default(),
                                })
                            })
                            .collect()
                    })
                    .unwrap_or_default(),
            },
        },
        None => RuleFilter::Sql { expression: "1=1".into() },
    };
    Some(RuleInfo {
        name,
        filter,
        action: d.child("Action").and_then(|a| a.text_of("SqlExpression")).map(str::to_string),
        created_at: text(d, "CreatedAt"),
    })
}
```

```rust
mod details;
mod rules;
pub use details::{EntityDetails, EntityKind, EntityProperty};
pub use rules::{CorrelationProperty, RuleFilter, RuleInfo};

impl ManagementClient {
    /// One entity's description. `path` is a queue or topic name, or `topic/subscriptions/name`.
    /// A missing entity, or one of another kind, is `NotFound`.
    pub async fn entity_details(&self, kind: EntityKind, path: &str) -> Result<EntityDetails> {
        let (url_path, topic) = match kind {
            EntityKind::Queue | EntityKind::Topic => (segment(path), None),
            EntityKind::Subscription => {
                let e = crate::EntityPath::parse(path)?;
                let sub = e.subscription.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
                    Error::Invalid(format!("`{path}` is not a subscription; use `topic/subscriptions/name`"))
                })?;
                (format!("{}/Subscriptions/{}", segment(&e.entity), segment(&sub)), Some(e.entity))
            }
        };
        let root = self.get(&url_path, &[("enrich", "true")]).await?;
        entries(&root)
            .into_iter()
            .find_map(|e| details::details_from_entry(kind, topic.as_deref(), e))
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::NotFound(format!("{} `{path}`", kind.as_str())))
    }

    /// The rules of `topic`'s subscription `subscription`.
    pub async fn list_rules(&self, topic: &str, subscription: &str) -> Result<Vec<RuleInfo>> {
        self.list(&format!("{}/Subscriptions/{}/Rules", segment(topic), segment(subscription)), rules::rule_from_entry)
            .await
    }
}

```rust
    /// One entity's description; missing queue or subscription counts are counted by peeking
    /// (approximate), as in the listings.
    pub async fn entity_details(&self, kind: EntityKind, path: &str) -> Result<EntityDetails> {
        let mut d = self.mgmt.entity_details(kind, path).await?;
        if d.counts.is_none() && kind != EntityKind::Topic {
            let entity = EntityPath::parse(path)?;
            match self.count_by_peek(&entity, Self::COUNT_CAP).await {
                Ok(c) => d.counts = Some(c),
                Err(e) => tracing::debug!(entity = %path, error = %e, "approximate count failed"),
            }
        }
        Ok(d)
    }

    /// The rules of a subscription.
    pub async fn list_rules(&self, topic: &str, subscription: &str) -> Result<Vec<RuleInfo>> {
        self.mgmt.list_rules(topic, subscription).await
    }

### Task 5: Core: sessions listing, test seeding for sessions, and the M1c emulator gate

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/dispatched-core/src/messaging/sessions.rs`, `crates/dispatched-core/tests/emulator_m1c.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dispatched-core/src/messaging/mod.rs`, `crates/dispatched-core/src/messaging/scan.rs` (`walk_messages`, `count_messages` on top of it), `crates/dispatched-core/src/messaging/testing.rs`, `crates/dispatched-core/src/explorer.rs`, `crates/dispatched-core/src/lib.rs`, `crates/dispatched-core/Cargo.toml` (`[[test]] name = "emulator_m1c"`), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (emulator job runs three gate tests)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`PeekSource`, `scan_messages`, `peek_tail`, `peek_message`, `collect_sequences`), Task 4 (`entity_details`).
- Produces:
  - `sessions::list_request(skip: i32, top: i32)`, `sessions::parse_list_response(MgmtResponse) -> Result<Vec<String>>`, `sessions::list_session_ids(conn, address) -> Result<Vec<String>>` (pages of 100, at most `MAX_SESSIONS = 10_000`)
  - `sessions::SessionTally`, `sessions::tally_sessions<S: PeekSource + ?Sized>(src, address, cap: u32) -> Result<SessionTally>`
  - `sessions::SessionSummary { id, messages: Option<u64>, first_enqueued: Option<OffsetDateTime>, last_enqueued: Option<OffsetDateTime> }` (RFC 3339 in JSON)
  - `sessions::SessionListing { sessions: Vec<SessionSummary>, from_broker: bool, scanned: u64, truncated: bool }`
  - `sessions::merge(broker: Option<Vec<String>>, tally: Option<SessionTally>) -> SessionListing` (sorted by id)
  - `sessions::combine(broker: Result<Vec<String>>, tally: Result<SessionTally>) -> Result<SessionListing>` (an error only when both failed: the broker's)
  - `scan::walk_messages(src, address, cap, visit) -> Result<Walked { examined, truncated }>` (`pub(crate)`), shared by `count_messages` and `tally_sessions`
  - `Explorer::list_sessions(&self, entity: &EntityPath) -> Result<SessionListing>` (fails only when both sources fail, with the broker's error; the tally runs over `PerBatch`)
  - `testing::send_session_text(conn, address, session_id, body, props)` (feature `test-support`)

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::messaging::{PeekedMessage, test_message};
    use async_trait::async_trait;
    use fe2o3_amqp::types::primitives::Array;

    fn resp(status: u16, body: Value) -> MgmtResponse {
        MgmtResponse { status, description: None, body }
    }

    #[test]
    fn request_asks_from_the_epoch_with_paging() {
        let b = list_request(100, 100);
        assert!(matches!(b.get("last-updated-time"), Some(Value::Timestamp(t)) if t.milliseconds() == 0));
        assert_eq!(b.get("skip"), Some(&Value::Int(100)));
        assert_eq!(b.get("top"), Some(&Value::Int(100)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn response_ids_come_from_an_array_or_a_list() {
        let mut map = OrderedMap::new();
        map.insert(Value::String("sessions-ids".into()), Value::Array(Array(vec![Value::String("a".into()), Value::String("b".into())])));
        assert_eq!(parse_list_response(resp(200, Value::Map(map))).unwrap(), ["a", "b"]);
        let mut map = OrderedMap::new();
        map.insert(Value::String("sessions-ids".into()), Value::List(vec![Value::String("c".into())]));
        assert_eq!(parse_list_response(resp(200, Value::Map(map))).unwrap(), ["c"]);
        assert!(parse_list_response(resp(204, Value::Null)).unwrap().is_empty());
        assert!(matches!(parse_list_response(resp(403, Value::Null)).unwrap_err(), Error::Forbidden { .. }));
    }

    struct Fake(Vec<PeekedMessage>);

    #[async_trait]
    impl PeekSource for Fake {
        async fn peek_batch(&mut self, _a: &str, from: i64, max: u32) -> Result<Vec<PeekedMessage>> {
            Ok(self.0.iter().filter(|m| m.sequence_number >= from).take(max as usize).cloned().collect())
        }
    }

    fn msg(seq: i64, session: Option<&str>, minute: i64) -> PeekedMessage {
        let mut m = test_message(seq);
        m.session_id = session.map(str::to_string);
        m.enqueued_time = Some(time::OffsetDateTime::UNIX_EPOCH + time::Duration::minutes(minute));
        m
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn tally_counts_per_session_with_first_and_last_times() {
        let mut src = Fake(vec![msg(1, Some("b"), 5), msg(2, Some("a"), 1), msg(3, None, 2), msg(4, Some("b"), 9)]);
        let t = tally_sessions(&mut src, "q", 100).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(t.scanned, 4);
        assert!(!t.truncated);
        let b = &t.per_session["b"];
        assert_eq!(b.messages, 2);
        assert_eq!(b.first.unwrap().minute(), 5);
        assert_eq!(b.last.unwrap().minute(), 9);
        let capped = tally_sessions(&mut src, "q", 2).await.unwrap();
        assert!(capped.truncated);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn merge_keeps_broker_sessions_without_messages() {
        let mut src = Fake(vec![msg(1, Some("b"), 1)]);
        let tally = tally_sessions(&mut src, "q", 100).await.unwrap();
        let l = merge(Some(vec!["c".into(), "b".into()]), Some(tally));
        assert!(l.from_broker);
        let ids: Vec<&str> = l.sessions.iter().map(|s| s.id.as_str()).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, ["b", "c"]);
        assert_eq!(l.sessions[0].messages, Some(1));
        assert_eq!(l.sessions[1].messages, Some(0), "known to the broker, no messages in the scan");
        let only_tally = merge(None, Some(tally_sessions(&mut src, "q", 100).await.unwrap()));
        assert!(!only_tally.from_broker);
        let only_broker = merge(Some(vec!["x".into()]), None);
        assert_eq!(only_broker.sessions[0].messages, None, "not counted");
    }

    #[test]
    fn list_fails_only_when_both_sources_fail() {
        let broker_err = || Err(Error::Network("down".into()));
        let tally_err = || Err(Error::Protocol("refused".into()));
        assert!(combine(Ok(vec!["a".into()]), tally_err()).unwrap().from_broker);
        assert!(!combine(broker_err(), Ok(SessionTally::default())).unwrap().from_broker);
        assert!(matches!(combine(broker_err(), tally_err()).unwrap_err(), Error::Network(_)), "the broker's error wins");
    }
}

`crates/dispatched-core/tests/emulator_m1c.rs`:

```rust
//! M1c emulator gate: tail peeks, single-message reads, selected sequences, sessions
//! (listing and per-session peeks), entity details. Per-run ids keep leftovers harmless.
//! Run: `testing/emulator/emulator.sh up && DISPATCHED_EMULATOR=1 cargo test -p dispatched-core --features test-support --test emulator_m1c -- --nocapture`

use dispatched_core::filter::{FilterSpec, MessageFilter};
use dispatched_core::messaging::{ServiceBusConnection, testing};
use dispatched_core::{ConnectionString, EntityKind, EntityPath, Explorer, Namespace, PeekOptions, PeekView};

const EMULATOR: &str = "Endpoint=sb://localhost;SharedAccessKeyName=RootManageSharedAccessKey;SharedAccessKey=SAS_KEY_VALUE;UseDevelopmentEmulator=true;";

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread")]
async fn m1c_tail_sequences_sessions_details() {
    if std::env::var("DISPATCHED_EMULATOR").as_deref() != Ok("1") {
        // CI greps for this line to fail the job if the gate was skipped.
        eprintln!("skipped: set DISPATCHED_EMULATOR=1 and start testing/emulator/emulator.sh up");
        return;
    }
    let ns = Namespace::from_connection_string(&ConnectionString::parse(EMULATOR).unwrap()).unwrap();
    let run = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
    let explorer = Explorer::new(ns.clone()).unwrap();
    let mut conn = ServiceBusConnection::open(&ns).await.unwrap();

    // Plain queue: five messages of this run.
    for i in 0..5 {
        testing::send_text(&mut conn, "m1a-browse", &format!("{{\"run\":\"{run}\",\"i\":{i}}}"), &[("run", &run), ("i", &i.to_string())])
            .await
            .unwrap();
    }
    // Session queue: two in session A, one in session B.
    let (a, b) = (format!("{run}-a"), format!("{run}-b"));
    for (s, i) in [(&a, 0), (&a, 1), (&b, 2)] {
        testing::send_session_text(&mut conn, "sessions-q", s, &format!("{{\"run\":\"{run}\",\"i\":{i}}}"), &[("run", &run)])
            .await
            .unwrap();
    }
    conn.close().await.unwrap();

    let q = EntityPath::queue("m1a-browse");
    let ours = FilterSpec { property: Some(format!("run = '{run}'")), ..Default::default() }.build().unwrap();

    // From the end: the last three of this run, oldest first.
    let tail = explorer
        .peek_tail(&q, PeekView::Active, &PeekOptions::new(3).scan_limit(100_000).filter(ours.clone()))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let is: Vec<String> = tail.messages.iter().map(|m| m.application_properties["i"].as_str().unwrap().to_string()).collect();
    assert_eq!(is, ["2", "3", "4"], "tail of this run");

    // One message by sequence number, and a gone one.
    let seq = tail.messages[0].sequence_number;
    assert_eq!(explorer.peek_message(&q, PeekView::Active, seq).await.unwrap().unwrap().sequence_number, seq);
    assert!(explorer.peek_message(&q, PeekView::DeadLetter, seq).await.unwrap().is_none(), "not in the DLQ");

    // Selected sequences.
    let picked: Vec<i64> = tail.messages.iter().map(|m| m.sequence_number).collect();
    let c = explorer.collect_sequences(&q, PeekView::Active, &picked, 100_000, None).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(c.messages.len(), 3);
    assert!(c.missing.is_empty());

    // Sessions: listing (broker, peek tally, or both) and a per-session peek.
    let sq = EntityPath::queue("sessions-q");
    let listing = explorer.list_sessions(&sq).await.unwrap();
    eprintln!("GATE-NOTE m1c sessions: from_broker={} scanned={} count={}", listing.from_broker, listing.scanned, listing.sessions.len());
    for id in [&a, &b] {
        assert!(listing.sessions.iter().any(|s| &s.id == id), "session {id} listed");
    }
    let in_a = MessageFilter { session_id: Some(a.clone()), ..Default::default() };
    let page = explorer.peek(&sq, PeekView::Active, &PeekOptions::new(10).filter(in_a)).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(page.messages.len(), 2, "session A's messages");
    assert!(page.messages.iter().all(|m| m.session_id.as_deref() == Some(a.as_str())));

    // Details.
    let d = explorer.entity_details(EntityKind::Queue, "m1a-browse").await.unwrap();
    assert!(d.properties.iter().any(|p| p.name == "MaxDeliveryCount" && p.value == "10"), "{:?}", d.properties);
    assert!(d.counts.is_some(), "reported or counted by peeking");
    match explorer.list_rules("events", "all").await {
        Ok(rules) => eprintln!("GATE-NOTE m1c rules on events/all: {:?}", rules.iter().map(|r| &r.name).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
        Err(e) => eprintln!("GATE-NOTE m1c rules unsupported on the emulator: {e}"),
    }
    explorer.close().await;
}
```

`crates/dispatched-core/src/messaging/sessions.rs` (above the tests):

```rust
//! Sessions of a session-enabled entity: the broker's list plus a peek tally.

use super::connection::ServiceBusConnection;
use super::management::MgmtResponse;
use super::scan::{PeekSource, walk_messages};
use crate::{Error, Result};
use fe2o3_amqp::types::primitives::{OrderedMap, Timestamp, Value};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use time::OffsetDateTime;

const OPERATION: &str = "com.microsoft:get-message-sessions";
const PAGE: i32 = 100;
/// Most session ids read from the broker.
pub const MAX_SESSIONS: usize = 10_000;

/// Request body: sessions active or with state changed since the Unix epoch (so empty
/// sessions with state are included where the broker allows it).
pub fn list_request(skip: i32, top: i32) -> OrderedMap<String, Value> {
    let mut body = OrderedMap::new();
    body.insert("last-updated-time".to_string(), Value::Timestamp(Timestamp::from(OffsetDateTime::UNIX_EPOCH)));
    body.insert("skip".to_string(), Value::Int(skip));
    body.insert("top".to_string(), Value::Int(top));
    body
}

/// Session ids from a response; `204` means none.
pub fn parse_list_response(resp: MgmtResponse) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    match resp.status {
        204 => return Ok(Vec::new()),
        200 => {}
        _ => return Err(resp.error(OPERATION)),
    }
    let Value::Map(map) = &resp.body else { return Ok(Vec::new()) };
    let items: Vec<&Value> = match map.get(&Value::String("sessions-ids".into())) {
        None | Some(Value::Null) => return Ok(Vec::new()),
        Some(Value::Array(a)) => a.0.iter().collect(),
        Some(Value::List(l)) => l.iter().collect(),
        Some(other) => return Err(Error::Protocol(format!("sessions: unexpected `sessions-ids` value {other:?}"))),
    };
    Ok(items
        .into_iter()
        .filter_map(|v| match v {
            Value::String(s) => Some(s.clone()),
            Value::Symbol(s) => Some(s.0.clone()),
            _ => None,
        })
        .collect())
}

/// Every session id the broker lists for `address`, up to [`MAX_SESSIONS`].
pub async fn list_session_ids(conn: &mut ServiceBusConnection, address: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    loop {
        let skip = i32::try_from(out.len()).unwrap_or(i32::MAX);
        let link = conn.management(address).await?;
        let page = parse_list_response(link.call(OPERATION, list_request(skip, PAGE)).await?)?;
        let n = page.len();
        out.extend(page);
        if n < PAGE as usize || out.len() >= MAX_SESSIONS {
            out.truncate(MAX_SESSIONS);
            return Ok(out);
        }
    }
}

/// Messages of one session seen by a tally.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SessionCount {
    /// Messages (any state) in the main queue.
    pub messages: u64,
    /// Earliest enqueued time seen.
    pub first: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
    /// Latest enqueued time seen.
    pub last: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
}

/// Messages per session in the first `cap` messages of an entity.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SessionTally {
    /// By session id.
    pub per_session: BTreeMap<String, SessionCount>,
    /// Messages examined.
    pub scanned: u64,
    /// More messages exist beyond the cap.
    pub truncated: bool,
}

/// Tally messages per session by peeking `address`, examining at most `cap` (plus one to
/// learn whether more exist). Uses the same walker as `count_messages`, so stale and
/// unsequenced messages skip or end the tally exactly as they do a count.
pub async fn tally_sessions<S: PeekSource + ?Sized>(src: &mut S, address: &str, cap: u32) -> Result<SessionTally> {
    let mut per_session: BTreeMap<String, SessionCount> = BTreeMap::new();
    let walked = walk_messages(src, address, cap, |m| {
        if let Some(id) = &m.session_id {
            let c = per_session.entry(id.clone()).or_default();
            c.messages += 1;
            if let Some(at) = m.enqueued_time {
                c.first = Some(c.first.map_or(at, |f| f.min(at)));
                c.last = Some(c.last.map_or(at, |l| l.max(at)));
            }
        }
    })
    .await?;
    Ok(SessionTally { per_session, scanned: walked.examined, truncated: walked.truncated })
}

/// One session in a listing.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct SessionSummary {
    /// Session id.
    pub id: String,
    /// Messages seen by the tally (`None` when no tally ran).
    pub messages: Option<u64>,
    /// Earliest enqueued time seen.
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339::option")]
    pub first_enqueued: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
    /// Latest enqueued time seen.
    #[serde(with = "time::serde::rfc3339::option")]
    pub last_enqueued: Option<OffsetDateTime>,
}

/// Sessions of an entity.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct SessionListing {
    /// Sorted by id.
    pub sessions: Vec<SessionSummary>,
    /// The broker's list was available.
    pub from_broker: bool,
    /// Messages the tally examined.
    pub scanned: u64,
    /// The tally hit its cap; message counts are lower bounds.
    pub truncated: bool,
}

/// Combine what the broker and the tally know.
pub fn merge(broker: Option<Vec<String>>, tally: Option<SessionTally>) -> SessionListing {
    let counted = tally.is_some();
    let t = tally.unwrap_or_default();
    let mut ids: std::collections::BTreeSet<String> = t.per_session.keys().cloned().collect();
    let from_broker = broker.is_some();
    ids.extend(broker.unwrap_or_default());
    let sessions = ids
        .into_iter()
        .map(|id| {
            let c = t.per_session.get(&id).copied().unwrap_or_default();
            SessionSummary { messages: counted.then_some(c.messages), first_enqueued: c.first, last_enqueued: c.last, id }
        })
        .collect();
    SessionListing { sessions, from_broker, scanned: t.scanned, truncated: t.truncated }
}

/// The listing from both attempts; an error only when both failed (the broker's).
pub fn combine(broker: Result<Vec<String>>, tally: Result<SessionTally>) -> Result<SessionListing> {
    match (broker, tally) {
        (Err(b), Err(_)) => Err(b),
        (b, t) => {
            if let Err(e) = &b {
                tracing::debug!(error = %e, "broker session list unavailable; using the peek tally");
            }
            if let Err(e) = &t {
                tracing::debug!(error = %e, "session tally unavailable; using the broker list");
            }
            Ok(merge(b.ok(), t.ok()))
        }
    }
}
```

`messaging/mod.rs`: `pub mod sessions;`.

`messaging/scan.rs`: one walker for `count_messages` and `tally_sessions` (L8: the tally is not a fourth copy of the scan loop). `count_messages` keeps its signature, doc and tests; its body becomes a call to the walker:

```rust
/// What [`walk_messages`] examined.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Walked {
    /// Messages handed to the visitor.
    pub examined: u64,
    /// A message exists beyond the cap.
    pub truncated: bool,
}

/// Hand every message of `address` to `visit`, from the start, examining at most `cap` (and
/// asking for one more to learn whether anything lies beyond it). Stale messages (below the
/// current position) are skipped; a message without a sequence number or a batch that makes
/// no progress ends the walk, so it always terminates.
pub(crate) async fn walk_messages<S: PeekSource + ?Sized>(
    src: &mut S,
    address: &str,
    cap: u32,
    mut visit: impl FnMut(&PeekedMessage) + Send,
) -> Result<Walked> {
    let mut w = Walked::default();
    let mut next = 0i64;
    'outer: loop {
        let want = (u64::from(cap).saturating_sub(w.examined)).min(u64::from(MAX_PER_REQUEST)).max(1) as u32;
        let batch = src.peek_batch(address, next, want).await?;
        if batch.is_empty() {
            break;
        }
        let mut advanced = false;
        for m in &batch {
            if m.sequence_number < 0 {
                break 'outer;
            }
            if m.sequence_number < next {
                continue;
            }
            if w.examined >= u64::from(cap) {
                w.truncated = true;
                break 'outer;
            }
            w.examined += 1;
            next = m.sequence_number + 1;
            advanced = true;
            visit(m);
        }
        if !advanced {
            break;
        }
    }
    Ok(w)
}

pub async fn count_messages(src: &mut (impl PeekSource + ?Sized), address: &str, cap: u32) -> Result<StateCounts> {
    let mut counts = StateCounts::default();
    let walked = walk_messages(src, address, cap, |m| match m.state {
        MessageState::Active => counts.active += 1,
        MessageState::Deferred => counts.deferred += 1,
        MessageState::Scheduled => counts.scheduled += 1,
    })
    .await?;
    counts.total = walked.examined;
    counts.truncated = walked.truncated;
    Ok(counts)
}
```

The existing `count_*` tests in `scan.rs` (cap, exactly-the-cap, paging, stuck source, duplicates and unsequenced) now cover the walker and must pass unchanged.

`explorer.rs` (the tally reads up to `COUNT_CAP` messages, so it runs over Task 2's `PerBatch` and takes the AMQP lock per batch; the broker list is short and keeps the lock for its few pages):

```rust
    /// Sessions of `entity`: the broker's list merged with a peek tally of up to
    /// `COUNT_CAP` messages. Fails only when both fail.
    pub async fn list_sessions(&self, entity: &EntityPath) -> Result<SessionListing> {
        let address = entity.address(SubQueue::Active);
        let broker = with_one_retry(|_| async {
            let mut guard = self.connection().await?;
            let r = sessions::list_session_ids(guard.as_mut().expect("opened"), &address).await;
            Self::settle(&mut guard, r).await
        })
        .await;
        let tally = with_one_retry(|_| async {
            sessions::tally_sessions(&mut PerBatch { explorer: self }, &address, Self::COUNT_CAP).await
        })
        .await;
        sessions::combine(broker, tally)
    }
```

`inspect.rs` (above the tests):

```rust
//! `dispatched show`, `session ls`, `rule ls`: read-only inspection.

use crate::Format;
use crate::commands::browse::explorer_for;
use crate::context::Ctx;
use crate::output::{sanitize, table};
use crate::resolve::Selection;
use dispatched_core::mgmt::{EntityDetails, EntityKind, RuleFilter, RuleInfo};
use dispatched_core::messaging::sessions::{SessionListing, SessionSummary};
use dispatched_core::{EntityPath, Error};
use time::format_description::well_known::Rfc3339;

fn quoted(name: &str, v: &Option<String>) -> Option<String> {
    v.as_ref().map(|v| format!("{name} = '{v}'"))
}

/// One line for a filter: the SQL text, or the correlation fields ANDed.
pub fn filter_text(f: &RuleFilter) -> String {
    match f {
        RuleFilter::Sql { expression } => expression.clone(),
        RuleFilter::Correlation { correlation_id, message_id, to, reply_to, subject, session_id, reply_to_session_id, content_type, properties } => {
            let mut parts: Vec<String> = [
                quoted("CorrelationId", correlation_id), quoted("MessageId", message_id), quoted("To", to),
                quoted("ReplyTo", reply_to), quoted("Subject", subject), quoted("SessionId", session_id),
                quoted("ReplyToSessionId", reply_to_session_id), quoted("ContentType", content_type),
            ]
            .into_iter()
            .flatten()
            .collect();
            parts.extend(properties.iter().map(|p| format!("{} = '{}'", p.name, p.value)));
            if parts.is_empty() { "(matches nothing: no fields set)".into() } else { parts.join(" AND ") }
        }
    }
}

pub fn rules_table(rules: &[RuleInfo]) -> String {
    let rows: Vec<Vec<String>> = rules
        .iter()
        .map(|r| vec![sanitize(&r.name), sanitize(&filter_text(&r.filter)), sanitize(r.action.as_deref().unwrap_or(""))])
        .collect();
    table(&["Rule", "Filter", "Action"], &rows)
}

fn time_cell(t: Option<time::OffsetDateTime>) -> String {
    t.and_then(|t| t.format(&Rfc3339).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}

pub fn sessions_table(l: &SessionListing) -> String {
    let rows: Vec<Vec<String>> = l
        .sessions
        .iter()
        .map(|s: &SessionSummary| {
            let n = match s.messages {
                None => "?".to_string(),
                Some(n) if l.truncated => format!("~{n}+"),
                Some(n) => n.to_string(),
            };
            vec![sanitize(&s.id), n, time_cell(s.first_enqueued), time_cell(s.last_enqueued)]
        })
        .collect();
    table(&["Session", "Messages", "First enqueued", "Last enqueued"], &rows)
}

pub fn sessions_note(l: &SessionListing) -> Option<String> {
    match (l.from_broker, l.truncated) {
        (_, true) => Some(format!("(counts are from the first {} messages; there are more)", l.scanned)),
        (false, false) => Some("(the service did not list sessions; showing sessions found by peeking)".into()),
        _ => None,
    }
}

pub fn details_table(d: &EntityDetails) -> String {
    let mut rows: Vec<Vec<String>> = Vec::new();
    if let Some(s) = &d.status {
        rows.push(vec!["Status".into(), sanitize(s)]);
    }
    if let Some(c) = &d.counts {
        let q = if c.truncated { "+" } else { "" };
        let a = if c.approximate { "~" } else { "" };
        for (k, v) in [("Active", c.active), ("Dead-letter", c.dead_letter), ("Scheduled", c.scheduled), ("Transfer", c.transfer), ("Transfer DLQ", c.transfer_dead_letter)] {
            rows.push(vec![k.into(), format!("{a}{v}{q}")]);
        }
    }
    rows.extend(d.properties.iter().filter(|p| p.name != "Status").map(|p| vec![sanitize(&p.name), sanitize(&p.value)]));
    table(&["Property", "Value"], &rows)
}

pub async fn show(ctx: &Ctx, sel: &Selection<'_>, entity: &str, format: Format) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let path = EntityPath::parse(entity)?;
    let ex = explorer_for(ctx, sel)?;
    let result = if path.subscription.is_some() {
        ex.entity_details(EntityKind::Subscription, entity).await
    } else {
        match ex.entity_details(EntityKind::Queue, entity).await {
            Err(Error::NotFound(_)) => ex.entity_details(EntityKind::Topic, entity).await,
            other => other,
        }
    };
    ex.close().await;
    let d = result?;
    match format {
        Format::Json => println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&d)?),
        _ => println!("{}", details_table(&d)),
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn sessions(ctx: &Ctx, sel: &Selection<'_>, entity: &str, format: Format) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let path = EntityPath::parse(entity)?;
    let ex = explorer_for(ctx, sel)?;
    let result = ex.list_sessions(&path).await;
    ex.close().await;
    let l = result?;
    match format {
        Format::Json => println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&l)?),
        _ => {
            println!("{}", sessions_table(&l));
            if l.sessions.is_empty() {
                eprintln!("(no sessions)");
            }
            if let Some(n) = sessions_note(&l) {
                eprintln!("{n}");
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn rules(ctx: &Ctx, sel: &Selection<'_>, subscription: &str, format: Format) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let path = EntityPath::parse(subscription)?;
    let Some(sub) = path.subscription.as_deref() else {
        anyhow::bail!("rules belong to a subscription: pass <topic>/subscriptions/<subscription>");
    };
    let ex = explorer_for(ctx, sel)?;
    let result = ex.list_rules(&path.entity, sub).await;
    ex.close().await;
    let rules = result?;
    match format {
        Format::Json => println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&rules)?),
        _ => println!("{}", rules_table(&rules)),
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

(`crate::output::table(headers: &[&str], rows: &[Vec<String>]) -> String` and `sanitize(&str) -> String` exist in `output.rs`; keep `sanitize` on every remote string.)

`events.rs`:

```rust
/// Run `work` and record an activity entry only if it fails (reads that happen on every
/// selection would otherwise flood the log). A cancellation is not a failure.
pub async fn tracked_failures<T>(
    ev: &dyn Events,
    failed: &str,
    work: impl Future<Output = Result<T, CommandError>>,
) -> Result<T, CommandError> {
    let started = Instant::now();
    let result = work.await;
    if let Err(e) = &result
        && e.kind != "cancelled"
    {
        ev.activity(ActivityEntry {
            at: now_rfc3339(),
            action: failed.to_string(),
            duration_ms: started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
            error: Some(ActivityError { kind: e.kind.to_string(), message: e.message.clone() }),
        });
    }
    result
}
```

`ops/browse.rs` (above the tests):

```rust
//! Message browsing: streamed peek pages of summaries, one message's detail, filter checks.

use crate::error::CommandError;
use crate::events::{Events, tracked_failures};
use crate::state::AppState;
use dispatched_core::{
    DETAIL_BODY_CAP, EntityPath, FilterIssue, FilterSpec, MessageDetail, MessageSummary, PeekOptions, PeekPage, PeekView,
};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Most summaries in one page.
pub const PAGE_MAX: u32 = 500;
/// Most summaries in one "from the end" call (it has no continuation).
pub const TAIL_MAX: u32 = 5_000;
/// Most messages one page call examines.
pub const SCAN_PER_CALL: u32 = 5_000;
/// Most messages a "from the end" call examines.
pub const TAIL_BUDGET: u32 = 100_000;

/// Where a peek starts.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum PeekFrom {
    /// The oldest message.
    Start,
    /// This sequence number.
    Sequence { sequence: i64 },
    /// The newest messages ("from the end").
    End,
}

/// One page request from the UI.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct PeekRequest {
    /// `queue` or `topic/subscriptions/name`.
    pub entity: String,
    pub view: PeekView,
    pub from: PeekFrom,
    pub max: u32,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub scan_limit: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub filter: FilterSpec,
}

/// One page for the grid.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct PeekPageView {
    pub messages: Vec<MessageSummary>,
    pub scanned: u32,
    pub next_sequence: Option<i64>,
    pub exhausted: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Prepared {
    pub entity: EntityPath,
    pub view: PeekView,
    pub opts: PeekOptions,
    pub tail: bool,
}

/// Validate and bound a request. Fails (kind `invalid`) before any network work.
pub fn prepare(req: &PeekRequest) -> Result<Prepared, CommandError> {
    let entity = EntityPath::parse(&req.entity)?;
    let filter = req.filter.build()?;
    let tail = req.from == PeekFrom::End;
    let (max_cap, scan_cap) = if tail { (TAIL_MAX, TAIL_BUDGET) } else { (PAGE_MAX, SCAN_PER_CALL) };
    let from = match req.from {
        PeekFrom::Sequence { sequence } => sequence.max(0),
        _ => 0,
    };
    let opts = PeekOptions::new(req.max.clamp(1, max_cap))
        .from_sequence(from)
        .scan_limit(req.scan_limit.unwrap_or(scan_cap).clamp(1, scan_cap))
        .filter(filter);
    Ok(Prepared { entity, view: req.view, opts, tail })
}

pub fn page_view(page: &PeekPage) -> PeekPageView {
    PeekPageView {
        messages: page.messages.iter().map(MessageSummary::from).collect(),
        scanned: page.scanned,
        next_sequence: page.next_sequence,
        exhausted: page.exhausted,
    }
}

/// One page of `req.view` of `req.entity` in `profile_id`, which must be the connected profile.
pub async fn peek_page(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, profile_id: &str, req: PeekRequest) -> Result<PeekPageView, CommandError> {
    let p = prepare(&req)?;
    let active = state.active_for(profile_id).await?;
    // A load is up to 20 page calls: only failures are logged, never each page.
    tracked_failures(ev, &format!("Peeking {} failed", p.entity), async {
        let ex = &active.explorer;
        let page = if p.tail { ex.peek_tail(&p.entity, p.view, &p.opts).await? } else { ex.peek(&p.entity, p.view, &p.opts).await? };
        Ok(page_view(&page))
    })
    .await
}

/// The full message (body capped at [`DETAIL_BODY_CAP`]), or `None` when it is no longer in the view.
pub async fn message_detail(
    state: &AppState,
    ev: &dyn Events,
    profile_id: &str,
    entity: &str,
    view: PeekView,
    sequence: i64,
) -> Result<Option<MessageDetail>, CommandError> {
    let path = EntityPath::parse(entity)?;
    let active = state.active_for(profile_id).await?;
    tracked_failures(ev, &format!("Reading message {sequence} of {path} failed"), async {
        Ok(active.explorer.peek_message(&path, view, sequence).await?.map(|m| MessageDetail::new(m, DETAIL_BODY_CAP)))
    })
    .await
}

/// Problems with `spec`, one per field; empty when it compiles.
pub fn check_filter(spec: &FilterSpec) -> Vec<FilterIssue> {
    spec.compile().err().unwrap_or_default()
}
```

        assert_eq!(v.rows.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!((v.rows[0].kind, v.rows[0].id.as_str()), (RowKind::Queue, "orders"));
        assert_eq!((v.rows[1].kind, v.rows[1].id.as_str(), v.rows[1].topic.as_deref()), (RowKind::Subscription, "events/subscriptions/audit", Some("events")));

`ops/entity.rs`:

```rust
//! One entity: its details, a subscription's rules, a session-enabled entity's sessions.

use crate::error::CommandError;
use crate::events::{Events, tracked, tracked_failures};
use crate::ops::tree::count;
use crate::state::AppState;
use dispatched_core::messaging::sessions::SessionListing;
use dispatched_core::{EntityDetails, EntityKind, EntityPath, RuleInfo};

/// Overview polls this every 10 s while it is visible, so only failures are logged.
pub async fn entity_details(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, profile_id: &str, kind: EntityKind, entity: &str) -> Result<EntityDetails, CommandError> {
    let active = state.active_for(profile_id).await?;
    tracked_failures(ev, &format!("Reading the details of {entity} failed"), async {
        Ok(active.explorer.entity_details(kind, entity).await?)
    })
    .await
}

pub async fn list_rules(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, profile_id: &str, entity: &str) -> Result<Vec<RuleInfo>, CommandError> {
    let path = EntityPath::parse(entity)?;
    let Some(sub) = path.subscription.clone() else {
        return Err(CommandError::new("invalid", format!("{entity} is not a subscription; only subscriptions have rules.")));
    };
    let active = state.active_for(profile_id).await?;
    tracked(
        ev,
        &format!("Listing the rules of {path} failed"),
        async { Ok(active.explorer.list_rules(&path.entity, &sub).await?) },
        |r| format!("Listed {} of {path}", count(r.len(), "rule", "rules")),
    )
    .await
}

pub async fn list_sessions(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, profile_id: &str, entity: &str) -> Result<SessionListing, CommandError> {
    let path = EntityPath::parse(entity)?;
    let active = state.active_for(profile_id).await?;
    tracked(
        ev,
        &format!("Listing the sessions of {path} failed"),
        async { Ok(active.explorer.list_sessions(&path).await?) },
        |l| format!("Listed {} of {path}", count(l.sessions.len(), "session", "sessions")),
    )
    .await
}
```

(`list_rules` checks the path before `active_for`, so the test's "invalid" holds without a connection.)

`ops/dashboard.rs`:

```rust
//! The Dashboard: counts of every queue and subscription.

use crate::error::CommandError;
use crate::events::{Events, tracked_failures};
use crate::state::AppState;
use dispatched_core::namespace::known_entities;
use dispatched_core::{EntityCounts, Error, QueueInfo, SubscriptionInfo};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;

/// Topics whose subscriptions are listed at the same time.
const PARALLEL_TOPICS: usize = 8;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum RowKind {
    Queue,
    Subscription,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct DashboardRow {
    pub kind: RowKind,
    /// The entity's tab id: a queue name or `topic/subscriptions/name`.
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub topic: Option<String>,
    pub status: Option<String>,
    pub counts: Option<EntityCounts>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct DashboardFailure {
    pub topic: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub message: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct DashboardView {
    pub rows: Vec<DashboardRow>,
    pub failures: Vec<DashboardFailure>,
    /// Why the dashboard shows only known entities.
    pub limited: Option<String>,
}

pub fn rows_from(queues: Vec<QueueInfo>, subscriptions: Vec<(String, Result<Vec<SubscriptionInfo>, Error>)>) -> DashboardView {
    let mut rows: Vec<DashboardRow> = queues
        .into_iter()
        .map(|q| DashboardRow { kind: RowKind::Queue, id: q.name.clone(), name: q.name, topic: None, status: q.status, counts: q.counts })
        .collect();
    let mut failures = Vec::new();
    for (topic, result) in subscriptions {
        match result {
            Ok(subs) => rows.extend(subs.into_iter().map(|s| DashboardRow {
                kind: RowKind::Subscription,
                id: format!("{}/subscriptions/{}", s.topic, s.name),
                name: s.name,
                topic: Some(s.topic),
                status: s.status,
                counts: s.counts,
            })),
            Err(e) => {
                let e = CommandError::from(e);
                failures.push(DashboardFailure { topic, kind: e.kind.to_string(), message: e.message });
            }
        }
    }
    DashboardView { rows, failures, limited: None }
}

pub fn limited_view(entity_path: Option<&str>, pins: &[String], needed: Option<String>) -> DashboardView {
    let rows = known_entities(entity_path, pins)
        .into_iter()
        .map(|k| match k.subscription {
            Some(sub) => DashboardRow { kind: RowKind::Subscription, id: format!("{}/subscriptions/{sub}", k.entity), name: sub, topic: Some(k.entity), status: None, counts: None },
            None => DashboardRow { kind: RowKind::Queue, id: k.entity.clone(), name: k.entity, topic: None, status: None, counts: None },
        })
        .collect();
    let right = needed.unwrap_or_else(|| "Manage".into());
    DashboardView {
        rows,
        failures: vec![],
        limited: Some(format!("This identity can't list the namespace (needs {right}). Showing pinned entities without counts.")),
    }
}

/// The Dashboard refreshes itself on a timer, so only failures are logged (first load included).
pub async fn dashboard(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, profile_id: &str) -> Result<DashboardView, CommandError> {
    let active = state.active_for(profile_id).await?;
    let pins = state.store.get_by_id(&active.profile_id)?.map(|p| p.pinned_entities).unwrap_or_default();
    tracked_failures(
        ev,
        "Loading the dashboard failed",
        async {
            let ex = active.explorer.clone();
            let (queues, topics) = match tokio::try_join!(ex.list_queues(), ex.list_topics()) {
                Ok(v) => v,
                Err(Error::Forbidden { needed, .. }) => return Ok(limited_view(ex.namespace().entity_path(), &pins, needed)),
                Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
            };
            let gate = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(PARALLEL_TOPICS));
            let mut set = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
            for t in topics {
                let (ex, gate) = (ex.clone(), gate.clone());
                set.spawn(async move {
                    let _permit = gate.acquire_owned().await.expect("semaphore open");
                    let r = ex.list_subscriptions(&t.name).await;
                    (t.name, r)
                });
            }
            let mut subs = Vec::new();
            while let Some(joined) = set.join_next().await {
                subs.push(joined.map_err(|e| CommandError::new("protocol", format!("dashboard task failed: {e}")))?);
            }
            subs.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
            Ok(rows_from(queues, subs))
        },
    )
    .await
}
```

`Cargo.toml`: `tokio = { version = "1", features = ["sync", "macros", "time", "rt"] }`.

**Files:**
- Create: `app/src-tauri/src/jobs.rs`, `app/src-tauri/src/ops/export.rs`
- Modify: `app/src-tauri/src/state.rs` (`jobs: Arc<Jobs>`), `app/src-tauri/src/events.rs` (`JobEvent::Finished` gains `summary`, `output`; drop the `dead_code` allows on job items), `app/src-tauri/src/ops/mod.rs`, `app/src-tauri/src/commands.rs`, `app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs`, `app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml` (`tauri-plugin-dialog`, `async-trait`, `uuid`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`Explorer::collect_sequences`, `SequenceCollection`), Task 3 (`stream_pages`, `FileExport`, `Continuation`), Task 7 (`SCAN_PER_CALL`, `PeekFrom` not needed), Task 8 (tokio `rt`, which `tokio::spawn` needs), M1b `Events::job`, `JobEvent`, `tracked`.
- Produces:
  - `ExportScope` = `{ kind: "all", filter: FilterSpec, from_sequence: i64 = 0, max: Option<u64> }` | `{ kind: "selected", sequences: Vec<i64> }`
  - `ExportRequest { entity, view: PeekView, scope: ExportScope, format: "json" | "ndjson", path: String, overwrite: bool = false, total_hint: Option<u64> }`
  - commands: `pick_export_path(entity, view, format) -> string | null` (native save dialog, suggested name `{entity-slug}-{view}-{yyyyMMdd-HHmm}.{json|ndjson}`; Rust remembers the returned path in `Jobs`); `export_start(profileId, request) -> { id }` (`overwrite` is honoured only for that exact remembered path, once; otherwise it is `false`); `job_cancel(id) -> bool`; `job_reveal(id)` (opens the folder of a finished export)
  - `Jobs::{set_picked, take_picked}`, `ops::export::authorize_overwrite(jobs, &mut req)`
  - job events: `started { id, title, total }`, `progress { id, done, total }` after every page, `finished { id, error, summary, output }` (`output` = full file path; `summary` e.g. `Exported 1,284 messages to orders-dead-letter-20261016-0941.json`)
  - `ExportSource` trait (implemented by `Explorer`, faked in tests); `run(plan, source, cancel, ev, id) -> Result<ExportSummary, CommandError>`; `suggested_file_name(entity, view, format, now) -> String`

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::events::Recorder;
    use dispatched_core::{MessageBody, MessageState, PeekPage, PeekedMessage};
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    fn msg(seq: i64) -> PeekedMessage {
        PeekedMessage {
            sequence_number: seq, state: MessageState::Active, message_id: None, correlation_id: None, session_id: None,
            subject: None, content_type: None, to: None, reply_to: None, enqueued_time: None, scheduled_enqueue_time: None,
            locked_until: None, delivery_count: 0, time_to_live_ms: None, dead_letter_reason: None, dead_letter_description: None,
            dead_letter_source: None, application_properties: Default::default(), body: MessageBody::Text(format!("secret-body-{seq}")),
        }
    }

    /// Pages of 2 over messages 1..=n; `sequences` finds what exists.
    struct Fake {
        n: i64,
        calls: Mutex<u32>,
        cancel_after: Option<(u32, CancellationToken)>,
    }

    #[async_trait::async_trait]
    impl ExportSource for Fake {
        async fn page(&self, _e: &EntityPath, _v: PeekView, opts: &PeekOptions) -> dispatched_core::Result<PeekPage> {
            let mut calls = self.calls.lock().unwrap();
            *calls += 1;
            if let Some((after, token)) = &self.cancel_after
                && *calls >= *after
            {
                token.cancel();
            }
            let from = opts.from_sequence.max(1);
            let messages: Vec<PeekedMessage> = (from..=self.n).take(2.min(opts.max as usize)).map(msg).collect();
            let last = messages.last().map(|m| m.sequence_number).unwrap_or(from);
            let exhausted = last >= self.n;
            Ok(PeekPage { scanned: messages.len() as u32, next_sequence: (!exhausted).then_some(last + 1), exhausted, messages })
        }
        async fn sequences(&self, _e: &EntityPath, _v: PeekView, seqs: &[i64], _b: u64, _c: &CancellationToken) -> dispatched_core::Result<SequenceCollection> {
            let (found, missing): (Vec<i64>, Vec<i64>) = seqs.iter().partition(|s| **s <= self.n);
            Ok(SequenceCollection { messages: found.into_iter().map(msg).collect(), missing, scanned: 0 })
        }
    }

    fn request(dir: &std::path::Path, scope: ExportScope) -> ExportRequest {
        ExportRequest {
            entity: "orders".into(), view: PeekView::DeadLetter, scope, format: "json".into(),
            path: dir.join("out.json").to_string_lossy().into_owned(), overwrite: false, total_hint: Some(5),
        }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn exports_every_page_with_progress_and_a_summary() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let ev = Recorder::default();
        let plan = plan(request(dir.path(), ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None })).unwrap();
        let fake = Fake { n: 5, calls: Mutex::new(0), cancel_after: None };
        let s = run(plan, &fake, &CancellationToken::new(), &ev, "j1").await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.written, 5);
        assert_eq!(s.summary, "Exported 5 messages to out.json");
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(dir.path().join("out.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v.as_array().unwrap().len(), 5);
        let jobs = ev.jobs.lock().unwrap();
        let done: Vec<u64> = jobs.iter().filter_map(|j| match j { JobEvent::Progress { done, .. } => Some(*done), _ => None }).collect();
        assert_eq!(done, [2, 4, 5]);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn cancel_stops_the_export_and_leaves_no_file() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let ev = Recorder::default();
        let token = CancellationToken::new();
        let plan = plan(request(dir.path(), ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None })).unwrap();
        let fake = Fake { n: 100, calls: Mutex::new(0), cancel_after: Some((2, token.clone())) };
        let e = run(plan, &fake, &token, &ev, "j1").await.unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(e.kind, "cancelled");
        assert_eq!(e.message, "Export cancelled.");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_dir(dir.path()).unwrap().count(), 0, "temp file removed");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn selected_export_summary_counts_missing_messages() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let ev = Recorder::default();
        let plan = plan(request(dir.path(), ExportScope::Selected { sequences: vec![1, 2, 9] })).unwrap();
        let fake = Fake { n: 5, calls: Mutex::new(0), cancel_after: None };
        let s = run(plan, &fake, &CancellationToken::new(), &ev, "j1").await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(s.written, 2);
        assert_eq!(s.summary, "Exported 2 messages to out.json; 1 selected message was no longer there");
    }

    #[test]
    fn plan_refuses_bad_input_before_any_work() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let mut r = request(dir.path(), ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None });
        r.format = "csv".into();
        assert_eq!(plan(r).unwrap_err().kind, "invalid");
        let mut r = request(dir.path(), ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None });
        r.path = "relative.json".into();
        assert_eq!(plan(r).unwrap_err().kind, "invalid");
        let r = request(dir.path(), ExportScope::Selected { sequences: vec![] });
        assert_eq!(plan(r).unwrap_err().kind, "invalid");
        std::fs::write(dir.path().join("out.json"), "x").unwrap();
        let r = request(dir.path(), ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None });
        assert!(plan(r).unwrap_err().message.contains("already exists"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn overwrite_is_allowed_only_for_the_path_the_dialog_returned() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let target = dir.path().join("out.json");
        std::fs::write(&target, "keep").unwrap();
        let all = || ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None };
        let asking = || {
            let mut r = request(dir.path(), all());
            r.overwrite = true;
            r
        };
        let jobs = Jobs::default();
        // The webview asks to replace a path the dialog never returned: refused.
        let mut r = asking();
        authorize_overwrite(&jobs, &mut r);
        assert!(!r.overwrite);
        assert!(plan(r).unwrap_err().message.contains("already exists"));
        // Another path than the picked one: refused.
        jobs.set_picked(Some(dir.path().join("other.json")));
        let mut r = asking();
        authorize_overwrite(&jobs, &mut r);
        assert!(!r.overwrite);
        // The picked path itself: allowed, once.
        jobs.set_picked(Some(target.clone()));
        let mut r = asking();
        authorize_overwrite(&jobs, &mut r);
        assert!(r.overwrite);
        let mut again = asking();
        authorize_overwrite(&jobs, &mut again);
        assert!(!again.overwrite, "a pick authorises one export");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&target).unwrap(), "keep");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn export_events_name_the_file_not_the_messages() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let ev = Recorder::default();
        let plan = plan(request(dir.path(), ExportScope::All { filter: Default::default(), from_sequence: 0, max: None })).unwrap();
        let fake = Fake { n: 3, calls: Mutex::new(0), cancel_after: None };
        run(plan, &fake, &CancellationToken::new(), &ev, "j1").await.unwrap();
        let events = serde_json::to_string(&*ev.jobs.lock().unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert!(!events.contains("secret-body"), "{events}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn suggested_names_are_safe_and_dated() {
        let at = time::macros::datetime!(2026-10-16 09:41 UTC);
        assert_eq!(suggested_file_name("events/subscriptions/audit", PeekView::DeadLetter, "json", at), "events-subscriptions-audit-dead-letter-20261016-0941.json");
        assert_eq!(suggested_file_name("a b:c", PeekView::Active, "ndjson", at), "a-b-c-active-20261016-0941.ndjson");
    }
}

mod tests {

        assert_eq!(jobs.take_picked().unwrap().to_string_lossy(), "/tmp/a.json");

The default toolchain here is newer than the MSRV and CI builds on stable, so a plain `cargo build` never reveals a plugin that needs a newer Rust; check against 1.88 explicitly (the 1.88 toolchain is installed):

```bash
df -h /System/Volumes/Data
cargo +1.88 check -p dispatched-app --locked
```

If it fails on a `rust-version` or on newer syntax, step down (`cargo update -p tauri-plugin-dialog --precise <older 2.x>`) and check again. Pin the version that passes as `tauri-plugin-dialog = "~2.N"` and commit `Cargo.lock` (CI builds `--locked`). Dev-dependencies: `tempfile = { workspace = true }`, `time = { workspace = true, features = ["macros"] }` (add `macros` to the workspace `time` features if absent). No npm plugin package is added (the dialog is opened from Rust) and no capability permission: `app/src-tauri/capabilities/default.json` stays as it is.

`jobs.rs`:

```rust
//! Running and finished jobs: their cancel tokens and output files.

use horizon_azure_kit::safety::CancellationToken;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Default)]
struct Entry {
    cancel: Option<CancellationToken>,
    output: Option<PathBuf>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Jobs {
    inner: Mutex<HashMap<String, Entry>>,
    /// The path the native save dialog returned last; the user answered its "replace?" there.
    picked: Mutex<Option<PathBuf>>,
}

impl Jobs {
    /// Remember what the save dialog returned (`None` when it was cancelled).
    pub fn set_picked(&self, path: Option<PathBuf>) {
        *self.picked.lock().unwrap() = path;
    }

    /// The last picked path, once: a pick authorises one export.
    pub fn take_picked(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.picked.lock().unwrap().take()
    }

    /// A new job id and its cancel token.
    pub fn start(&self) -> (String, CancellationToken) {
        let id = format!("job-{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple());
        let token = CancellationToken::new();
        self.inner.lock().unwrap().insert(id.clone(), Entry { cancel: Some(token.clone()), output: None });
        (id, token)
    }

    /// Ask a running job to stop. False when there is no such running job.
    pub fn cancel(&self, id: &str) -> bool {
        match self.inner.lock().unwrap().get(id).and_then(|e| e.cancel.as_ref()) {
            Some(t) => {
                t.cancel();
                true
            }
            None => false,
        }
    }

    /// Mark a job finished, keeping the file it wrote.
    pub fn finish(&self, id: &str, output: Option<PathBuf>) {
        if let Some(e) = self.inner.lock().unwrap().get_mut(id) {
            e.cancel = None;
            e.output = output;
        }
    }

    /// The file a finished job wrote.
    pub fn output(&self, id: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        self.inner.lock().unwrap().get(id).and_then(|e| e.output.clone())
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Export a view, or selected messages, to a JSON or NDJSON file as a cancellable job.

use crate::error::CommandError;
use crate::events::{ActivityError, Events, JobEvent, tracked};
use crate::jobs::Jobs;
use crate::ops::browse::SCAN_PER_CALL;
use dispatched_core::messaging::SequenceCollection;
use dispatched_core::{
    Continuation, EntityPath, Error, ExportFormat, Explorer, FileExport, FilterSpec, MessageFilter, PeekOptions, PeekPage,
    PeekView, stream_pages,
};
use horizon_azure_kit::safety::CancellationToken;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;

/// Most messages examined to find selected sequence numbers again.
const SELECTED_BUDGET: u64 = 1_000_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum ExportScope {
    All {
        #[serde(default)]
        filter: FilterSpec,
        #[serde(default)]
        from_sequence: i64,
        #[serde(default)]
        max: Option<u64>,
    },
    Selected { sequences: Vec<i64> },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ExportRequest {
    pub entity: String,
    pub view: PeekView,
    pub scope: ExportScope,
    pub format: String,
    pub path: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub overwrite: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub total_hint: Option<u64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct JobStarted {
    pub id: String,
}

pub enum Scope {
    All { filter: MessageFilter, from: i64, max: Option<u64> },
    Selected(Vec<i64>),
}

pub struct ExportPlan {
    pub entity: EntityPath,
    pub view: PeekView,
    pub scope: Scope,
    pub file: FileExport,
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub title: String,
    pub total: Option<u64>,
}

pub struct ExportSummary {
    pub written: u64,
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub summary: String,
}

/// Reads an export needs (the explorer; a fake in tests).
#[async_trait::async_trait]
pub trait ExportSource: Send + Sync {
    async fn page(&self, entity: &EntityPath, view: PeekView, opts: &PeekOptions) -> dispatched_core::Result<PeekPage>;
    async fn sequences(&self, entity: &EntityPath, view: PeekView, sequences: &[i64], budget: u64, cancel: &CancellationToken) -> dispatched_core::Result<SequenceCollection>;
}

#[async_trait::async_trait]
impl ExportSource for Explorer {
    async fn page(&self, entity: &EntityPath, view: PeekView, opts: &PeekOptions) -> dispatched_core::Result<PeekPage> {
        self.peek(entity, view, opts).await
    }
    async fn sequences(&self, entity: &EntityPath, view: PeekView, sequences: &[i64], budget: u64, cancel: &CancellationToken) -> dispatched_core::Result<SequenceCollection> {
        self.collect_sequences(entity, view, sequences, budget, Some(cancel)).await
    }
}

fn file_name(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
    path.file_name().map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_else(|| path.display().to_string())
}

fn thousands(n: u64) -> String {
    let s = n.to_string();
    let mut out = String::new();
    for (i, c) in s.chars().enumerate() {
        if i > 0 && (s.len() - i) % 3 == 0 {
            out.push(',');
        }
        out.push(c);
    }
    out
}

fn messages(n: u64) -> String {
    format!("{} {}", thousands(n), if n == 1 { "message" } else { "messages" })
}

/// `{entity-slug}-{view}-{yyyyMMdd-HHmm}.{ext}`; anything but letters, digits, `.`, `_`, `-` becomes `-`.
pub fn suggested_file_name(entity: &str, view: PeekView, format: &str, now: time::OffsetDateTime) -> String {
    let slug: String = entity.chars().map(|c| if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '.' | '_' | '-') { c } else { '-' }).collect();
    let ext = if format.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ndjson") { "ndjson" } else { "json" };
    format!(
        "{slug}-{}-{:04}{:02}{:02}-{:02}{:02}.{ext}",
        view.as_str(), now.year(), u8::from(now.month()), now.day(), now.hour(), now.minute()
    )
}

/// Validate a request and open the export file (fails early when it exists and may not be replaced).
pub fn plan(req: ExportRequest) -> Result<ExportPlan, CommandError> {
    let entity = EntityPath::parse(&req.entity)?;
    let format = ExportFormat::parse(&req.format)?;
    let path = PathBuf::from(&req.path);
    if !path.is_absolute() {
        return Err(CommandError::new("invalid", "Choose where to save the export."));
    }
    let scope = match req.scope {
        ExportScope::All { filter, from_sequence, max } => Scope::All { filter: filter.build()?, from: from_sequence.max(0), max },
        ExportScope::Selected { sequences } if sequences.is_empty() => {
            return Err(CommandError::new("invalid", "Select the messages to export."));
        }
        ExportScope::Selected { sequences } => Scope::Selected(sequences),
    };
    let total = match &scope {
        Scope::Selected(s) => Some(s.len() as u64),
        Scope::All { max, .. } => match (*max, req.total_hint) {
            (Some(m), Some(h)) => Some(m.min(h)),
            (m, h) => m.or(h),
        },
    };
    let file = FileExport::create(&path, format, req.overwrite)?;
    let title = format!("Export {entity} ({}) to {}", req.view.as_str(), file_name(&path));
    Ok(ExportPlan { entity, view: req.view, scope, file, path, title, total })
}

fn cancelled(e: Error) -> CommandError {
    match e {
        Error::Cancelled => CommandError::new("cancelled", "Export cancelled."),
        other => other.into(),
    }
}

/// Write the export, reporting progress after every page.
pub async fn run(plan: ExportPlan, source: &dyn ExportSource, cancel: &CancellationToken, ev: &dyn Events, id: &str) -> Result<ExportSummary, CommandError> {
    let ExportPlan { entity, view, scope, mut file, path, total, .. } = plan;
    let mut note = String::new();
    match scope {
        Scope::All { filter, from, max } => {
            let fetch = |from: i64, n: u32| {
                let opts = PeekOptions::new(n).from_sequence(from).scan_limit(SCAN_PER_CALL).filter(filter.clone());
                let entity = &entity;
                async move { source.page(entity, view, &opts).await }
            };
            let streamed = stream_pages(max, from, Some(cancel), fetch, |page, _| {
                file.write(&page)?;
                ev.job(JobEvent::Progress { id: id.to_string(), done: file.written(), total });
                Ok(())
            })
            .await
            .map_err(cancelled)?;
            if streamed.end == Continuation::Stalled {
                note = "; the scan stopped early, so the end of the entity was not confirmed".into();
            }
        }
        Scope::Selected(sequences) => {
            let found = source.sequences(&entity, view, &sequences, SELECTED_BUDGET, cancel).await.map_err(cancelled)?;
            if cancel.is_cancelled() {
                return Err(CommandError::new("cancelled", "Export cancelled."));
            }
            file.write(&found.messages)?;
            ev.job(JobEvent::Progress { id: id.to_string(), done: file.written(), total });
            match found.missing.len() {
                0 => {}
                1 => note = "; 1 selected message was no longer there".into(),
                n => note = format!("; {n} selected messages were no longer there"),
            }
        }
    }
    let written = file.finish()?;
    Ok(ExportSummary { written, summary: format!("Exported {} to {}{note}", messages(written), file_name(&path)), path })
}

/// `overwrite` comes from the webview, which must not be able to replace any file it names
/// (injected script could). It is honoured only for the exact path the native save dialog
/// returned last, where the user answered "replace?"; every other path is written only when
/// nothing is there. Taking the pick also spends it.
pub fn authorize_overwrite(jobs: &Jobs, req: &mut ExportRequest) {
    let picked = jobs.take_picked();
    req.overwrite = req.overwrite && picked.is_some_and(|p| p == std::path::Path::new(&req.path));
}

/// Validate, then run the export in the background. Returns at once with the job id.
pub fn start(jobs: &Arc<Jobs>, explorer: Arc<Explorer>, ev: Arc<dyn Events>, mut req: ExportRequest) -> Result<JobStarted, CommandError> {
    authorize_overwrite(jobs, &mut req);
    let plan = plan(req)?;
    let (id, cancel) = jobs.start();
    ev.job(JobEvent::Started { id: id.clone(), title: plan.title.clone(), total: plan.total });
    let (jobs, job_id) = (jobs.clone(), id.clone());
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let failed = format!("{} failed", plan.title);
        let result = tracked(&*ev, &failed, run(plan, &*explorer, &cancel, &*ev, &job_id), |s| s.summary.clone()).await;
        match result {
            Ok(s) => {
                jobs.finish(&job_id, Some(s.path.clone()));
                ev.job(JobEvent::Finished { id: job_id, error: None, summary: Some(s.summary), output: Some(s.path.display().to_string()) });
            }
            Err(e) => {
                jobs.finish(&job_id, None);
                ev.job(JobEvent::Finished { id: job_id, error: Some(ActivityError { kind: e.kind.to_string(), message: e.message }), summary: None, output: None });
            }
        }
    });
    Ok(JobStarted { id })
}

/// Open the folder holding a finished export's file.
pub fn reveal(jobs: &Jobs, id: &str) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    let path = jobs.output(id).ok_or_else(|| CommandError::new("not_found", "That export has no file to show."))?;
    let folder = path.parent().map(std::path::Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or(path);
    open::that(&folder).map_err(|e| CommandError::new("io", format!("Couldn't open the folder: {e}")))
}
```

`commands.rs` (register all four, plus `.plugin(tauri_plugin_dialog::init())` in `lib.rs`):

```rust
#[tauri::command]
pub async fn pick_export_path(app: AppHandle, state: State<'_, AppState>, entity: String, view: PeekView, format: String) -> Result<Option<String>, CommandError> {
    use tauri_plugin_dialog::DialogExt;
    let name = ops::export::suggested_file_name(&entity, view, &format, time::OffsetDateTime::now_local().unwrap_or_else(|_| time::OffsetDateTime::now_utc()));
    let (label, ext) = if format.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ndjson") { ("NDJSON", "ndjson") } else { ("JSON", "json") };
    let picked = tauri::async_runtime::spawn_blocking(move || app.dialog().file().set_file_name(&name).add_filter(label, &[ext]).blocking_save_file())
        .await
        .map_err(|e| CommandError::new("io", format!("The save dialog failed: {e}")))?;
    let path = picked.and_then(|p| p.into_path().ok());
    // Only this exact path may be replaced by the next export_start (see authorize_overwrite).
    state.jobs.set_picked(path.clone());
    Ok(path.map(|p| p.display().to_string()))
}

#[tauri::command]
pub async fn export_start(app: AppHandle, state: State<'_, AppState>, profile_id: String, request: ExportRequest) -> Result<JobStarted, CommandError> {
    let active = state.active_for(&profile_id).await?;
    ops::export::start(&state.jobs, active.explorer, Arc::new(TauriEvents(app)), request)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn job_cancel(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> bool {
    state.jobs.cancel(&id)
}

#[tauri::command]
pub fn job_reveal(state: State<'_, AppState>, id: String) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    ops::export::reveal(&state.jobs, &id)
}
```

(`time` needs the `local-offset` feature for `now_local`; if the workspace does not enable it, use `now_utc()` and note it in the report.) The save dialog shows the platform's own "replace?" question, so the frontend passes `overwrite: true` for paths that came from it (Task 17's call is unchanged). Rust does not trust that flag: `export_start` honours it only for the exact path `pick_export_path` returned last, once (`authorize_overwrite`); any other path, typed or injected, is written only when no file is there.

**Files:**

- Modify: `app/src-tauri/src/ops/profiles.rs` (`draft_sign_in`, `test_draft`, `copy_entity_connection_string`), `app/src-tauri/src/ops/account.rs` (`StoredSignIn`, `sign_ins`, `sign_out`, prompt URL, `open_sign_in_page`), `app/src-tauri/src/commands.rs`, `app/src-tauri/src/lib.rs`, `app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml` (`tauri-plugin-clipboard-manager`), `Cargo.lock`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M1b `ProfileDraft`, `DraftAuth`, `namespace_for`, `normalize_tenant`, `entra_sign_in`, `test_connection`, `AppState::{current_sign_in, usable_sign_in, session}`, `ops::profiles::bound_sign_in`, `state::same_identity`; Task 9 `AppState.last_prompt_url` (Task 9 added the field, so this task runs after Task 9).
- Produces:
  - core `connstr::entity_connection_string(cs: &str, entity: &EntityPath) -> Result<SecretString>` (replaces any `EntityPath=`; refuses a string scoped to another entity)
  - core `profiles::namespace_for_draft(draft, existing: Option<&Profile>, secrets, entra, sign_in) -> Result<Namespace>`: a typed connection string is used as is; otherwise the draft becomes a transient `Profile` (the existing connection-string profile, or an Entra profile bound to `sign_in` with `normalize_tenant(draft tenant, sign_in's tenant)`) and goes through `namespace_for`; transport and proxy are applied by the shared `with_options`
  - core `ProfileStore::sign_ins(&self) -> Result<Vec<SignIn>>` (ordered by id; rows that don't deserialize are skipped, as `sign_ins_for_tenant` does since M1b)
  - app `ops::profiles::draft_sign_in(state, &draft, existing) -> Result<Option<SignIn>, CommandError>`: an edited Entra profile uses `state.usable_sign_in(sign_in_id, draft_tenant).1` (what connecting uses); a new one uses `entra_sign_in` (what saving binds)
  - commands: `profile_test_draft(draft) -> ConnectionReport`; `copy_entity_connection_string(profileId, entity)` (writes the clipboard from Rust, returns nothing); `sign_ins_list() -> StoredSignIn[]`; `sign_out(id)`; `open_sign_in_page()`
  - `StoredSignIn { id, user_principal, display_name, tenant_id, current: bool, signed_in: bool, same_account: bool, profiles: string[] }` (named `StoredSignIn`, not `SignInView`: M1b's `ops::account::SignInView { account, tenant_sign_in }` is what `sign_in` returns and stays as it is). `same_account` is `same_identity(current, s)`; `profiles` lists each Entra profile under the sign-in it actually uses, `bound_sign_in(state, p)?.id`
  - activity: `Tested Logistics (unsaved): management ok, messaging ok`, `Copied the connection string for orders`, `Signed out ops@contoso.example`
  - Keychain: this task adds no Keychain item and no new kind of Keychain access. `sign_ins` checks refresh tokens with `session.is_signed_in` exactly as `profiles_list` already does, copying reads the stored connection string as connecting does, and sign-out deletes the sign-in's own token. Check with `npm run tauri:dev` that none of these shows a Keychain prompt.

    #[test]
    fn draft_namespace_uses_the_typed_string_or_the_stored_one() {
        let secrets = MemoryStore::new();
        let draft = |cs: Option<&str>| ProfileDraft {
            id: None, name: "A".into(), auth: DraftAuth::ConnectionString { connection_string: cs.map(str::to_string) },
            production: false, color: None, transport: Transport::Amqp, proxy: None, pinned_entities: vec![],
        };
        let cs = "Endpoint=sb://typed.servicebus.windows.net/;SharedAccessKeyName=r;SharedAccessKey=a2V5";
        assert_eq!(namespace_for_draft(&draft(Some(cs)), None, &secrets, &session(), None).unwrap().host(), "typed.servicebus.windows.net");
        let e = namespace_for_draft(&draft(None), None, &secrets, &session(), None).unwrap_err();
        assert!(matches!(e, Error::Invalid(_)));
        let stored = Profile::connection_string("B", &ConnectionString::parse(cs).unwrap());
        secrets.put(&secret_key(&stored.id), &SecretString::from(cs.to_string())).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(namespace_for_draft(&draft(None), Some(&stored), &secrets, &session(), None).unwrap().host(), "typed.servicebus.windows.net");
        let entra = ProfileDraft { auth: DraftAuth::Entra { namespace: "myns".into(), tenant: None }, ..draft(None) };
        assert!(matches!(namespace_for_draft(&entra, None, &secrets, &session(), None).unwrap_err(), Error::NotSignedIn(_)));
        // With a sign-in the draft goes through namespace_for as a transient Entra profile.

        let guest = ProfileDraft { auth: DraftAuth::Entra { namespace: "myns".into(), tenant: Some(" T ".into()) }, ..draft(None) };
        assert_eq!(namespace_for_draft(&guest, None, &secrets, &session(), Some(&who)).unwrap().host(), "myns.servicebus.windows.net");
    }
