```rust
    #[test]
    fn a_draft_test_uses_the_sign_in_connecting_or_saving_would_use() {
        use crate::ops::test_util::{remember_guest_sign_in, remember_sign_in};
        let s = state();
        let home = remember_sign_in(&s, "oid-1", "tenant-1", true);
        s.store.set_current_sign_in(Some(&home.id)).unwrap();
        remember_guest_sign_in(&s, &home, "oid-1g", "tenant-guest", true);
        let entra = |tenant: Option<&str>, id: Option<&str>| ProfileDraft {
            id: id.map(str::to_string),
            auth: DraftAuth::Entra { namespace: "myns".into(), tenant: tenant.map(str::to_string) },
            ..draft("E", None)
        };
        let used = |d: &ProfileDraft, existing: Option<&Profile>| draft_sign_in(&s, d, existing).unwrap().map(|x| x.id);

        // A new profile: the sign-in saving would bind (entra_sign_in).
        assert_eq!(used(&entra(None, None), None).as_deref(), Some("oid-1"));
        assert_eq!(used(&entra(Some("tenant-guest"), None), None).as_deref(), Some("oid-1g"));

        // An edited profile bound to the home sign-in and asking for the guest tenant: the same
        // person's own sign-in there comes first, as when connecting (usable_sign_in).
        let p = Profile::entra("E", "myns.servicebus.windows.net", "oid-1", Some("tenant-guest"));
        s.store.insert(&p).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(used(&entra(Some("tenant-guest"), Some(&p.id)), Some(&p)).as_deref(), Some("oid-1g"));

        // An edited profile whose bound sign-in expired: the same person's live sign-in in that tenant.
        let old = remember_sign_in(&s, "oid-2", "tenant-2", false);
        remember_guest_sign_in(&s, &old, "oid-2b", "tenant-2", true);
        let q = Profile::entra("F", "myns.servicebus.windows.net", "oid-2", None);
        s.store.insert(&q).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(used(&entra(None, Some(&q.id)), Some(&q)).as_deref(), Some("oid-2b"));

        // Expired with no replacement: none, so the test reports "sign in again" as connecting would,
        // never the current account instead.
        remember_sign_in(&s, "oid-3", "tenant-3", false);
        let r = Profile::entra("G", "myns.servicebus.windows.net", "oid-3", None);
        s.store.insert(&r).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(used(&entra(None, Some(&r.id)), Some(&r)), None);
    }
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn a_profile_is_listed_under_the_sign_in_it_connects_with() {
        use crate::ops::test_util::{remember_guest_sign_in, remember_sign_in};
        let s = state();
        let home = remember_sign_in(&s, "oid-1", "tenant-1", true);
        s.store.set_current_sign_in(Some(&home.id)).unwrap();
        remember_guest_sign_in(&s, &home, "oid-1g", "tenant-guest", true);
        // Saved with the home sign-in, asking for the guest tenant: connects with the same person's
        // own sign-in there (bound_sign_in), so it is listed under that row.
        let p = dispatched_core::profiles::Profile::entra("Guest NS", "g.servicebus.windows.net", "oid-1", Some("tenant-guest"));
        s.store.insert(&p).unwrap();
        let list = sign_ins(&s).unwrap();
        let h = list.iter().find(|v| v.id == "oid-1").unwrap();
        let g = list.iter().find(|v| v.id == "oid-1g").unwrap();
        assert!(h.profiles.is_empty(), "{:?}", h.profiles);
        assert_eq!(g.profiles, ["Guest NS"]);
        assert!(g.same_account && !g.current);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn sign_out_forgets_the_sign_in_and_clears_current() {
        let s = state();
        let ev = Recorder::default();
        let home = crate::ops::test_util::remember_sign_in(&s, "oid-1", "tenant-1", true);
        s.store.set_current_sign_in(Some(&home.id)).unwrap();
        sign_out(&s, &ev, "oid-1").await.unwrap();
        assert!(s.store.sign_in("oid-1").unwrap().is_none());
        assert!(s.current_sign_in().unwrap().is_none());
        assert_eq!(sign_out(&s, &ev, "oid-1").await.unwrap_err().kind, "not_found");
    }

    #[test]
    fn open_sign_in_page_needs_a_waiting_prompt() {
        let s = state();
        assert_eq!(open_sign_in_page(&s).unwrap_err().kind, "not_found");
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`connstr.rs`:

```rust
/// `cs` scoped to `entity` (`EntityPath=` replaced or added). Refuses a string that is
/// already scoped to another entity, since its key works only there. The result is a secret.
pub fn entity_connection_string(cs: &str, entity: &crate::EntityPath) -> Result<SecretString> {
    let parsed = ConnectionString::parse(cs)?;
    let base = entity.base();
    if let Some(scoped) = &parsed.entity_path {
        let same = crate::EntityPath::parse(scoped).map(|p| p.base().eq_ignore_ascii_case(&base)).unwrap_or(false);
        if !same {
            return Err(Error::Invalid(format!("this connection string only works for `{scoped}`")));
        }
    }
    let mut parts: Vec<String> = cs
        .trim()
        .split(';')
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && !s.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("entitypath="))
        .map(str::to_string)
        .collect();
    parts.push(format!("EntityPath={base}"));
    Ok(SecretString::from(parts.join(";")))
}
```

(`use secrecy::ExposeSecret;` in the test module.)

`profiles/mod.rs`: extract the transport and proxy tail of `namespace_for` into one helper (its last four lines become `with_options(ns, profile.transport, profile.proxy.as_deref())`), and build drafts on `namespace_for` so a test connects exactly as connecting will:

```rust
use store::normalize_tenant;

/// Apply a transport and proxy (then the environment's proxy) to `ns`.
fn with_options(ns: Namespace, transport: Transport, proxy: Option<&str>) -> Result<Namespace> {
    let ns = ns.with_transport(transport)?;
    let proxy = proxy.map(ProxyConfig::parse).transpose()?;
    with_env_proxy(ns.with_proxy(proxy)?)
}

/// The namespace an unsaved draft points at, so it can be tested before it is saved. Nothing is
/// stored. A typed connection string is used as is. Otherwise the draft becomes the profile it
/// would save as, made only for this call: `existing` (a connection-string profile, for its stored
/// string) or an Entra profile bound to `sign_in`, its tenant normalised as a saved profile's is
/// (`None` when it is `sign_in`'s own tenant). That profile goes through [`namespace_for`].
pub fn namespace_for_draft(
    draft: &ProfileDraft,
    existing: Option<&Profile>,
    secrets: &dyn CredentialStore,
    entra: &Arc<EntraSession>,
    sign_in: Option<&SignIn>,
) -> Result<Namespace> {
    let proxy = draft.proxy.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|p| !p.is_empty());
    if let DraftAuth::ConnectionString { connection_string } = &draft.auth
        && let Some(text) = connection_string.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
    {
        let ns = Namespace::from_connection_string(&ConnectionString::parse(text)?)?;
        return with_options(ns, draft.transport, proxy);
    }
    let mut transient = match &draft.auth {
        DraftAuth::ConnectionString { .. } => existing
            .filter(|p| matches!(p.auth, ProfileAuth::ConnectionString))
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::Invalid("paste the namespace's connection string".into()))?,
        DraftAuth::Entra { namespace, tenant } => {
            let s = sign_in.ok_or_else(|| Error::NotSignedIn("sign in with Entra ID first, then test the connection".into()))?;
            let tenant = normalize_tenant(tenant.as_deref(), Some(&s.tenant_id));
            Profile::entra(draft.name.trim(), &namespace_host(namespace)?, &s.id, tenant.as_deref())
        }
    };
    transient.transport = draft.transport;
    transient.proxy = proxy.map(str::to_string);
    namespace_for(&transient, secrets, entra, sign_in)
}
```

`profiles/store.rs`:

```rust
    /// Every remembered sign-in, ordered by id. A record that can't be read is skipped, so one
    /// damaged row does not break the list (or any lookup built on it).
    pub fn sign_ins(&self) -> Result<Vec<SignIn>> {
        let conn = self.conn.lock().expect("profile db poisoned");
        let mut stmt = conn.prepare("SELECT record FROM sign_ins ORDER BY id").map_err(|e| self.err(e))?;
        let records = stmt
            .query_map([], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))
            .map_err(|e| self.err(e))?
            .collect::<std::result::Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
            .map_err(|e| self.err(e))?;
        Ok(records.iter().filter_map(|r| serde_json::from_str::<SignIn>(r).ok()).collect())
    }
```

(`sign_ins_for_tenant` keeps its doc and becomes `Ok(self.sign_ins()?.into_iter().filter(|s| s.tenant_id.eq_ignore_ascii_case(tenant_id)).collect())`; M1b's `an_unreadable_sign_in_row_is_skipped_not_fatal` must still pass.)

`app/src-tauri/src/ops/profiles.rs`:

```rust
use dispatched_core::connstr::entity_connection_string;
use dispatched_core::profiles::{namespace_for_draft, secret_key};
use secrecy::ExposeSecret;

/// The sign-in a draft test uses: for an edited Entra profile, the one connecting uses
/// (`AppState::usable_sign_in`: the same person's own sign-in in the draft's tenant first, then
/// the bound one, then the same person's live sign-in in the bound one's tenant); for a new
/// one, the one saving would bind (`entra_sign_in`). `None` for connection strings.
pub fn draft_sign_in(state: &AppState, draft: &ProfileDraft, existing: Option<&Profile>) -> Result<Option<SignIn>, CommandError> {
    let DraftAuth::Entra { tenant, .. } = &draft.auth else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let tenant = tenant.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|t| !t.is_empty());
    match existing.map(|p| &p.auth) {
        Some(ProfileAuth::Entra { sign_in_id, .. }) => Ok(state.usable_sign_in(sign_in_id, tenant)?.1),
        _ => entra_sign_in(state, tenant),
    }
}

/// Test a draft before it is saved. The draft may carry a connection string: it is used
/// for this test only and never logged.
pub async fn test_draft(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, draft: ProfileDraft) -> Result<ConnectionReport, CommandError> {
    let name = draft.name.trim().to_string();
    let existing = match &draft.id {
        Some(id) => state.store.get_by_id(id)?,
        None => None,
    };
    tracked(
        ev,
        "Testing the connection failed",
        async {
            let sign_in = draft_sign_in(state, &draft, existing.as_ref())?;
            let ns = namespace_for_draft(&draft, existing.as_ref(), state.secrets.as_ref(), &state.session, sign_in.as_ref())?;
            let explorer = Explorer::new(ns)?;
            let report = test_connection(&explorer, None).await;
            explorer.close().await;
            Ok(report)
        },
        |r| tested_line(&name, check_word(&r.management), check_word(&r.messaging)),
    )
    .await
}

/// The activity line of a draft test.
pub fn tested_line(name: &str, management: &str, messaging: &str) -> String {
    format!("Tested {name} (unsaved): management {management}, messaging {messaging}")
}

/// Put `entity`'s connection string on the clipboard through `put`. The string never
/// leaves Rust otherwise; the activity line names the entity only.
pub async fn copy_entity_connection_string(
    state: &AppState,
    ev: &dyn Events,
    profile_id: &str,
    entity: &str,
    put: impl FnOnce(String) -> Result<(), CommandError>,
) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    let p = state.store.get_by_id(profile_id)?.ok_or_else(|| CommandError::new("not_found", "That profile no longer exists."))?;
    let path = dispatched_core::EntityPath::parse(entity)?;
    tracked(
        ev,
        &format!("Copying the connection string for {path} failed"),
        async {
            if !matches!(p.auth, ProfileAuth::ConnectionString) {
                return Err(CommandError::new("invalid", "Entra ID connections have no connection string. Copy the URL instead."));
            }
            let secret = state.secrets.get(&secret_key(&p.id))?.ok_or_else(|| {
                CommandError::new("invalid", format!("{} has no stored connection string. Edit the connection and paste it again.", p.name))
            })?;
            let scoped = entity_connection_string(secret.expose_secret(), &path)?;
            put(scoped.expose_secret().to_string())
        },
        |_| format!("Copied the connection string for {path}"),
    )
    .await
}
```

(`secrecy = { workspace = true }` moves from dev-dependencies to dependencies in `app/src-tauri/Cargo.toml`.)

`app/src-tauri/src/ops/account.rs`: in `sign_in`, both prompt callbacks also store the URL (`*state.last_prompt_url.lock().unwrap() = Some(url.clone())`), and the URL is cleared when `sign_in` returns (success, failure or cancel). Add (the list item is `StoredSignIn`: `SignInView` already names what `sign_in` returns):

```rust
/// One remembered sign-in, for the Settings list.
#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct StoredSignIn {
    pub id: String,
    pub user_principal: String,
    pub display_name: Option<String>,
    pub tenant_id: String,
    /// It is the current account's sign-in.
    pub current: bool,
    /// Its refresh token exists.
    pub signed_in: bool,
    /// It is the current account's identity (same object id or user principal); another
    /// account's kept tenant sign-in is not.
    pub same_account: bool,
    /// Names of the profiles that connect with this sign-in now (`bound_sign_in`), which may be
    /// the same person's tenant sign-in rather than the one the profile was saved with.
    pub profiles: Vec<String>,
}

pub fn sign_ins(state: &AppState) -> Result<Vec<StoredSignIn>, CommandError> {
    let current = state.current_sign_in()?;
    let mut users: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
    for p in state.store.list()? {
        if let Some(b) = crate::ops::profiles::bound_sign_in(state, &p)? {
            users.push((b.id, p.name));
        }
    }
    state
        .store
        .sign_ins()?
        .into_iter()
        .map(|s| {
            let profiles = users.iter().filter(|(id, _)| *id == s.id).map(|(_, name)| name.clone()).collect();
            Ok(StoredSignIn {
                signed_in: state.session.is_signed_in(&s)?,
                current: current.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| c.id == s.id),
                same_account: current.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| same_identity(c, &s)),
                id: s.id,
                user_principal: s.user_principal,
                display_name: s.display_name,
                tenant_id: s.tenant_id,
                profiles,
            })
        })
        .collect()
}

pub async fn sign_out(state: &AppState, ev: &dyn Events, id: &str) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    let s = state.store.sign_in(id)?.ok_or_else(|| CommandError::new("not_found", "That sign-in is already gone."))?;
    let who = s.user_principal.clone();
    tracked(
        ev,
        "Signing out failed",
        async {
            state.session.logout(&s)?;
            state.store.remove_sign_in(&s.id)?;
            if state.store.current_sign_in()?.is_none_or(|c| c.id == s.id) {
                state.store.set_current_sign_in(None)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        },
        |_| format!("Signed out {who}"),
    )
    .await
}

/// Open the page of the sign-in that is waiting (device code), in the default browser.
pub fn open_sign_in_page(state: &AppState) -> Result<(), CommandError> {
    let url = state.last_prompt_url.lock().unwrap().clone().ok_or_else(|| CommandError::new("not_found", "No sign-in is waiting."))?;
    open::that(&url).map_err(|e| CommandError::new("io", format!("Couldn't open the browser: {e}")))
}
```

(Removing the current sign-in's record makes `current_sign_in` read `None`; the explicit clear keeps the settings row tidy.)

`app/src/engine/peek.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createQueryClient, setTransport } from "@/engine";
import { createMockTransport } from "./mock/transport";
import { usePeekStream, type PeekStreamOptions } from "./peek";

function setup(scenario: Parameters<typeof createMockTransport>[0] = "default", delayMs = 0) {
  const mock = createMockTransport(scenario, delayMs, { last_profile: "p-logistics" });
  setTransport(mock);
  const client = createQueryClient();
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => <QueryClientProvider client={client}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>;
  return { mock, wrapper };
}

async function connected(mock: ReturnType<typeof createMockTransport>) {
  await mock.invoke("connect", { profileId: "p-logistics" });
}

const base: PeekStreamOptions = {
  profileId: "p-logistics",
  entity: "shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync",
  view: "dead-letter",
  from: { kind: "start" },
  topN: 1200,
  filter: {},
};

describe("usePeekStream", () => {
  it("streams pages until Top N and asks for no more", async () => {
    const { mock, wrapper } = setup();
    await connected(mock);
    const { result } = renderHook(() => usePeekStream(base), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages).toHaveLength(1200));
    const pages = mock.calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === "peek_page");
    expect(pages.map((c) => (c.args as { request: { max: number } }).request.max)).toEqual([500, 500, 200]);
    expect(result.current.canPeekMore).toBe(true);
    expect(result.current.exhausted).toBe(false);
  });

  it("peek more reads another Top N", async () => {
    const { mock, wrapper } = setup();
    await connected(mock);
    const { result } = renderHook(() => usePeekStream({ ...base, topN: 100 }), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages).toHaveLength(100));
    act(() => result.current.peekMore());
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages).toHaveLength(200));
  });

  it("stream stops at the scan budget and reports it", async () => {
    const { mock, wrapper } = setup("many-messages");
    await connected(mock);
    const nothing = { property: "sys.Subject = 'no-such-subject'" };
    const { result } = renderHook(() => usePeekStream({ ...base, entity: "customs-docs", view: "active", filter: nothing, scanBudget: 6_000 }), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.budgetHit).toBe(true));
    expect(result.current.messages).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(result.current.scanned).toBe(10_000);
    expect(result.current.canPeekMore).toBe(true);
  });

  it("stop keeps the rows already loaded", async () => {
    // A 20 ms mock, so a page is still in flight when Stop runs (a 0 ms one could finish first).
    const { mock, wrapper } = setup("default", 20);
    await connected(mock);
    const { result } = renderHook(() => usePeekStream({ ...base, topN: 3000 }), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(500));
    act(() => result.current.stop());
    const kept = result.current.messages.length;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
    expect(result.current.messages.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(kept + 500);
    expect(result.current.stopped).toBe(true);
  });

  it("from the end returns the last N with no continuation", async () => {
    const { mock, wrapper } = setup();
    await connected(mock);
    const { result } = renderHook(() => usePeekStream({ ...base, from: { kind: "end" }, topN: 10 }), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages).toHaveLength(10));
    const seqs = result.current.messages.map((m) => m.sequence_number);
    expect(seqs).toEqual([...seqs].sort((a, b) => a - b));
    expect(seqs.at(-1)).toBe(48213);
    expect(result.current.canPeekMore).toBe(false);
    expect(result.current.exhausted).toBe(true);
  });

  it("peek stream ignores pages after the key changes", async () => {
    const { mock, wrapper } = setup();
    await connected(mock);
    const { result, rerender } = renderHook((o: PeekStreamOptions) => usePeekStream(o), { wrapper, initialProps: base });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages.length).toBeGreaterThan(0));
    rerender({ ...base, entity: "invoice-generate" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.messages).toHaveLength(37));
    expect(result.current.messages.every((m) => m.sequence_number < 44_000)).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe("M1c commands on the mock", () => {
  it("peeks summaries, reads one message, and refuses another profile", async () => {
    const mock = createMockTransport("default", 0);
    setTransport(mock);
    await commands.connect("p-logistics");
    const page = await commands.peekPage("p-logistics", { entity: "invoice-generate", view: "dead-letter", from: { kind: "start" }, max: 10, filter: {} });
    expect(page.messages).toHaveLength(10);
    expect(page.messages[0]).not.toHaveProperty("body");
    expect(page.messages[0].dead_letter_reason).toBeTruthy();
    const d = await commands.messageDetail("p-logistics", "invoice-generate", "dead-letter", page.messages[0].sequence_number);
    expect(d?.body.kind).toBeDefined();
    expect(await commands.messageDetail("p-logistics", "invoice-generate", "dead-letter", 1)).toBeNull();
    await expect(commands.peekPage("p-other", { entity: "x", view: "active", from: { kind: "start" }, max: 1, filter: {} })).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: "not_connected" });

    expect(issues.map((i) => i.field)).toEqual(["property", "body_regex"]);

    const mock = createMockTransport("default", 0);
    setTransport(mock);
    await commands.connect("p-logistics");

    await mock.listen("job", (e) => events.push(e));
    const path = await commands.pickExportPath("invoice-generate", "dead-letter", "json");
    expect(path).toMatch(/invoice-generate-dead-letter-\d{8}-\d{4}\.json$/);
    const { id } = await commands.exportStart("p-logistics", { entity: "invoice-generate", view: "dead-letter", scope: { kind: "all", filter: {} }, format: "json", path: path!, overwrite: true, total_hint: 37 });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 700));
    expect(events.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ type: "finished", id, error: null, summary: expect.stringContaining("Exported 37 messages") });
    const slow = createMockTransport("export-slow", 0);
    setTransport(slow);
    await commands.connect("p-logistics");
    const done: unknown[] = [];
    await slow.listen("job", (e) => done.push(e));
    const job = await commands.exportStart("p-logistics", { entity: "invoice-generate", view: "dead-letter", scope: { kind: "all", filter: {} }, format: "json", path: "/tmp/x.json", overwrite: true });
    expect(await commands.jobCancel(job.id)).toBe(true);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
    expect(done.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ type: "finished", error: { kind: "cancelled", message: "Export cancelled." } });
  });

  it("lists sessions, rules, details and the dashboard", async () => {
    setTransport(createMockTransport("default", 0));
    await commands.connect("p-logistics");
    const s = await commands.listSessions("p-logistics", "returns-intake");
    expect(s.sessions.map((x) => x.id)).toContain("RMA-1001");
    expect(s.sessions.find((x) => x.id === "RMA-0999")?.messages).toBe(0);
    const rules = await commands.listRules("p-logistics", "shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync");
    expect(rules.map((r) => r.filter.kind)).toEqual(["sql", "sql", "correlation"]);
    const d = await commands.entityDetails("p-logistics", "queue", "invoice-generate");
    expect(d.counts?.dead_letter).toBe(37);
    expect(d.properties.some((p) => p.name === "LockDuration")).toBe(true);
    const dash = await commands.dashboard("p-logistics");
    expect(dash.rows.some((r) => r.id === "shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("never returns a connection string from the copy command", async () => {
    setTransport(createMockTransport("default", 0));
    expect(await commands.copyEntityConnectionString("p-logistics", "orders")).toBeNull();
    await expect(commands.copyEntityConnectionString("p-logistics-dev", "orders")).rejects.toMatchObject({ kind: "invalid" });
  });
});
```

```ts
export type PeekView = "active" | "scheduled" | "deferred" | "dead-letter" | "transfer-dead-letter";
export type MessageState = "active" | "deferred" | "scheduled";
export type PropValue =
  | { type: "null" }
  | { type: "bool"; value: boolean }
  | { type: "int" | "uint" | "double"; value: number }
  | { type: "string" | "timestamp" | "uuid" | "binary" | "other"; value: string };
/** `binary` values are base64. `value` and `sequence` bodies are AMQP values rendered as JSON. */
export type MessageBody =
  | { kind: "binary"; value: string }
  | { kind: "text"; value: string }
  | { kind: "value" | "sequence"; value: unknown }
  | { kind: "empty" };

interface MessageFields {
  sequence_number: number;
  state: MessageState;
  message_id: string | null;
  correlation_id: string | null;
  session_id: string | null;
  subject: string | null;
  content_type: string | null;
  to: string | null;
  reply_to: string | null;
  enqueued_time: string | null;
  scheduled_enqueue_time: string | null;
  delivery_count: number;
  time_to_live_ms: number | null;
  dead_letter_reason: string | null;
  dead_letter_description: string | null;
  dead_letter_source: string | null;
  application_properties: Record<string, PropValue>;
}

/** A grid row: no body, a 160-character preview. */
export interface MessageSummary extends MessageFields {
  body_kind: MessageBody["kind"];
  body_size: number;
  body_preview: string | null;
}

/** One message; bodies above 4 MiB arrive cut, with `body_truncated`. */
export interface MessageDetail extends MessageFields {
  locked_until: string | null;
  body: MessageBody;
  body_size: number;
  body_truncated: boolean;
}

export interface FilterSpec {
  property?: string | null;
  body_contains?: string | null;
  body_regex?: string | null;
  body_json_path?: string | null;
  body_json_equals?: string | null;
  session_id?: string | null;
}
export type FilterField = "property" | "body_regex" | "body_json_path" | "body_json_equals";
export interface FilterIssue { field: FilterField; message: string }

export type PeekFrom = { kind: "start" } | { kind: "sequence"; sequence: number } | { kind: "end" };
export interface PeekRequest { entity: string; view: PeekView; from: PeekFrom; max: number; scan_limit?: number | null; filter: FilterSpec }
export interface PeekPageView { messages: MessageSummary[]; scanned: number; next_sequence: number | null; exhausted: boolean }

export type EntityKindName = "queue" | "topic" | "subscription";
export interface EntityProperty { name: string; value: string }
export interface EntityDetails {
  kind: EntityKindName;
  name: string;
  topic: string | null;
  status: string | null;
  counts: EntityCounts | null;
  size_in_bytes: number | null;
  properties: EntityProperty[];
}

export interface CorrelationProperty { name: string; value: string }
export type RuleFilter =
  | { kind: "sql"; expression: string }
  | {
      kind: "correlation";
      correlation_id: string | null; message_id: string | null; to: string | null; reply_to: string | null;
      subject: string | null; session_id: string | null; reply_to_session_id: string | null; content_type: string | null;
      properties: CorrelationProperty[];
    };
export interface RuleInfo { name: string; filter: RuleFilter; action: string | null; created_at: string | null }

export interface SessionSummary { id: string; messages: number | null; first_enqueued: string | null; last_enqueued: string | null }
export interface SessionListing { sessions: SessionSummary[]; from_broker: boolean; scanned: number; truncated: boolean }

export interface DashboardRow { kind: "queue" | "subscription"; id: string; name: string; topic: string | null; status: string | null; counts: EntityCounts | null }
export interface DashboardFailure { topic: string; kind: string; message: string }
export interface DashboardView { rows: DashboardRow[]; failures: DashboardFailure[]; limited: string | null }

export type ExportScope =
  | { kind: "all"; filter: FilterSpec; from_sequence?: number; max?: number | null }
  | { kind: "selected"; sequences: number[] };
export interface ExportRequest { entity: string; view: PeekView; scope: ExportScope; format: "json" | "ndjson"; path: string; overwrite: boolean; total_hint?: number | null }
export interface JobStarted { id: string }

/** One remembered sign-in in Settings (Rust `StoredSignIn`). `SignInView` stays what `signIn` returns. */
export interface StoredSignIn {
  id: string; user_principal: string; display_name: string | null; tenant_id: string;
  current: boolean; signed_in: boolean;
  /** The current account's identity; another account's kept tenant sign-in is not. */
  same_account: boolean;
  /** Profiles that connect with this sign-in now (`bound_sign_in`). */
  profiles: string[];
}
```

```ts
export function useMessageDetail(profileId: string | null, entity: string, view: PeekView, sequence: number | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.message(profileId, entity, view, sequence),
    queryFn: () => commands.messageDetail(profileId!, entity, view, sequence!),
    enabled: profileId !== null && sequence !== null,
    staleTime: Infinity,
    gcTime: 60_000,
  });
}

export function useEntityDetails(profileId: string | null, kind: EntityKindName, entity: string, refetchMs = 0) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.details(profileId, kind, entity),
    queryFn: () => commands.entityDetails(profileId!, kind, entity),
    enabled: profileId !== null,
    refetchInterval: refetchMs > 0 ? refetchMs : false,
    placeholderData: (prev, prevQuery) => (prevQuery?.queryKey[1] === profileId ? prev : undefined),
  });
}

export const useRules = (profileId: string | null, entity: string, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: keys.rules(profileId, entity), queryFn: () => commands.listRules(profileId!, entity), enabled: enabled && profileId !== null });

export const useSessions = (profileId: string | null, entity: string, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: keys.sessions(profileId, entity), queryFn: () => commands.listSessions(profileId!, entity), enabled: enabled && profileId !== null });

export const useDashboard = (profileId: string | null, refetchMs = 0) =>
  useQuery({
    queryKey: keys.dashboard(profileId),
    queryFn: () => commands.dashboard(profileId!),
    enabled: profileId !== null,
    refetchInterval: refetchMs > 0 ? refetchMs : false,
  });

const isEmptyFilter = (f: FilterSpec) => !Object.values(f).some((v) => typeof v === "string" && v.trim() !== "");

/** Problems with a filter, checked by the engine; an empty filter needs no call (`data` stays undefined: no issues). */
export const useFilterCheck = (filter: FilterSpec) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: ["filter-check", filter], queryFn: () => commands.checkFilter(filter), enabled: !isEmptyFilter(filter), staleTime: Infinity, gcTime: 30_000 });

export const useStartExport = () =>
  useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ profileId, request }: { profileId: string; request: ExportRequest }) => commands.exportStart(profileId, request) });

export const useSignIns = () => useQuery({ queryKey: keys.signIns, queryFn: commands.signInsList });

export function useSignOut() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => commands.signOut(id),
    // Signing out changes which profiles can connect (`bound_sign_in.live`), the tenants and the discovery too.
    onSuccess: () =>
      Promise.all(
        [keys.signIns, keys.account, keys.profiles, ["tenants"], ["discovery"]].map((queryKey) => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey })),
      ),
  });
}

/** The draft can carry a connection string: never kept in the mutation cache. */
export const useTestDraft = () => useMutation({ mutationFn: (draft: ProfileDraft) => commands.profileTestDraft(draft), gcTime: 0 });
```

`app/src/engine/peek.ts`:

```ts
import { useInfiniteQuery, type InfiniteData } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import * as commands from "./commands";
import type { EngineError } from "./ipc";
import type { FilterSpec, MessageSummary, PeekFrom, PeekPageView, PeekView } from "./types";

/** Summaries per page (the engine's cap). */
export const PAGE_SIZE = 500;
/** Most messages "from the end" returns. */
export const TAIL_MAX = 5_000;
/** Most messages one load examines before asking the user to continue. */
export const SCAN_BUDGET = 100_000;

export interface PeekStreamOptions {
  profileId: string | null;
  entity: string;
  view: PeekView;
  from: PeekFrom;
  /** Matches to load per round ("Top N"). */
  topN: number;
  filter: FilterSpec;
  enabled?: boolean;
  /** Tests lower it; the app uses SCAN_BUDGET. */
  scanBudget?: number;
}

export interface PeekStream {
  messages: MessageSummary[];
  scanned: number;
  /** The first page is on its way. */
  loading: boolean;
  /** Further pages are on their way. */
  streaming: boolean;
  error: EngineError | null;
  /** The end of the view was reached. */
  exhausted: boolean;
  /** This round examined the scan budget without reaching Top N. */
  budgetHit: boolean;
  stopped: boolean;
  /** A continuation exists. */
  canPeekMore: boolean;
  peekMore(): void;
  stop(): void;
}

interface Param { from: PeekFrom }

/** Peek a view in pages of summaries, continuing until Top N, the scan budget, the end, or Stop. */
export function usePeekStream(o: PeekStreamOptions): PeekStream {
  const tail = o.from.kind === "end";
  const budget = o.scanBudget ?? SCAN_BUDGET;
  const key = ["peek", o.profileId, o.entity, o.view, o.from, o.filter, tail ? Math.min(o.topN, TAIL_MAX) : null] as const;
  const keyText = JSON.stringify(key);
  const [round, setRound] = useState({ key: keyText, n: 1, rows: 0, scanned: 0, stopped: false });
  const current = round.key === keyText ? round : { key: keyText, n: 1, rows: 0, scanned: 0, stopped: false };
  if (current !== round) setRound(current);
  const target = useRef(o.topN);
  target.current = o.topN * current.n;

  const q = useInfiniteQuery<PeekPageView, EngineError, InfiniteData<PeekPageView, Param>, typeof key, Param>({
    queryKey: key,
    initialPageParam: { from: o.from },
    queryFn: ({ pageParam, client, queryKey }) => {
      const loaded = (client.getQueryData<InfiniteData<PeekPageView, Param>>(queryKey)?.pages ?? []).reduce((n, p) => n + p.messages.length, 0);
      const max = tail ? Math.min(o.topN, TAIL_MAX) : Math.max(1, Math.min(PAGE_SIZE, target.current - loaded));
      return commands.peekPage(o.profileId!, { entity: o.entity, view: o.view, from: pageParam.from, max, filter: o.filter });
    },
    getNextPageParam: (last) => (tail || last.next_sequence == null ? undefined : { from: { kind: "sequence", sequence: last.next_sequence } }),
    enabled: (o.enabled ?? true) && o.profileId !== null,
    staleTime: Infinity,
    gcTime: 60_000,
    retry: false,
  });

  const data = q.data;
  const messages = useMemo(() => data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.messages) ?? [], [data]);
  const scanned = useMemo(() => data?.pages.reduce((n, p) => n + p.scanned, 0) ?? 0, [data]);
  const roundScanned = scanned - current.scanned;
  const below = messages.length < target.current;
  const wantMore = !tail && !current.stopped && q.hasNextPage && !q.isFetching && !q.isError && below && roundScanned < budget;
  const { fetchNextPage } = q;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (wantMore) void fetchNextPage();
  }, [wantMore, fetchNextPage, messages.length]);

  const last = data?.pages.at(-1);
  return {
    messages,
    scanned,
    loading: q.isPending && q.fetchStatus !== "idle",
    streaming: q.isFetchingNextPage,
    error: q.error ?? null,
    exhausted: tail ? !!data : !!last?.exhausted,
    budgetHit: !tail && q.hasNextPage && below && roundScanned >= budget,
    stopped: current.stopped,
    canPeekMore: !tail && q.hasNextPage && !q.isFetching,
    peekMore: () => setRound({ ...current, n: current.n + 1, rows: messages.length, scanned, stopped: false }),
    stop: () => setRound({ ...current, stopped: true }),
  };
}
```

(The `queryFn` context exposes `client` in TanStack Query 5.8x; if the installed 5.104 types differ, read the loaded count through a `useRef` that mirrors `messages.length` instead. `peekMore` after the budget was hit starts a fresh budget for the new round.)

`app/src/engine/mock/messages.ts`: a deterministic generator (no `Math.random`):

```ts
import type { MessageDetail, MessageSummary, PeekView, PropValue } from "../types";

/** A mock message: everything the engine returns, with the full body. */
export type MockMessage = MessageDetail & { sub: "main" | "dlq" | "tdlq" };

const REASONS: [string, string][] = [
  ["MaxDeliveryCountExceeded", "Message could not be consumed after 10 delivery attempts."],
  ["TTLExpiredException", "The message expired and was dead lettered."],
  ["SchemaValidationFailed", "Field 'invoiceId' is required."],
];
const SUBJECTS = ["shipment.updated", "shipment.created", "label.requested", "invoice.ready", "customs.cleared"];
const T0 = Date.parse("2026-10-15T09:00:00Z");

/** A small deterministic hash so every run sees the same messages. */
const pick = (i: number, n: number) => ((i * 2654435761) >>> 0) % n;

function body(i: number, kind: number): Pick<MessageDetail, "body" | "content_type" | "body_size"> {
  switch (kind) {
    case 0: {
      // Written by hand so the body carries an integer too large for a JS number (the viewer must keep it exact).
      const json = `{"shipmentId":"SHP-${100000 + i}","status":"${["in_transit", "delivered", "held"][pick(i, 3)]}","weightKg":${1 + pick(i, 40)},"carrier":{"code":"DHL","hub":"LEJ"},"trackingNumber":12345678901234567890}`;
      return { body: { kind: "binary", value: btoa(json) }, content_type: "application/json", body_size: json.length };
    }
    case 1: {
      const xml = `<?xml version="1.0"?><invoice id="INV-${i}"><line sku="A-${pick(i, 90)}">2</line><note><![CDATA[fragile & urgent]]></note></invoice>`;
      return { body: { kind: "binary", value: btoa(xml) }, content_type: "application/xml", body_size: xml.length };
    }
    case 2: {
      const text = `Label ${i} printed at station ${pick(i, 12)}`;
      return { body: { kind: "text", value: text }, content_type: "text/plain", body_size: text.length };
    }
    default: {
      const bytes = String.fromCharCode(0xff, 0xfe, 0x00, 0x01, i & 0xff, 0x7f, 0x80, 0x41);
      return { body: { kind: "binary", value: btoa(bytes) }, content_type: "application/octet-stream", body_size: 8 };
    }
  }
}

export interface Spec { entity: string; sub: "main" | "dlq" | "tdlq"; count: number; firstSeq: number; scheduledEvery?: number; deferredEvery?: number; sessions?: string[] }

export function generate(s: Spec): MockMessage[] {
  return Array.from({ length: s.count }, (_, i) => {
    const seq = s.firstSeq + i;
    const state = s.scheduledEvery && i % s.scheduledEvery === s.scheduledEvery - 1 ? "scheduled" : s.deferredEvery && i % s.deferredEvery === s.deferredEvery - 1 ? "deferred" : "active";
    const dl = s.sub !== "main" ? REASONS[pick(i, REASONS.length)] : null;
    const props: Record<string, PropValue> = {
      tenant: { type: "string", value: ["contoso", "fabrikam", "northwind"][pick(i, 3)] },
      attempt: { type: "int", value: 1 + pick(i, 5) },
      priority: { type: "bool", value: pick(i, 4) === 0 },
    };
    if (dl) {
      props.DeadLetterReason = { type: "string", value: dl[0] };
      props.DeadLetterErrorDescription = { type: "string", value: dl[1] };
    }
    const enq = new Date(T0 - (s.count - i) * 37_000).toISOString();
    return {
      sub: s.sub,
      sequence_number: seq,
      state,
      message_id: `msg-${s.entity.replace(/\W+/g, "-")}-${seq}`,
      correlation_id: pick(i, 3) === 0 ? `corr-${pick(i, 999)}` : null,
      session_id: s.sessions ? s.sessions[i % s.sessions.length] : null,
      subject: SUBJECTS[pick(i, SUBJECTS.length)],
      to: null,
      reply_to: null,
      enqueued_time: enq,
      scheduled_enqueue_time: state === "scheduled" ? new Date(T0 + 3_600_000 + i * 60_000).toISOString() : null,
      locked_until: null,
      delivery_count: dl ? 10 : pick(i, 3),
      time_to_live_ms: null,
      dead_letter_reason: dl?.[0] ?? null,
      dead_letter_description: dl?.[1] ?? null,
      dead_letter_source: null,
      application_properties: props,
      body_truncated: false,
      ...body(i, pick(i + 7, 4)),
    };
  });
}

export function summarize(m: MockMessage): MessageSummary {
  const { body: b, body_truncated: _t, locked_until: _l, sub: _s, ...rest } = m;
  let text: string | null = null;
  if (b.kind === "text") text = b.value;
  else if (b.kind === "binary") {
    const raw = atob(b.value);
    text = /^[\x09\x0a\x0d\x20-\x7e]*$/.test(raw) ? raw : null;
  } else if (b.kind !== "empty") text = JSON.stringify(b.value);
  const preview = text ? text.split(/\s+/).join(" ").trim().slice(0, 160) || null : null;
  return { ...rest, body_kind: b.kind, body_size: m.body_size, body_preview: preview };
}

export function accepts(view: PeekView, m: MockMessage): boolean {
  if (view === "dead-letter") return m.sub === "dlq";
  if (view === "transfer-dead-letter") return m.sub === "tdlq";
  return m.sub === "main" && m.state === (view === "active" ? "active" : view);
}
```

```ts
import { generate, type MockMessage } from "./messages";

export const RMA = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => `RMA-${1001 + i}`);

/** Messages per entity, lazily generated once. Counts match the listing (invoice-generate: 1,288 generated =
 *  1,284 active + 3 scheduled + 1 deferred; the listing says 1,284 active). */
const specs = (scenario: string): Record<string, Parameters<typeof generate>[0][]> => ({
  "customs-docs": [{ entity: "customs-docs", sub: "main", count: scenario === "many-messages" ? 12_000 : 112, firstSeq: 1 }],
  "invoice-generate": [
    { entity: "invoice-generate", sub: "main", count: 1288, firstSeq: 20_000, scheduledEvery: 400, deferredEvery: 600 },
    { entity: "invoice-generate", sub: "dlq", count: 37, firstSeq: 41_000 },
    { entity: "invoice-generate", sub: "tdlq", count: 2, firstSeq: 41_500 },
  ],
  "label-print": [{ entity: "label-print", sub: "main", count: 6, firstSeq: 300 }],
  "returns-intake": [
    { entity: "returns-intake", sub: "main", count: 48, firstSeq: 9_000, sessions: RMA },
    { entity: "returns-intake", sub: "dlq", count: 211, firstSeq: 8_000 },
  ],
  "shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync": [
    { entity: "tracking-sync", sub: "main", count: 2906, firstSeq: 48_300 },
    { entity: "tracking-sync", sub: "dlq", count: 3417, firstSeq: 48_213 - 3416 },
  ],
  "carrier-updates/subscriptions/eta-recalc": [
    { entity: "eta-recalc", sub: "main", count: 58, firstSeq: 700 },
    { entity: "eta-recalc", sub: "dlq", count: 9, firstSeq: 650 },
  ],
  "shipment-events/subscriptions/warehouse-notify": [
    { entity: "warehouse-notify", sub: "main", count: 14, firstSeq: 1_000 },
    { entity: "warehouse-notify", sub: "dlq", count: 1, firstSeq: 990 },
  ],
});

const cache = new Map<string, MockMessage[]>();
export function messagesOf(entity: string, scenario: string): MockMessage[] {
  const key = `${scenario === "many-messages" ? "many" : "default"}|${entity}`;
  let all = cache.get(key);
  if (!all) {
    all = (specs(scenario)[entity] ?? []).flatMap(generate);
    // The newest tracking-sync dead letter has a 5 MiB body that arrives cut.
    const big = all.find((m) => m.sub === "dlq" && m.sequence_number === 48_213);
    if (big) Object.assign(big, { body_size: 5 * 1024 * 1024, body_truncated: true });
    cache.set(key, all);
  }
  return all;
}

export const details = (kind: "queue" | "topic" | "subscription", c: EntityCounts | null, session: boolean) => [
  { name: "LockDuration", value: "PT30S" },
  { name: "MaxSizeInMegabytes", value: "1024" },
  { name: "RequiresDuplicateDetection", value: "false" },
  { name: "RequiresSession", value: String(session) },
  { name: "DefaultMessageTimeToLive", value: "P14D" },
  { name: "DeadLetteringOnMessageExpiration", value: "true" },
  { name: "MaxDeliveryCount", value: "10" },
  { name: "EnableBatchedOperations", value: "true" },
  ...(kind === "queue" ? [{ name: "EnablePartitioning", value: "false" }] : []),
  { name: "AutoDeleteOnIdle", value: "P10675199DT2H48M5.4775807S" },
  { name: "CreatedAt", value: "2026-09-01T08:00:00Z" },
  { name: "UpdatedAt", value: "2026-10-01T08:00:00Z" },
  ...(c ? [{ name: "MessageCount", value: String(c.active + c.dead_letter) }] : []),
];

export const rules: Record<string, RuleInfo[]> = {
  "shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync": [
    { name: "$Default", filter: { kind: "sql", expression: "1=1" }, action: null, created_at: "2026-09-01T08:00:00Z" },
    { name: "eu-only", filter: { kind: "sql", expression: "region = 'eu' AND sys.Label LIKE 'shipment%'" }, action: "SET priority = 'high'", created_at: "2026-09-12T10:30:00Z" },
    {
      name: "priority",
      filter: { kind: "correlation", correlation_id: "batch-7", message_id: null, to: null, reply_to: null, subject: "priority", session_id: null, reply_to_session_id: null, content_type: null, properties: [{ name: "region", value: "eu" }, { name: "tier", value: "2" }] },
      action: null,
      created_at: null,
    },
  ],
};
```

In `transport.ts`, add the scenarios `"many-messages" | "export-slow"` to `Scenario`, and these `case`s to `handle` (shared helpers above the switch):

```ts
const connectedTo = (profileId: unknown) => {
  if (!active || active.id !== profileId) throw new EngineError("not_connected", "Choose a connection first.");
};
const SIMPLE = /^\s*(sys\.)?([A-Za-z_][\w.-]*)\s*(=|<>|>=|<=|>|<)\s*('([^']*)'|-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$/;
function issuesOf(f: FilterSpec): FilterIssue[] {
  const out: FilterIssue[] = [];
  const p = f.property?.trim();
  if (p && !SIMPLE.test(p)) {
    const at = p.indexOf("==");
    out.push({ field: "property", message: at >= 0 ? `filter: unexpected \`=\` at column ${at + 2}` : `filter: expected a value at column ${p.length + 1}` });
  }
  if (f.body_regex?.trim()) {
    try { new RegExp(f.body_regex); } catch (e) { out.push({ field: "body_regex", message: `body regex: ${(e as Error).message}` }); }
  }
  if (f.body_json_equals?.trim() && !f.body_json_path?.trim()) out.push({ field: "body_json_equals", message: "add a JSONPath to compare this value with" });
  return out;
}
function field(m: MockMessage, name: string, sys: boolean): unknown {
  if (sys) {
    const map: Record<string, unknown> = { subject: m.subject, label: m.subject, messageid: m.message_id, sessionid: m.session_id, deliverycount: m.delivery_count, sequencenumber: m.sequence_number, deadletterreason: m.dead_letter_reason, correlationid: m.correlation_id };
    return map[name.toLowerCase()];
  }
  const v = m.application_properties[name];
  return v && "value" in v ? v.value : undefined;
}
function textOf(m: MockMessage): string {
  const b = m.body;
  return b.kind === "text" ? b.value : b.kind === "binary" ? atob(b.value) : b.kind === "empty" ? "" : JSON.stringify(b.value);
}
function matches(m: MockMessage, f: FilterSpec): boolean {
  if (f.session_id && m.session_id !== f.session_id) return false;
  const p = f.property?.trim();
  const s = p ? SIMPLE.exec(p) : null;
  if (s) {
    const v = field(m, s[2], !!s[1]);
    const lit: unknown = s[5] ?? Number(s[4]);
    const cmp = typeof v === "number" && typeof lit === "number" ? v - lit : String(v ?? "").localeCompare(String(lit));
    const ok = { "=": cmp === 0, "<>": cmp !== 0, ">": cmp > 0, "<": cmp < 0, ">=": cmp >= 0, "<=": cmp <= 0 }[s[3] as "="];
    if (v === undefined || v === null || !ok) return false;
  }
  if (f.body_contains && !textOf(m).toLowerCase().includes(f.body_contains.toLowerCase())) return false;
  if (f.body_regex?.trim() && !new RegExp(f.body_regex).test(textOf(m))) return false;
  if (f.body_json_path?.trim()) {
    try {
      let node: unknown = JSON.parse(textOf(m));
      for (const part of f.body_json_path.replace(/^\$\.?/, "").split(".").filter(Boolean)) node = (node as Record<string, unknown>)?.[part];
      if (node === undefined) return false;
      if (f.body_json_equals?.trim() && JSON.stringify(node) !== f.body_json_equals.trim() && String(node) !== f.body_json_equals.trim()) return false;
    } catch { return false; }
  }
  return true;
}
const slug = (s: string) => s.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9._-]/g, "-");
const stamp = () => { const d = new Date(); const p = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0"); return `${d.getFullYear()}${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}${p(d.getDate())}-${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`; };
const jobs = new Map<string, { timer?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>; finish: (error: ActivityError | null) => void }>();
let lastPrompt: string | null = null;
```

```ts
      case "peek_page": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const r = args.request as PeekRequest;
        const bad = issuesOf(r.filter);
        if (bad.length) throw new EngineError("invalid", bad[0].message);
        const all = fx.messagesOf(r.entity, scenario).filter((m) => accepts(r.view, m));
        if (r.from.kind === "end") {
          const hits = all.filter((m) => matches(m, r.filter));
          const out = hits.slice(-Math.min(r.max, 5000));
          return { messages: out.map(summarize), scanned: all.length, next_sequence: null, exhausted: true } satisfies PeekPageView;
        }
        const from = r.from.kind === "sequence" ? r.from.sequence : 0;
        const max = Math.min(r.max, 500);
        const scanCap = Math.min(r.scan_limit ?? 5000, 5000);
        const out: MockMessage[] = [];
        let scanned = 0;
        let i = all.findIndex((m) => m.sequence_number >= from);
        if (i < 0) i = all.length;
        for (; i < all.length && out.length < max && scanned < scanCap; i++) {
          scanned++;
          if (matches(all[i], r.filter)) out.push(all[i]);
        }
        const exhausted = i >= all.length;
        // As Rust: a page is logged only when it fails, never on success.
        return { messages: out.map(summarize), scanned, next_sequence: exhausted ? null : all[i].sequence_number, exhausted } satisfies PeekPageView;
      }
      case "message_detail": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const m = fx.messagesOf(args.entity as string, scenario).find((x) => x.sequence_number === args.sequence && accepts(args.view as PeekView, x));
        if (!m) return null;
        const { sub: _sub, ...detail } = m;
        return structuredClone(detail) satisfies MessageDetail;
      }
      case "check_filter":
        return issuesOf(args.filter as FilterSpec);
      case "entity_details": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const kind = args.kind as EntityKindName;
        const id = args.entity as string;
        const sub = kind === "subscription" ? id.split("/subscriptions/") : null;
        const info = kind === "queue" ? fx.queues.find((x) => x.name === id) : kind === "topic" ? fx.topics.find((x) => x.name === id) : fx.subscriptions[sub![0]]?.find((x) => x.name === sub![1]);
        if (!info) throw new EngineError("not_found", `not found: ${kind} \`${id}\``);
        const c = "counts" in info ? info.counts : null;
        // Polled by Overview: as Rust, only failures are logged.
        return {
          kind, name: sub ? sub[1] : id, topic: sub ? sub[0] : null, status: "Active", counts: c, size_in_bytes: "size_in_bytes" in info ? info.size_in_bytes : null,
          properties: fx.details(kind, c, "requires_session" in info && info.requires_session),
        } satisfies EntityDetails;
      }
      case "list_rules": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const id = args.entity as string;
        if (!id.includes("/subscriptions/")) throw new EngineError("invalid", `${id} is not a subscription; only subscriptions have rules.`);
        const r = fx.rules[id] ?? [{ name: "$Default", filter: { kind: "sql", expression: "1=1" }, action: null, created_at: null }];
        activity(`Listed ${count(r.length, "rule", "rules")} of ${id}`, started);
        return structuredClone(r);
      }
      case "list_sessions": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const id = args.entity as string;
        const msgs = fx.messagesOf(id, scenario).filter((m) => m.sub === "main" && m.session_id);
        const per = new Map<string, MockMessage[]>();
        for (const m of msgs) per.set(m.session_id!, [...(per.get(m.session_id!) ?? []), m]);
        const sessions: SessionSummary[] = [...per.entries()].map(([sid, ms]) => ({ id: sid, messages: ms.length, first_enqueued: ms[0].enqueued_time, last_enqueued: ms.at(-1)!.enqueued_time }));
        if (id === "returns-intake") sessions.push({ id: "RMA-0999", messages: 0, first_enqueued: null, last_enqueued: null });
        sessions.sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
        activity(`Listed ${count(sessions.length, "session", "sessions")} of ${id}`, started);
        return { sessions, from_broker: true, scanned: msgs.length, truncated: false } satisfies SessionListing;
      }
      case "dashboard": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const rows: DashboardRow[] = [
          ...fx.queues.map((q) => ({ kind: "queue" as const, id: q.name, name: q.name, topic: null, status: q.status, counts: q.counts })),
          ...Object.values(fx.subscriptions).flat().map((s) => ({ kind: "subscription" as const, id: `${s.topic}/subscriptions/${s.name}`, name: s.name, topic: s.topic, status: s.status, counts: s.counts })),
        ];
        // Auto-refreshed: as Rust, only failures are logged.
        return { rows, failures: [], limited: null } satisfies DashboardView;
      }
      case "pick_export_path":
        return `/Users/ops/Downloads/${slug(args.entity as string)}-${args.view}-${stamp()}.${args.format}`;
      case "export_start": {
        connectedTo(args.profileId);
        const r = args.request as ExportRequest;
        const id = `job-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`;
        const all = r.scope.kind === "selected"
          ? fx.messagesOf(r.entity, scenario).filter((m) => (r.scope as { sequences: number[] }).sequences.includes(m.sequence_number))
          : fx.messagesOf(r.entity, scenario).filter((m) => accepts(r.view, m) && matches(m, (r.scope as { filter: FilterSpec }).filter));
        const file = r.path.split("/").at(-1)!;
        const title = `Export ${r.entity} (${r.view}) to ${file}`;
        const total = all.length;
        emit("job", { type: "started", id, title, total });
        const finish = (error: ActivityError | null) => {
          clearTimeout(jobs.get(id)?.timer);
          jobs.delete(id);
          const summary = error ? null : `Exported ${count(total, "message", "messages").replace(/^(\d+)/, (n) => Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US"))} to ${file}`;
          if (error?.kind === "cancelled") activity("Export cancelled", started);
          else activity(summary ?? `${title} failed`, started, error ? new EngineError(error.kind, error.message) : null);
          emit("job", { type: "finished", id, error, summary, output: error ? null : r.path });
        };
        let done = 0;
        const tick = () => {
          done = Math.min(total, done + Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / 3)));
          emit("job", { type: "progress", id, done: scenario === "export-slow" ? Math.floor(done / 2) : done, total });
          if (scenario !== "export-slow" && done >= total) finish(null);
          else jobs.set(id, { timer: setTimeout(tick, 150), finish });
        };
        jobs.set(id, { timer: setTimeout(tick, 150), finish });
        return { id } satisfies JobStarted;
      }
      case "job_cancel": {
        const j = jobs.get(args.id as string);
        if (!j) return false;
        j.finish({ kind: "cancelled", message: "Export cancelled." });
        return true;
      }
      case "job_reveal":
        return null;
      case "profile_test_draft": {
        const d = args.draft as ProfileDraft;
        if (d.auth.kind === "connection-string" && !d.id && !d.auth.connection_string?.trim()) throw new EngineError("invalid", "paste the namespace's connection string");
        activity(`Tested ${d.name.trim()} (unsaved): management ok, messaging ok`, started);
        return {
          host: d.auth.kind === "entra" ? d.auth.namespace : hostFromConnectionString(d.auth.connection_string), emulator: false, transport: d.transport, auth: d.auth.kind === "entra" ? "Entra ID ops@contoso.example" : "SAS key reader",
          management: { status: "ok", detail: "listed 4 queues (Manage)" }, messaging: { status: "ok", detail: "peeked customs-docs (Listen)" },
        };
      }
      case "copy_entity_connection_string": {
        const p = profiles.find((o) => o.id === args.profileId);
        if (!p) throw new EngineError("not_found", "That profile no longer exists.");
        if (p.auth.kind === "entra") throw new EngineError("invalid", "Entra ID connections have no connection string. Copy the URL instead.");
        activity(`Copied the connection string for ${args.entity}`, started);
        return null;
      }
      case "sign_ins_list": {
        // As Rust `sign_ins`, over M1b's mock model: every record, ordered by id; whose it is; and the
        // profiles that connect with it now (`boundSignIn`, which may be the same person's tenant sign-in).
        const c = current();
        const names = fx.accounts as Record<string, { display_name: string | null } | undefined>;
        return [...records.values()]
          .sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
          .map((s) => ({
            id: s.id,
            user_principal: s.user_principal,
            display_name: names[s.id]?.display_name ?? null,
            tenant_id: s.tenant_id,
            current: account?.id === s.id,
            signed_in: live.has(s.id),
            same_account: c !== null && sameIdentity(c, s),
            profiles: profiles.filter((p) => boundSignIn(p)?.id === s.id).map((p) => p.name),
          })) satisfies StoredSignIn[];
      }
      case "sign_out": {
        const s = records.get(args.id as string);
        if (!s) throw new EngineError("not_found", "That sign-in is already gone.");
        records.delete(s.id);
        live.delete(s.id);
        if (account?.id === s.id) account = null;
        activity(`Signed out ${s.user_principal}`, started);
        return null;
      }
      case "open_sign_in_page":
        if (!lastPrompt) throw new EngineError("not_found", "No sign-in is waiting.");
        return null;
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/src/ui/grid/DataGrid.tsx`, `app/src/ui/grid/DataGrid.test.tsx`, `app/src/dev/Catalogue.tsx` (grid section uses sorting and row numbers)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M1b DataGrid.
- Produces (additive; existing callers keep working):
  - `export interface GridSort { id: string; desc: boolean }`
  - new props: `sorting?: GridSort | null`, `onSortingChange?(s: GridSort | null): void` (header click cycles ascending → descending → none; columns sort when they have an `accessorFn`/`accessorKey` and `enableSorting !== false`), `rowNumbers?: boolean` (a leading `#` column numbering rows in display order). No `columnVisibility` or `footer` props: nothing in M1c would use them (Task 14 shows "reading more" in its status line, not a grid footer)
  - keyboard: PageUp/PageDown move a screenful; Shift+J/K extend like Shift+arrows; the range anchor is a row id, so it survives re-sorting
  - a11y: `aria-busy` while loading; `aria-sort` on sorted headers; `aria-activedescendant` only when the focused row is rendered; loading and empty bodies sit in a `row` > `gridcell`

```tsx
const sortable: ColumnDef<Row, unknown>[] = [
  { id: "seq", header: "Seq", accessorFn: (r) => r.seq, meta: { mono: true, width: "64px" } },
  { id: "subject", header: "Subject", accessorFn: (r) => r.subject },
];

function Sorted(props: Partial<DataGridProps<Row>> & { initial?: GridSort | null }) {
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<GridSort | null>(props.initial ?? null);
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<{ ids: Set<string>; focus: string | null }>({ ids: new Set(), focus: null });
  return (
    <DataGrid<Row>
      label="Messages"
      columns={sortable}
      rows={rows.slice(0, 5)}
      getRowId={(r) => String(r.seq)}
      selectedIds={sel.ids}
      focusId={sel.focus}
      onSelectionChange={(ids, focus) => setSel({ ids, focus })}
      rowHeight={22}
      initialRect={{ width: 800, height: 600 }}
      sorting={sorting}
      onSortingChange={setSorting}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

const seqColumn = () => screen.getAllByRole("row").slice(1).map((r) => within(r).getAllByRole("gridcell")[0].textContent);

describe("DataGrid sorting and columns", () => {
  it("cycles a header through ascending, descending and none", async () => {
    render(<Sorted />);
    const header = screen.getByRole("columnheader", { name: /Seq/ });
    await userEvent.click(within(header).getByRole("button"));
    expect(header).toHaveAttribute("aria-sort", "ascending");
    await userEvent.click(within(header).getByRole("button"));
    expect(header).toHaveAttribute("aria-sort", "descending");
    expect(seqColumn()[0]).toBe(String(rows[4].seq));
    await userEvent.click(within(header).getByRole("button"));
    expect(header).not.toHaveAttribute("aria-sort");
  });

  it("numbers rows in display order", () => {
    render(<Sorted rowNumbers initial={{ id: "seq", desc: true }} />);
    const first = screen.getAllByRole("row")[1];
    expect(within(first).getAllByRole("gridcell")[0]).toHaveTextContent("1");
    expect(within(first).getAllByRole("gridcell")[1]).toHaveTextContent(String(rows[4].seq));
  });

  it("keeps a shift range anchored to the row after re-sorting", async () => {
    render(<Sorted />);
    const grid = screen.getByRole("grid");
    await userEvent.click(screen.getAllByRole("row")[1]); // the smallest seq, rows[0]
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole("columnheader", { name: /Seq/ })).getByRole("button"));
    await userEvent.click(within(screen.getByRole("columnheader", { name: /Seq/ })).getByRole("button")); // descending
    // rows[0] is now the last row, so extend upwards: Shift+J would clamp there and select one row.
    await userEvent.keyboard("{Shift>}K{/Shift}");
    const selected = [...grid.querySelectorAll('[aria-selected="true"]')].map((r) => within(r as HTMLElement).getAllByRole("gridcell")[0].textContent);
    expect(selected.sort()).toEqual([String(rows[0].seq), String(rows[1].seq)].sort());
  });

  it("moves a page with PageDown and marks itself busy while loading", async () => {
    const { rerender } = render(<Harness count={200} />);
    const grid = screen.getByRole("grid");
    grid.focus();
    await userEvent.keyboard("{ArrowDown}{PageDown}");
    expect(grid.getAttribute("aria-activedescendant")).toMatch(/-\d+$/);
    expect(Number(grid.getAttribute("aria-activedescendant")!.split("-").at(-1))).toBeGreaterThan(rows[10].seq - 1);
    rerender(<Harness count={0} loading />);
    expect(screen.getByRole("grid")).toHaveAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
  });
});
```

In `DataGrid.tsx`:

- Import `getSortedRowModel`, `type SortingState` and `ArrowDown, ArrowUp` from `lucide-react`.
- Table options: `getSortedRowModel: getSortedRowModel()`, `manualSorting: false`, `enableSortingRemoval: true`, `state: { sorting: p.sorting ? [p.sorting] : [] }`, `onSortingChange: (u) => { const next = typeof u === "function" ? u(p.sorting ? [p.sorting] : []) : u; p.onSortingChange?.(next[0] ? { id: next[0].id, desc: next[0].desc } : null); }`.
- Row numbers: when `p.rowNumbers`, the grid template starts with `44px` and each row renders a first `gridcell` with `v.index + 1` (`text-right tabular-nums text-muted font-mono text-11`); the header gets a `#` cell (`text-right`).
- Index map and id anchor:

```tsx
const indexOf = useMemo(() => new Map(rows.map((r, i) => [r.id, i])), [rows]);
const anchor = useRef<string | null>(null);
const focusIndex = p.focusId != null ? (indexOf.get(p.focusId) ?? -1) : -1;
```

`selectOnly(i)` stores `anchor.current = idAt(i)`; `selectRange(to)` resolves `const from = (anchor.current != null ? indexOf.get(anchor.current) : undefined) ?? (focusIndex >= 0 ? focusIndex : to);` and keeps `anchor.current` unchanged.
- Keys: `const key = e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toLowerCase() : e.key;` then `j`/`k` as before (Shift with `J`/`K` now extends); `PageDown`/`PageUp`: `const page = Math.max(1, Math.floor((scrollRef.current?.clientHeight ?? p.rowHeight * 10) / p.rowHeight) - 1); to = step(±page)`.
- Header cell:

```tsx
{header.headers.map((h) => {
  const meta = h.column.columnDef.meta;

    >

          onClick={h.column.getToggleSortingHandler()}
          className={cn("inline-flex max-w-full items-center gap-1 rounded-ctl text-muted hover:text-fg focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-1 focus-visible:outline-fg", meta?.align === "end" && "flex-row-reverse")}
        >
          <span className="truncate">{label}</span>
          {dir === "asc" && <ArrowUp aria-hidden="true" className="size-3 shrink-0 text-icon" />}
          {dir === "desc" && <ArrowDown aria-hidden="true" className="size-3 shrink-0 text-icon" />}

      ) : (
        label
      )}
    </div>
  );
})}
```

- `aria-activedescendant` only when the focused row is among `virtualizer.getVirtualItems()`; `aria-busy={p.loading || undefined}` on the grid.
- Loading, empty and error bodies are wrapped: `<div role="row"><div role="gridcell" aria-colspan={leafColumns.length}>{body}</div></div>` (the skeleton rows keep their look inside it).

In `Catalogue.tsx` the grid section passes `rowNumbers`, a `sorting` state and accessor-based columns, so the finish review sees the header affordances.

```ts
import { base64ToBytes, detectFormat, hexDump, prettyJson, prettyXml, utf8OrNull } from "./format";

describe("prettyJson", () => {
  it("indents nested values and keeps literals exactly", () => {
    expect(prettyJson('{"a":[1,{"b":12345678901234567890}],"s":"x\\"y, z"}')).toBe(
      '{\n  "a": [\n    1,\n    {\n      "b": 12345678901234567890\n    }\n  ],\n  "s": "x\\"y, z"\n}',
    );
  });
  it("keeps empty containers on one line", () => {
    expect(prettyJson('{"a":{},"b":[]}')).toBe('{\n  "a": {},\n  "b": []\n}');
  });
  it("returns null for invalid JSON", () => {
    expect(prettyJson("{oops")).toBeNull();
    expect(prettyJson("")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("prettyXml", () => {
  it("indents elements and keeps leaf text inline", () => {
    expect(prettyXml('<a x="1"><b>t</b><c/></a>')).toBe('<a x="1">\n  <b>t</b>\n  <c/>\n</a>');
  });
  it("keeps CDATA and comments and the declaration", () => {
    const out = prettyXml('<?xml version="1.0"?><a><!-- note --><b><![CDATA[x < y & z]]></b></a>')!;
    expect(out).toContain("<![CDATA[x < y & z]]>");
    expect(out).toContain("<!-- note -->");
    expect(out.startsWith('<?xml version="1.0"?>\n<a>')).toBe(true);
  });
  it("returns null when tags do not nest", () => {
    expect(prettyXml("<a><b></a></b>")).toBeNull();
    expect(prettyXml("plain text")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("detectFormat", () => {
  it("trusts content, not only the content type", () => {
    expect(detectFormat('{"a":1}')).toBe("json");
    expect(detectFormat("<a/>", "text/plain")).toBe("xml");
    expect(detectFormat("not json", "application/json")).toBe("text");
    expect(detectFormat("hello")).toBe("text");
  });
});

describe("bytes", () => {
  it("decodes base64 and UTF-8, refusing invalid UTF-8", () => {
    const utf8 = new TextEncoder().encode("héllo");
    expect(utf8OrNull(utf8)).toBe("héllo");
    expect(Array.from(base64ToBytes(btoa("Hi")))).toEqual([0x48, 0x69]);
    expect(utf8OrNull(new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xfe]))).toBeNull();

    expect(hexDump(new Uint8Array(40))).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(hexDump(new Uint8Array(40), 16, 32)[0].startsWith("00000010")).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`app/src/ui/viewer/format.ts`:

```ts
export type BodyFormat = "json" | "xml" | "text";
export const HEX_ROW = 16;
/** Display limit per line; longer lines are cut (copy and export keep everything). */
export const MAX_LINE = 20_000;

/** Re-indent JSON by walking its tokens, so numbers and string escapes stay byte-exact. */
export function prettyJson(text: string): string | null {
  try {
    JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  const out: string[] = [];
  let depth = 0;
  let inString = false;
  let escaped = false;
  const newline = () => out.push("\n" + "  ".repeat(depth));
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text[i];
    if (inString) {
      out.push(c);
      if (escaped) escaped = false;
      else if (c === "\\") escaped = true;
      else if (c === '"') inString = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (c === " " || c === "\n" || c === "\r" || c === "\t") continue;
    if (c === '"') {
      inString = true;
      out.push(c);
    } else if (c === "{" || c === "[") {
      const close = c === "{" ? "}" : "]";
      let j = i + 1;
      while (j < text.length && " \n\r\t".includes(text[j])) j++;
      if (text[j] === close) {
        out.push(c + close);
        i = j;
      } else {
        out.push(c);
        depth++;
        newline();
      }
    } else if (c === "}" || c === "]") {
      depth--;
      newline();
      out.push(c);
    } else if (c === ",") {
      out.push(",");
      newline();
    } else if (c === ":") {
      out.push(": ");
    } else {
      out.push(c);
    }
  }
  return out.join("");
}

const TOKEN = /<!--[\s\S]*?-->|<!\[CDATA\[[\s\S]*?\]\]>|<\?[\s\S]*?\?>|<![^>]*>|<\/[^>]+>|<[^>]+\/>|<[^>]+>|[^<]+/g;

/** Indent XML by its tags; `null` when there are no tags or they do not nest. */
export function prettyXml(text: string): string | null {
  const tokens = text.match(TOKEN) ?? [];
  if (!tokens.some((t) => t.startsWith("<") && !t.startsWith("<?"))) return null;
  const lines: string[] = [];
  const stack: string[] = [];
  const pad = () => "  ".repeat(stack.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
    const t = tokens[i];
    if (!t.startsWith("<")) {
      const s = t.trim();
      if (s) lines.push(pad() + s);
      continue;
    }
    if (t.startsWith("</")) {
      const name = t.slice(2, -1).trim();
      if (stack.pop() !== name) return null;
      lines.push(pad() + t);
      continue;
    }
    if (t.startsWith("<!--") || t.startsWith("<![CDATA[") || t.startsWith("<?") || t.startsWith("<!") || t.endsWith("/>")) {
      lines.push(pad() + t);
      continue;
    }
    const name = t.slice(1).split(/[\s>/]/)[0];
    const text = tokens[i + 1];
    const close = tokens[i + 2];
    // <b>leaf</b> stays on one line.
    if (text && !text.startsWith("<") && close === `</${name}>`) {
      lines.push(pad() + t + text.trim() + close);
      i += 2;
      continue;
    }
    lines.push(pad() + t);
    stack.push(name);
  }
  return stack.length === 0 ? lines.join("\n") : null;
}

export function detectFormat(text: string, contentType?: string | null): BodyFormat {
  const head = text.trimStart()[0];
  const ct = (contentType ?? "").toLowerCase();
  if ((head === "{" || head === "[" || ct.includes("json")) && prettyJson(text) !== null) return "json";
  if ((head === "<" || ct.includes("xml")) && prettyXml(text) !== null) return "xml";
  return "text";
}

export function base64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
  const raw = atob(b64);
  const out = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) out[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
  return out;
}

export function utf8OrNull(bytes: Uint8Array): string | null {
  try {
    return new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** `00000010  7b 22 69 …  |{"i…|` rows of 16 bytes, for bytes [start, end). */
export function hexDump(bytes: Uint8Array, start = 0, end = bytes.length): string[] {
  const rows: string[] = [];
  for (let at = start; at < Math.min(end, bytes.length); at += HEX_ROW) {
    const chunk = bytes.subarray(at, Math.min(at + HEX_ROW, end, bytes.length));
    const hex = Array.from(chunk, (b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    const left = hex.slice(0, 8).join(" ");
    const right = hex.slice(8).join(" ");
    const hexCol = (right ? `${left}  ${right}` : left).padEnd(HEX_ROW * 3, " ");
    const ascii = Array.from(chunk, (b) => (b >= 0x20 && b <= 0x7e ? String.fromCharCode(b) : ".")).join("");
    rows.push(`${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hexCol}  |${ascii}|`);
  }
  return rows;
}
```

(Check the `hexDump` padding against the test's exact expected line and adjust the pad width, not the test, if the column alignment needs one more space.)

`app/src/ui/viewer/CodeView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useVirtualizer } from "@tanstack/react-virtual";
import { useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { observeRectWithFloor } from "../lib/virtual";
import { MAX_LINE } from "./format";

const LINE = 16;

/** Read-only text, one virtualised row per line, with a line-number gutter. No syntax colours. */
export function CodeView({ text, label, height }: { text: string; label: string; height?: number }) {
  const lines = useMemo(() => text.split("\n"), [text]);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const rect = { width: 600, height: height ?? 400 };
  const v = useVirtualizer({ count: lines.length, getScrollElement: () => ref.current, estimateSize: () => LINE, overscan: 20, initialRect: rect, observeElementRect: observeRectWithFloor(rect) });
  const gutter = `${String(lines.length).length + 1}ch`;
  return (
    <div ref={ref} role="region" aria-label={label} tabIndex={0} className="h-full overflow-auto font-mono text-11 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-1 focus-visible:-outline-offset-1 focus-visible:outline-fg" data-selectable>
      <div className="relative w-max min-w-full" style={{ height: v.getTotalSize() }}>
        {v.getVirtualItems().map((row) => {
          const line = lines[row.index];
          const cut = line.length > MAX_LINE;
          return (
            <div key={row.index} data-line className="absolute left-0 flex whitespace-pre" style={{ height: LINE, transform: `translateY(${row.start}px)`, lineHeight: `${LINE}px` }}>
              <span aria-hidden="true" className="sticky left-0 shrink-0 select-none bg-side pr-3 text-right text-faint tabular-nums" style={{ width: gutter }}>{row.index + 1}</span>
              <span className="pr-4 text-fg">{cut ? line.slice(0, MAX_LINE) : line}</span>
              {cut && <span className="pr-4 text-muted">… line shortened; copy the body for all of it</span>}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`app/src/ui/viewer/HexView.tsx`: the same virtualised shell over `Math.ceil(bytes.length / HEX_ROW)` rows; each row renders three spans from `hexDump(bytes, i * 16, i * 16 + 16)[0]` split as offset (`text-muted`), hex (`text-fg`), ascii (`text-muted`): `const [, offset, hex, ascii] = /^(\S+)\s{2}(.*?)\s{2}(\|.*\|)$/.exec(line)!` (render `hex.trimEnd()` so the test's `48 69` matches).

`app/src/ui/components/toggle-group.tsx`:

```tsx
import { cn } from "../lib/cn";

export function ToggleGroup<T extends string>({ label, values, options, onChange }: {
  label: string;
  values: T[];
  options: { value: T; label: string }[];
  onChange(values: T[]): void;
}) {
  return (
    <div role="group" aria-label={label} className="inline-flex h-6 items-stretch rounded-ctl border border-line bg-field p-px">
      {options.map((o) => {
        const on = values.includes(o.value);
        return (
          <button
            key={o.value}
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={on}
            onClick={() => onChange(on ? values.filter((v) => v !== o.value) : [...values, o.value])}
            className={cn("rounded-[2px] px-2 text-12 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-1 focus-visible:outline-fg", on ? "bg-selection text-fg" : "text-muted hover:bg-hover hover:text-fg")}
          >
            {o.label}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`index.ts`: export `./viewer/format`, `./viewer/CodeView`, `./viewer/HexView`, `./components/toggle-group`. Catalogue gets a "Viewer" section: a JSON body (CodeView of `prettyJson`), an XML body, a HexView of 64 bytes, and the ToggleGroup.
