```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { renderShell } from "../test-utils";

const seedTab = (id: string, kind: "queue" | "subscription", subTab: string) => ({
  last_profile: "p-logistics",
  "shell:p-logistics": { tabs: [{ id, kind, subTab }], activeTab: id, expanded: [], selected: null, layout: { treeSize: 18, drawerOpen: false, drawerSize: 18, drawerTab: "activity" } },
});

const grid = () => screen.findByRole("grid", { name: /dead-letter|messages/i });

describe("message views", () => {
  it("lists dead letters with reasons, counts and the read-only note", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seedTab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter"));
    const g = await grid();
    expect(await within(g).findAllByText("MaxDeliveryCountExceeded")).not.toHaveLength(0);
    const status = screen.getByRole("status", { name: "Peek status" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("37 messages"));
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("End of the dead-letter queue");
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("Peek only, nothing locked");
  });

  it("peek more reads the next Top N", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seedTab("shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync", "subscription", "dead-letter"));
    const status = await screen.findByRole("status", { name: "Peek status" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("100 messages"));
    await userEvent.click(within(status).getByRole("button", { name: "Peek more" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("200 messages"));
  });

  it("from the end shows the newest messages and no Peek more", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seedTab("shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync", "subscription", "dead-letter"));
    await grid();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: "End" }));
    const status = screen.getByRole("status", { name: "Peek status" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("Last 100 messages"));
    expect(within(status).queryByRole("button", { name: "Peek more" })).toBeNull();
    // The last 100 dead letters are 48114..48213, oldest first.
    expect(within(await grid()).getAllByRole("row")[1]).toHaveTextContent("48114");
  });

  it("sorts by sequence number descending", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seedTab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter"));
    const g = await grid();
    await within(g).findAllByText("MaxDeliveryCountExceeded");
    const seqHeader = within(g).getByRole("columnheader", { name: /Seq/ });
    await userEvent.click(within(seqHeader).getByRole("button"));
    await userEvent.click(within(seqHeader).getByRole("button"));
    const first = within(g).getAllByRole("row")[1];
    expect(first).toHaveTextContent("41036");
  });

  it("hides a column from the Columns menu and remembers it", async () => {
    const { mock } = renderShell("default", seedTab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter"));
    const g = await grid();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Columns" }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("menuitemcheckbox", { name: "Description" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(g).queryByRole("columnheader", { name: "Description" })).toBeNull());
    await waitFor(() => expect(mock.calls.some((c) => c.cmd === "settings_set" && (c.args as { key: string }).key === "columns")).toBe(true));
  });

  it("shows a peek error with the fix", async () => {
    renderShell("peek-error", seedTab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter"));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "Can't read the dead-letter queue" })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Try again" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("an empty view says so in plain words", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seedTab("label-print", "queue", "dead-letter"));
    expect(await screen.findByRole("heading", { name: "No dead letters" })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("peek again after Peek more reads Top N again, not the grown total", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seedTab("shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync", "subscription", "dead-letter"));
    const status = await screen.findByRole("status", { name: "Peek status" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("100 messages"));
    await userEvent.click(within(status).getByRole("button", { name: "Peek more" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("200 messages"));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Peek again" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(status).toHaveTextContent("100 messages"));
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
    expect(status).toHaveTextContent("100 messages");
  });

  it("starts each test with fresh view state", async () => {
    // Sorting set by an earlier test must not carry over (the view store is module state).
    renderShell("default", seedTab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter"));
    const g = await grid();
    await within(g).findAllByText("MaxDeliveryCountExceeded");
    expect(within(g).getByRole("columnheader", { name: /Seq/ })).not.toHaveAttribute("aria-sort");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the helpers**

`app/src/ui/lib/format.ts`:

```ts
/** `812 B`, `1.2 KiB`, `4.0 MiB`, `1.1 GiB`. */
export function formatBytes(n: number): string {
  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`;
  const units = ["KiB", "MiB", "GiB", "TiB"];
  let v = n / 1024;
  let u = 0;
  while (v >= 1024 && u < units.length - 1) {
    v /= 1024;
    u++;
  }
  return `${v.toFixed(1)} ${units[u]}`;
}
```

(+ a test in `format.test.ts`: `formatBytes(812) === "812 B"`, `formatBytes(1229) === "1.2 KiB"`, `formatBytes(4 * 1024 * 1024) === "4.0 MiB"`.)

`app/src/shell/time.ts`:

```ts
const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
/** `2026-10-15 09:41:12` in local time; empty for null or unparsable input. */
export function formatTimestamp(iso: string | null): string {
  if (!iso) return "";
  const d = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
}
```

```ts
export const VIEW_OF: Partial<Record<SubTabId, PeekView>> = {
  messages: "active", "dead-letter": "dead-letter", "transfer-dlq": "transfer-dead-letter", scheduled: "scheduled", deferred: "deferred",
};
export const viewOf = (sub: SubTabId): PeekView | null => VIEW_OF[sub] ?? null;
export const VIEW_LABEL: Record<PeekView, string> = {
```

`messages/peekError.ts`:

```ts
export function describePeekError(kind: string, message: string, viewLabel: string): { title: string; body: string; fix: "retry" | "sessions" | "edit" } {
  if (/session/i.test(message) && /(requires|require|enabled|sessionful)/i.test(message))
    return { title: "This entity requires sessions", body: "Open Sessions and peek one session's messages.", fix: "sessions" };
  switch (kind) {
    case "forbidden": {
      const right = /needs ([A-Za-z,]+)/.exec(message)?.[1] ?? "Listen";
      return { title: `Can't read the ${viewLabel}`, body: `This identity needs ${right} on this entity to peek its messages.`, fix: "retry" };
    }
    case "not_found":
      return { title: "This entity wasn't found", body: "It may have been deleted or renamed. Refresh the tree to see what is there now.", fix: "retry" };
    case "not_signed_in":
      return { title: "Sign-in expired", body: "Sign in again from the connection, then try again.", fix: "edit" };
    case "auth":
      return { title: "Credentials rejected", body: "Replace the connection string or sign in again, then try again.", fix: "edit" };
    case "network":
    case "timeout":
      return { title: "Couldn't reach the namespace", body: "Check your network connection, then try again.", fix: "retry" };
    case "throttled":
      return { title: "The namespace is busy", body: "Service Bus is throttling requests. Wait a moment, then try again.", fix: "retry" };
    default:
      return { title: "Couldn't peek messages", body: "Try again. If it keeps failing, the detail below says why.", fix: "retry" };
  }
}
```

`messages/viewStore.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import type { FilterSpec, PeekFrom } from "@/engine";
import type { GridSort } from "@/ui";

export interface ViewState { from: PeekFrom; topN: number; filter: FilterSpec; filterOpen: boolean; sorting: GridSort | null; detailOpen: boolean }

/** View state lives for the session (not persisted): switching sub-tabs or tabs keeps it. */
const states = new Map<string, ViewState>();
const subs = new Set<() => void>();
const notify = () => subs.forEach((f) => f());

export const viewKey = (profileId: string | null, tabId: string, view: string) => `${profileId ?? ""}|${tabId}|${view}`;

export function setViewState(key: string, defaults: ViewState, patch: Partial<ViewState>) {
  states.set(key, { ...(states.get(key) ?? defaults), ...patch });
  notify();
}

export function clearViewStates(profileId: string) {
  for (const k of [...states.keys()]) if (k.startsWith(`${profileId}|`)) states.delete(k);
  notify();
}

/** Forget every view state. Tests call it between renders: this is module state, so a filter left
 *  open or a sort set by one test would otherwise reach the next. */
export function resetViewStates() {
  states.clear();
  notify();
}

export function useViewState(key: string, defaults: ViewState): [ViewState, (patch: Partial<ViewState>) => void] {
  const state = useSyncExternalStore(
    (cb) => (subs.add(cb), () => subs.delete(cb)),
    () => states.get(key) ?? defaults,
  );
  const update = useCallback((patch: Partial<ViewState>) => setViewState(key, defaults, patch), [key, defaults]);
  return [state, update];
}
```

(`defaults` must be stable: build it with `useMemo` in the caller.)

`app/src/shell/test-utils.tsx`: `renderShell` calls `resetViewStates()` beside `activityStore.clear()`. `app/src/test/setup.ts`: the `afterEach` also resets it, like the updater:

```ts
  if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
    (await import("@/engine/updater")).resetUpdater();
    (await import("@/shell/messages/viewStore")).resetViewStates();
  }
```

`messages/columns.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { commands, keys, useSetting, type MessageSummary, type PeekView, type PropValue } from "@/engine";
import { formatBytes } from "@/ui";
import { formatTimestamp } from "../time";

export type ColumnId =
  | "seq" | "enqueued" | "scheduled" | "subject" | "messageId" | "correlationId" | "session" | "contentType" | "to" | "replyTo"
  | "delivery" | "size" | "ttl" | "reason" | "description" | "source" | "state" | "preview" | `prop:${string}`;

export const COLUMN_LABEL: Record<Exclude<ColumnId, `prop:${string}`>, string> = {
  seq: "Seq", enqueued: "Enqueued", scheduled: "Scheduled for", subject: "Subject", messageId: "Message ID", correlationId: "Correlation ID",
  session: "Session", contentType: "Content type", to: "To", replyTo: "Reply to", delivery: "Deliveries", size: "Size", ttl: "Time to live",
  reason: "Reason", description: "Description", source: "Dead-letter source", state: "State", preview: "Body",
};
export const OPTIONAL: ColumnId[] = ["correlationId", "contentType", "to", "replyTo", "ttl", "source", "state", "session", "preview", "enqueued", "scheduled", "delivery", "size", "reason", "description", "subject", "messageId"];

type Group = "active" | "dead-letter" | "scheduled" | "deferred";
const groupOf = (v: PeekView): Group => (v === "transfer-dead-letter" ? "dead-letter" : v);

export function defaultColumns(view: PeekView, sessions: boolean): ColumnId[] {
  switch (groupOf(view)) {
    case "dead-letter": return ["seq", "enqueued", "subject", "messageId", "reason", "description", "delivery", "size"];
    case "scheduled": return ["seq", "scheduled", "subject", "messageId", "size", "preview"];
    case "deferred": return ["seq", "enqueued", "subject", "messageId", "delivery", "size", "preview"];
    default: return ["seq", "enqueued", "subject", "messageId", ...(sessions ? (["session"] as ColumnId[]) : []), "delivery", "size", "preview"];
  }
}

export const propColumn = (name: string): ColumnId => `prop:${name}`;
const isId = (v: unknown): v is ColumnId => typeof v === "string" && (v in COLUMN_LABEL || (v.startsWith("prop:") && v.length > 5));

/** A property value as text: `""` when absent, `nullText` for an explicit null. The detail pane
 *  (Task 16) uses this one helper too, with `nullText = "null"`. */
export function propText(v: PropValue | undefined, nullText = ""): string {
  if (!v) return "";
  return v.type === "null" ? nullText : String(v.value);
}

const text = (id: ColumnId, header: string, get: (m: MessageSummary) => string | number | null, meta: ColumnDef<MessageSummary, unknown>["meta"] = {}): ColumnDef<MessageSummary, unknown> => ({
  id, header, accessorFn: (m) => get(m) ?? "", meta: { width: "minmax(120px, 1fr)", ...meta },
  cell: (c) => { const v = c.getValue(); return v === "" ? null : <span title={String(v)}>{String(v)}</span>; },
});

export function buildColumns(ids: ColumnId[]): ColumnDef<MessageSummary, unknown>[] {
  return ids.map((id) => {
    if (id.startsWith("prop:")) {
      const name = id.slice(5);
      return text(id, name, (m) => propText(m.application_properties[name]), { mono: true, width: "minmax(100px, 160px)" });
    }
    switch (id) {
      case "seq": return text(id, "Seq", (m) => m.sequence_number, { mono: true, align: "end", width: "72px" });
      case "enqueued": return text(id, "Enqueued", (m) => formatTimestamp(m.enqueued_time), { mono: true, width: "136px" });
      case "scheduled": return text(id, "Scheduled for", (m) => formatTimestamp(m.scheduled_enqueue_time), { mono: true, width: "136px" });
      case "subject": return text(id, "Subject", (m) => m.subject, { width: "minmax(120px, 180px)" });
      case "messageId": return text(id, "Message ID", (m) => m.message_id, { mono: true, width: "minmax(120px, 200px)" });
      case "correlationId": return text(id, "Correlation ID", (m) => m.correlation_id, { mono: true, width: "minmax(120px, 200px)" });
      case "session": return text(id, "Session", (m) => m.session_id, { mono: true, width: "minmax(90px, 140px)" });
      case "contentType": return text(id, "Content type", (m) => m.content_type, { width: "minmax(100px, 150px)" });
      case "to": return text(id, "To", (m) => m.to, { width: "minmax(80px, 140px)" });
      case "replyTo": return text(id, "Reply to", (m) => m.reply_to, { width: "minmax(80px, 140px)" });
      case "delivery": return text(id, "Deliveries", (m) => m.delivery_count, { align: "end", width: "72px" });
      case "size": return { ...text(id, "Size", (m) => m.body_size, { align: "end", width: "76px" }), cell: (c) => formatBytes(c.getValue() as number) };
      case "ttl": return text(id, "Time to live", (m) => m.time_to_live_ms, { align: "end", width: "96px" });
      case "reason": return text(id, "Reason", (m) => m.dead_letter_reason, { width: "minmax(160px, 220px)" });
      case "description": return text(id, "Description", (m) => m.dead_letter_description, { width: "minmax(180px, 1fr)" });
      case "source": return text(id, "Dead-letter source", (m) => m.dead_letter_source, { width: "minmax(120px, 180px)" });
      case "state": return text(id, "State", (m) => m.state, { width: "80px" });
      default: return text(id, "Body", (m) => m.body_preview ?? (m.body_kind === "empty" ? "" : `${m.body_kind} body`), { mono: true, width: "minmax(200px, 2fr)" });
    }
  });
}

/** Columns per view group, saved in the `columns` setting; unknown or bad values fall back to the defaults. */
export function useColumnPrefs(view: PeekView, sessions: boolean): [ColumnId[], (ids: ColumnId[]) => void] {
  const q = useSetting("columns");
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const group = groupOf(view);
  const saved = (q.data as Record<string, unknown> | null)?.[group];
  const ids = Array.isArray(saved) && saved.every(isId) && saved.length > 0 ? (saved as ColumnId[]) : defaultColumns(view, sessions);
  const set = (next: ColumnId[]) => {
    const all = { ...((qc.getQueryData(keys.setting("columns")) as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? {}), [group]: next };
    qc.setQueryData(keys.setting("columns"), all);
    void commands.settingsSet("columns", all).catch(() => {});
  };
  return [ids, set];
}
```

`state.ts`: add `export type EntityTab = OpenTab & { kind: EntityKind };` (identical to `OpenTab` for now; Task 20 widens `OpenTab.kind` and `EntityTab` keeps every entity view free of the dashboard case). `EntityView({ tab }: { tab: EntityTab })` and `useEntityInfo(tab: EntityTab)` take it now. `Prefs` gains `peekSize: 50 | 100 | 500 | 1000` with default 100 (`parsePrefs` uses `oneOf(o.peekSize, [50, 100, 500, 1000] as const, 100)`), and `SettingsSheet` gets a "Peek size" row (`Segmented` 50 / 100 / 500 / 1,000; hint "Messages each view reads at first. Peek more reads the same number again."). `context.tsx` `refresh` also runs `qc.resetQueries({ queryKey: ["peek"] })` and invalidates `["entity-details"]`, `["rules"]`, `["sessions"]`, `["dashboard"]`, `["message"]`.

`engine/peek.ts` (Task 11's hook): Peek again and F5 reset the query, but the round (`n`, from Peek more) lives in the hook, so a reset after two Peek mores would reload 3 × Top N. Right after the `useInfiniteQuery` call, start a new round when the query has no pages:

```ts
  // A reset query (Peek again, F5) has no pages: start a new round at Top N, not at n × Top N.
  if (q.data === undefined && current.n > 1) {
    target.current = o.topN;
    setRound({ key: keyText, n: 1, rows: 0, scanned: 0, stopped: false });
  }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the toolbar and the view**

`messages/Toolbar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Columns3, Filter, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import type { PeekFrom } from "@/engine";
import {
  Button, DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuCheckboxItem, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuLabel, DropdownMenuSeparator, DropdownMenuTrigger,
  Hint, IconButton, Input, Segmented, Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue, formatNumber,
} from "@/ui";
import { COLUMN_LABEL, OPTIONAL, propColumn, type ColumnId } from "./columns";

export const TOP_N = [50, 100, 500, 1000, 5000, 10000, 50000] as const;

export function Toolbar(p: {
  from: PeekFrom;
  onFrom(f: PeekFrom): void;
  topN: number;
  onTopN(n: number): void;
  filterCount: number;
  filterOpen: boolean;
  onToggleFilter(): void;
  columns: ColumnId[];
  onColumns(ids: ColumnId[]): void;
  propertyNames: string[];
  onRefresh(): void;
  exportMenu?: ReactNode;
}) {
  const [seq, setSeq] = useState(p.from.kind === "sequence" ? String(p.from.sequence) : "");
  // Choosing Sequence shows the field; the peek starts there once a number is entered.
  const [picking, setPicking] = useState(false);
  const mode = picking ? "sequence" : p.from.kind;
  const applySeq = () => {
    const n = Number(seq.trim());
    if (seq.trim() !== "" && Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 0) {
      setPicking(false);
      p.onFrom({ kind: "sequence", sequence: n });
    }
  };
  const toggle = (id: ColumnId) => p.onColumns(p.columns.includes(id) ? p.columns.filter((c) => c !== id) : [...p.columns, id]);
  return (
    <div role="toolbar" aria-label="Peek options" className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line px-3">
      <span className="text-12 text-muted">From</span>
      <Segmented
        label="Peek from"
        value={mode}
        onChange={(v) => {
          if (v === "sequence") return seq ? applySeq() : setPicking(true);
          setPicking(false);
          p.onFrom({ kind: v as "start" | "end" });
        }}
        options={[{ value: "start", label: "Start" }, { value: "sequence", label: "Sequence" }, { value: "end", label: "End" }]}
      />
      {mode === "sequence" ? (
        <Input
          mono
          aria-label="From sequence number"
          inputMode="numeric"
          placeholder="Sequence number"
          className="w-[140px]"
          value={seq}
          onChange={(e) => setSeq(e.target.value.replace(/[^\d]/g, ""))}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && applySeq()}
          autoFocus={picking}
          onBlur={() => seq && applySeq()}
        />
      ) : null}
      <span className="ml-2 text-12 text-muted">Top</span>
      <Select value={String(p.topN)} onValueChange={(v) => p.onTopN(Number(v))}>
        <SelectTrigger aria-label="Messages to read" className="w-[88px]"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
        <SelectContent>
          {TOP_N.filter((n) => mode !== "end" || n <= 5000).map((n) => <SelectItem key={n} value={String(n)}>{formatNumber(n)}</SelectItem>)}
        </SelectContent>
      </Select>
      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <Button variant="quiet" aria-pressed={p.filterOpen} onClick={p.onToggleFilter}>
          <Filter aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5 text-icon" />
          Filter{p.filterCount > 0 && <span className="tabular-nums text-muted">{p.filterCount}</span>}
        </Button>
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button variant="quiet"><Columns3 aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5 text-icon" />Columns</Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto">
            <DropdownMenuLabel>Columns</DropdownMenuLabel>
            {OPTIONAL.map((id) => (
              <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem key={id} checked={p.columns.includes(id)} onCheckedChange={() => toggle(id)} onSelect={(e) => e.preventDefault()}>
                {COLUMN_LABEL[id as keyof typeof COLUMN_LABEL]}
              </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
            ))}
            {p.propertyNames.length > 0 && <DropdownMenuSeparator />}
            {p.propertyNames.length > 0 && <DropdownMenuLabel>Custom properties</DropdownMenuLabel>}
            {p.propertyNames.slice(0, 30).map((name) => (
              <DropdownMenuCheckboxItem key={name} checked={p.columns.includes(propColumn(name))} onCheckedChange={() => toggle(propColumn(name))} onSelect={(e) => e.preventDefault()}>
                <span className="font-mono text-11">{name}</span>
              </DropdownMenuCheckboxItem>
            ))}
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
        {p.exportMenu}
        <Hint label="Peek again" shortcut="f5">
          <IconButton label="Peek again" onClick={p.onRefresh}><RefreshCw /></IconButton>
        </Hint>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`DropdownMenuCheckboxItem` does not exist yet in `ui/components/dropdown-menu.tsx`: add it in the same style as `DropdownMenuItem` (Radix `M.CheckboxItem` with an `M.ItemIndicator` holding a 12px `Check` icon in `text-icon`, rows at `--row`, highlighted `bg-selection`), and export it.

`messages/MessagesView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { usePeekStream, type EntityCounts, type FilterSpec, type MessageSummary, type PeekView } from "@/engine";
import { Button, DataGrid, EmptyState, formatNumber, rowHeight } from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import type { EntityTab } from "../state";
import { VIEW_LABEL } from "../tabs/subtabs";
import { buildColumns, useColumnPrefs } from "./columns";
import { describePeekError } from "./peekError";
import { Toolbar } from "./Toolbar";
import { useViewState, viewKey, type ViewState } from "./viewStore";

const EMPTY: Record<PeekView, string> = {
  active: "No active messages", "dead-letter": "No dead letters", "transfer-dead-letter": "No transfer dead letters", scheduled: "No scheduled messages", deferred: "No deferred messages",
};

export const filterCount = (f: FilterSpec) =>
  [f.property, f.body_contains || f.body_regex || f.body_json_path, f.session_id].filter((v) => typeof v === "string" && v.trim() !== "").length;

export function MessagesView({ tab, view, counts, requiresSession }: { tab: EntityTab; view: PeekView; counts: EntityCounts | null; requiresSession: boolean }) {
  const { conn, prefs, dispatch, openSheet } = useShell();
  const profileId = conn.profileId;
  const defaults = useMemo<ViewState>(() => ({ from: { kind: "start" }, topN: prefs.peekSize, filter: {}, filterOpen: false, sorting: null, detailOpen: false }), [prefs.peekSize]);
  const [vs, setVs] = useViewState(viewKey(profileId, tab.id, view), defaults);
  const stream = usePeekStream({ profileId, entity: tab.id, view, from: vs.from, topN: vs.topN, filter: vs.filter });
  const [columnIds, setColumnIds] = useColumnPrefs(view, requiresSession);
  const columns = useMemo(() => buildColumns(columnIds), [columnIds]);
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<{ ids: Set<string>; focus: string | null }>({ ids: new Set(), focus: null });
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const label = VIEW_LABEL[view];
  const propertyNames = useMemo(() => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const m of stream.messages.slice(0, 500)) for (const k of Object.keys(m.application_properties)) if (!k.startsWith("DeadLetter")) seen.add(k);
    return [...seen].sort();
  }, [stream.messages]);

  const restart = () => void qc.resetQueries({ queryKey: ["peek", profileId, tab.id, view] });
  const failure = stream.error ? describePeekError(stream.error.kind, stream.error.message, label) : null;
  const n = stream.messages.length;
  const tail = vs.from.kind === "end";

  const status = (() => {
    if (stream.loading) return "Reading…";
    if (tail) return `Last ${formatNumber(n)} ${n === 1 ? "message" : "messages"}`;
    const base = `${formatNumber(n)} ${n === 1 ? "message" : "messages"} · scanned ${formatNumber(stream.scanned)}`;
    if (stream.streaming) return `${base} · reading more…`;
    if (stream.budgetHit) return n === 0 ? `Scanned ${formatNumber(stream.scanned)} messages, none matched. Peek more to keep scanning.` : `${base}. Peek more to keep scanning.`;
    if (stream.exhausted) return `${base} · End of the ${label}`;
    if (stream.stopped) return `${base} · stopped`;
    return `${base} · more available`;
  })();

  const body = failure ? (
    <EmptyState
      tone="error"
      title={failure.title}
      body={failure.body}
      detail={stream.error!.message}
      action={
        failure.fix === "sessions" ? <Button onClick={() => dispatch({ type: "subTab", id: tab.id, subTab: "sessions" })}>Open Sessions</Button>
        : failure.fix === "edit" && profileId ? <Button onClick={() => openSheet({ kind: "profile", profileId })}>Edit connection</Button>
        : <Button onClick={restart}>Try again</Button>
      }
    />
  ) : null;

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <Toolbar
        from={vs.from}
        onFrom={(from) => setVs({ from })}
        topN={vs.topN}
        onTopN={(topN) => setVs({ topN })}
        filterCount={filterCount(vs.filter)}
        filterOpen={vs.filterOpen}
        onToggleFilter={() => setVs({ filterOpen: !vs.filterOpen })}
        columns={columnIds}
        onColumns={setColumnIds}
        propertyNames={propertyNames}
        onRefresh={restart}
      />
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <DataGrid<MessageSummary>
          label={`${label[0].toUpperCase()}${label.slice(1)}`}
          columns={columns}
          rows={stream.messages}
          getRowId={(m) => String(m.sequence_number)}
          selectedIds={sel.ids}
          focusId={sel.focus}
          onSelectionChange={(ids, focus) => setSel({ ids, focus })}
          onActivate={() => setVs({ detailOpen: true })}
          rowHeight={rowHeight[prefs.density]}
          rowNumbers
          sorting={vs.sorting}
          onSortingChange={(sorting) => setVs({ sorting })}
          loading={stream.loading}
          error={body}
          empty={
            filterCount(vs.filter) > 0 ? (
              <EmptyState title="No messages match this filter" body={`Scanned ${formatNumber(stream.scanned)} messages.`} action={<Button onClick={() => setVs({ filter: {} })}>Clear filter</Button>}
              />
            ) : (
              <EmptyState title={EMPTY[view]} body={counts && view === "dead-letter" && counts.dead_letter > 0 ? "The count includes messages that arrived after this peek. Peek again to read them." : undefined} />
            )
          }
          initialRect={{ width: 900, height: 600 }}
        />
      </div>
      <div role="status" aria-label="Peek status" className="flex h-[22px] shrink-0 items-center gap-3 border-t border-line px-3 text-11 text-muted">
        <span className="tabular-nums">{status}</span>
        {stream.streaming && <Button size="sm" className="h-[18px]" onClick={stream.stop}>Stop</Button>}
        {!stream.streaming && stream.canPeekMore && <Button size="sm" className="h-[18px]" onClick={stream.peekMore}>Peek more</Button>}
        <span className="ml-auto">Peek only, nothing locked</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`app/src/shell/messages/FilterBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useFilterCheck, type FilterField, type FilterSpec } from "@/engine";
import { Button, Field, IconButton, Input, Segmented } from "@/ui";

type BodyMode = "contains" | "regex" | "json";
const modeOf = (f: FilterSpec): BodyMode => (f.body_regex ? "regex" : f.body_json_path ? "json" : "contains");

function useDebounced<T>(value: T, ms: number): T {
  const [v, setV] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setV(value), ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [value, ms]);
  return v;
}

export function FilterBar({ applied, onApply, onClose }: { applied: FilterSpec; onApply(f: FilterSpec): void; onClose(): void }) {
  const [property, setProperty] = useState(applied.property ?? "");
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<BodyMode>(modeOf(applied));
  const [body, setBody] = useState(applied.body_contains ?? applied.body_regex ?? applied.body_json_path ?? "");
  const [equals, setEquals] = useState(applied.body_json_equals ?? "");
  useEffect(() => {
    setProperty(applied.property ?? "");
    setMode(modeOf(applied));
    setBody(applied.body_contains ?? applied.body_regex ?? applied.body_json_path ?? "");
    setEquals(applied.body_json_equals ?? "");
  }, [applied]);
  // Memoised over its primitive fields: a new object on every render would restart the debounce
  // timer each time and make it set state every 250 ms for as long as the bar is open.
  const session = applied.session_id ?? null;
  const draft = useMemo<FilterSpec>(
    () => ({
      property: property || null,
      body_contains: mode === "contains" && body ? body : null,
      body_regex: mode === "regex" && body ? body : null,
      body_json_path: mode === "json" && body ? body : null,
      body_json_equals: mode === "json" && equals ? equals : null,
      session_id: session,
    }),
    [property, mode, body, equals, session],
  );
  const checked = useFilterCheck(useDebounced(draft, 250));
  const issue = (f: FilterField) => checked.data?.find((i) => i.field === f)?.message;
  const blocked = (checked.data?.length ?? 0) > 0;
  const apply = () => !blocked && onApply(draft);
  const keys = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === "Enter") apply();
    if (e.key === "Escape") onClose();
  };
  return (
    <div role="search" aria-label="Message filter" className="flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-2 border-b border-line bg-side px-3 py-2.5" onKeyDown={keys}>
      <Field label="Properties" hint="sys.MessageId, sys.Subject, sys.SessionId, sys.DeliveryCount, sys.EnqueuedTime, sys.DeadLetterReason and custom properties by name. = <> < > <= >= LIKE IN IS NULL, AND OR NOT." error={issue("property")}>
        {(ids) => <Input {...ids} mono placeholder="sys.DeliveryCount > 3 AND region = 'eu'" value={property} onChange={(e) => setProperty(e.target.value)} />}
      </Field>
      <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
        <Segmented
          label="Body condition"
          value={mode}
          onChange={(m) => setMode(m)}
          options={[{ value: "contains", label: "Contains" }, { value: "regex", label: "Regex" }, { value: "json", label: "JSONPath" }]}
        />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <Field label={mode === "contains" ? "Body contains" : mode === "regex" ? "Body regex" : "JSONPath"} error={issue(mode === "regex" ? "body_regex" : "body_json_path")}>
            {(ids) => <Input {...ids} mono placeholder={mode === "json" ? "$.order.status" : mode === "regex" ? "^\\{\"type\":\"refund\"" : "text in the body"} value={body} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} />}
          </Field>
        </div>
        {mode === "json" && (
          <div className="w-[180px]">
            <Field label="Equals" error={issue("body_json_equals")}>
              {(ids) => <Input {...ids} mono placeholder="optional" value={equals} onChange={(e) => setEquals(e.target.value)} />}
            </Field>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {applied.session_id && (
          <span className="inline-flex h-6 items-center gap-1 rounded-ctl border border-line pl-2 text-12">
            Session <span className="font-mono text-11">{applied.session_id}</span>
            <IconButton label={`Remove session ${applied.session_id}`} className="size-5" onClick={() => onApply({ ...draft, session_id: null })}><X /></IconButton>
          </span>
        )}
        <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
          <Button onClick={() => onApply({})}>Clear</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" disabled={blocked} onClick={apply}>Apply</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

In `MessagesView.tsx`, under the toolbar: `{vs.filterOpen && <FilterBar applied={vs.filter} onApply={(filter) => setVs({ filter })} onClose={() => setVs({ filterOpen: false })} />}`. When a session is set but the bar is closed, the toolbar's Filter count still shows it.

`app/src/shell/messages/exportJson.ts`:

```ts
import type { MessageBody, MessageDetail } from "@/engine";
import { base64ToBytes, utf8OrNull } from "@/ui";

export function bodyBytes(b: MessageBody): Uint8Array | null {
  if (b.kind === "binary") return base64ToBytes(b.value);
  if (b.kind === "text") return new TextEncoder().encode(b.value);
  return null;
}

export function bodyText(b: MessageBody): string | null {
  switch (b.kind) {
    case "binary": return utf8OrNull(base64ToBytes(b.value));
    case "text": return b.value;
    case "value":
    case "sequence": return JSON.stringify(b.value);
    default: return "";
  }
}

/** One message as the export file writes it (`dispatched.message/v1`). `body_text` only for binary
 *  bodies that are UTF-8, decoded by `bodyText` (the one decode path). */
export function toExportJson(d: MessageDetail): string {
  const { body_size: _size, body_truncated: _truncated, ...message } = d;
  const text = d.body.kind === "binary" ? bodyText(d.body) : null;
  return JSON.stringify({ schema: "dispatched.message/v1", ...message, ...(text !== null ? { body_text: text } : {}) }, null, 2);
}
```

`app/src/shell/messages/DetailPane.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Braces, Copy, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useMessageDetail, type MessageDetail, type PeekView } from "@/engine";
import {
  CodeView, EmptyState, HexView, Hint, IconButton, Segmented, SkeletonRows, Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger,
  detectFormat, formatBytes, prettyJson, prettyXml,
} from "@/ui";
import { formatTimestamp } from "../time";
import { VIEW_LABEL } from "../tabs/subtabs";
import { propText } from "./columns";
import { bodyBytes, bodyText, toExportJson } from "./exportJson";

type Mode = "formatted" | "text" | "hex";

function Rows({ label, rows }: { label: string; rows: [string, string | number | null | undefined][] }) {
  const shown = rows.filter(([, v]) => v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== "");
  return (
    <table aria-label={label} className="w-full border-collapse text-12">
      <tbody>
        {shown.map(([k, v]) => (
          <tr key={k} className="align-top">
            <th scope="row" className="w-[132px] py-0.5 pr-3 text-left font-normal text-muted">{k}</th>
            <td className="break-all py-0.5 font-mono text-11 text-fg" data-selectable>{String(v)}</td>
          </tr>
        ))}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}

function Body({ d }: { d: MessageDetail }) {
  const text = useMemo(() => bodyText(d.body), [d]);
  const bytes = useMemo(() => bodyBytes(d.body), [d]);
  const format = text !== null ? detectFormat(text, d.content_type) : null;
  const pretty = useMemo(() => (text === null || format === "text" ? null : format === "json" ? prettyJson(text) : prettyXml(text)), [text, format]);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>(pretty ? "formatted" : text !== null ? "text" : "hex");
  const options = [
    ...(pretty ? [{ value: "formatted" as Mode, label: "Formatted" }] : []),
    ...(text !== null ? [{ value: "text" as Mode, label: "Text" }] : []),
    ...(bytes ? [{ value: "hex" as Mode, label: "Hex" }] : []),
  ];
  const kind = format === "json" ? "JSON" : format === "xml" ? "XML" : text !== null ? "Text" : "Binary";
  if (d.body.kind === "empty") return <p className="m-0 px-3 py-2 text-12 text-muted">This message has no body.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 px-3">
        {options.length > 1 && <Segmented label="Body view" value={mode} onChange={setMode} options={options} />}
        <span className="text-11 text-muted">{kind} · {formatBytes(d.body_size)}</span>
      </div>
      {d.body_truncated && (
        <p className="m-0 px-3 pb-1.5 text-11 text-muted">Showing the first {formatBytes(DETAIL_CAP)} of {formatBytes(d.body_size)}. Export the message to get all of it.</p>
      )}
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 border-t border-line">
        {mode === "hex" && bytes ? <HexView bytes={bytes} label="Hex" /> : <CodeView text={mode === "formatted" && pretty ? pretty : (text ?? "")} label="Body" />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const DETAIL_CAP = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

export function DetailPane(p: { profileId: string | null; entity: string; view: PeekView; sequence: number | null; onClose(): void; onShowColumn(name: string): void }) {
  const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(p.sequence);
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(p.sequence), 120);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [p.sequence]);
  const q = useMessageDetail(p.profileId, p.entity, p.view, debounced);
  const d = q.data;
  const label = VIEW_LABEL[p.view];
  const text = d ? bodyText(d.body) : null;
  const copy = (s: string) => void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(s).catch(() => {});
  const dlq = p.view === "dead-letter" || p.view === "transfer-dead-letter";
  return (
    <section
      aria-label={p.sequence != null ? `Message ${p.sequence}` : "Message"}
      className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col bg-side"
      onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Escape" && p.onClose()}
    >
      <div className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line px-3">
        <span className="font-mono text-11 tabular-nums">Seq {p.sequence}</span>
        <span className="text-11 text-muted">{label}</span>
        <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1">
          <Hint label={text === null && d?.body.kind === "binary" ? "Copy body as base64" : "Copy body"}>
            <IconButton
              label={text === null && d?.body.kind === "binary" ? "Copy body as base64" : "Copy body"}
              disabled={!d || d.body_truncated}
              onClick={() => d && copy(text ?? (d.body.kind === "binary" ? d.body.value : ""))}
            ><Copy /></IconButton>
          </Hint>
          <Hint label="Copy as JSON">
            <IconButton label="Copy as JSON" disabled={!d || d.body_truncated} onClick={() => d && copy(toExportJson(d))}><Braces /></IconButton>
          </Hint>
          <IconButton label="Close message" onClick={p.onClose}><X /></IconButton>
        </div>
      </div>
      {q.isPending && debounced != null ? (
        <SkeletonRows rows={8} />
      ) : q.isError ? (
        <EmptyState tone="error" title="Couldn't read this message" body="Try selecting it again." detail={q.error.message} />
      ) : !d ? (
        <EmptyState
          title={`This message is no longer in the ${label}`}
          body="It may have been received, expired or moved since it was peeked. Peek again to see what is there now."
        />
      ) : (
        <Tabs defaultValue="body" className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
          {dlq && (d.dead_letter_reason || d.dead_letter_description) && (
            <div className="shrink-0 border-b border-line px-3 py-2">
              <p className="m-0 text-12 font-semibold">{d.dead_letter_reason}</p>
              {d.dead_letter_description && <p className="m-0 text-12 text-muted" data-selectable>{d.dead_letter_description}</p>}
            </div>
          )}
          <TabsList variant="underline" aria-label="Message parts" className="border-b border-line">
            <TabsTrigger value="body">Body</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="properties">Properties</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>
          <TabsContent value="body" className="min-h-0 flex-1"><Body key={`${d.sequence_number}`} d={d} /></TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="properties" className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
            <h3 className="m-0 mb-1 text-12 font-semibold text-muted">System</h3>
            <Rows
              label="System properties"
              rows={[
                ["Message ID", d.message_id], ["Sequence number", d.sequence_number], ["State", d.state],
                ["Enqueued", formatTimestamp(d.enqueued_time)], ["Scheduled for", formatTimestamp(d.scheduled_enqueue_time)],
                ["Delivery count", d.delivery_count], ["Session ID", d.session_id], ["Correlation ID", d.correlation_id],
                ["Subject", d.subject], ["Content type", d.content_type], ["To", d.to], ["Reply to", d.reply_to],
                ["Time to live", d.time_to_live_ms != null ? `${d.time_to_live_ms} ms` : null], ["Locked until", formatTimestamp(d.locked_until)],
                ["Dead-letter reason", d.dead_letter_reason], ["Dead-letter description", d.dead_letter_description], ["Dead-letter source", d.dead_letter_source],
                ["Body size", formatBytes(d.body_size)],
              ]}
            />
            <h3 className="m-0 mb-1 mt-3 text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Custom</h3>
            {Object.keys(d.application_properties).length === 0 ? (
              <p className="m-0 text-12 text-muted">No custom properties.</p>
            ) : (
              <table aria-label="Custom properties" className="w-full border-collapse text-12">
                <tbody>
                  {Object.entries(d.application_properties).map(([k, v]) => (
                    <tr key={k} className="group/prop align-top">
                      <th scope="row" className="w-[132px] py-0.5 pr-3 text-left font-mono text-11 font-normal text-fg">{k}</th>
                      <td className="w-[64px] py-0.5 text-11 text-muted">{v.type}</td>
                      <td className="break-all py-0.5 font-mono text-11" data-selectable>{propText(v, "null")}</td>
                      <td className="w-6 py-0.5 text-right">
                        <IconButton label={`Show ${k} as a column`} className="size-5 opacity-0 group-hover/prop:opacity-100 focus-visible:opacity-100" onClick={() => p.onShowColumn(k)}>
                          <Columns3Icon />
                        </IconButton>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            )}
          </TabsContent>
        </Tabs>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

(Import `Columns3` from `lucide-react` as `Columns3Icon`.)

`MessagesView.tsx`: wrap the grid and detail in `<PanelGroup direction="horizontal" onLayout={(s) => dispatch({ type: "layout", patch: { detailSize: Math.round(s[1] ?? shell.layout.detailSize) } })}>` with the grid `Panel` and, when `vs.detailOpen && sel.focus`, a `ResizeHandle` and a `Panel defaultSize={shell.layout.detailSize} minSize={20} maxSize={70}` holding `<DetailPane profileId={profileId} entity={tab.id} view={view} sequence={Number(sel.focus)} onClose={() => setVs({ detailOpen: false })} onShowColumn={(name) => setColumnIds([...columnIds.filter((c) => c !== propColumn(name)), propColumn(name)])} />`. Clicking a row while the pane is open updates it; Enter opens it. `state.ts`: `Layout.detailSize` (default 38, parsed with `num(l.detailSize, 20, 70, 38)`).

`Drawer.tsx` `JobsList` row: under the title, a finished job without error shows `j.summary` in `text-11 text-muted`; the right column for a running job holds the counts and `<Button size="sm" className="h-[18px]" onClick={() => void commands.jobCancel(j.id)} aria-label={`Cancel ${j.title}`}>Cancel</Button>` (the visible label stays "Cancel"; the accessible name carries the title so several jobs stay distinguishable); a finished job with `output` gets `<Button size="sm" className="h-[18px]" onClick={() => void commands.jobReveal(j.id)}>Show in folder</Button>`. Titles from Rust start with "Export", so the accessible name reads "Cancel Export invoice-generate (dead-letter) to …".

`context.tsx` `SheetState` adds the `export` member; `SheetHost` renders `<ExportSheet {...sheet} />` for it.

`app/src/shell/messages/ExportSheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { commands, useStartExport, type FilterSpec, type PeekView } from "@/engine";
import { Button, Problem, Segmented, Sheet, formatNumber } from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import { VIEW_LABEL } from "../tabs/subtabs";

export function ExportSheet(p: { entity: string; view: PeekView; filter: FilterSpec; selected: number[]; total: number | null }) {
  const { conn, closeSheet, dispatch } = useShell();
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<"all" | "selected">(p.selected.length > 0 ? "selected" : "all");
  const [format, setFormat] = useState<"json" | "ndjson">("json");
  const [picking, setPicking] = useState(false);
  const start = useStartExport();
  const filtered = Object.values(p.filter).some((v) => typeof v === "string" && v.trim());
  const what =
    scope === "selected"
      ? `${formatNumber(p.selected.length)} selected ${p.selected.length === 1 ? "message" : "messages"} of ${p.entity}, read again when the export runs.`
      : `Every message in the ${VIEW_LABEL[p.view]} of ${p.entity}${filtered ? " that matches the current filter" : ""}${p.total != null ? ` (about ${formatNumber(p.total)})` : ""}.`;
  const go = async () => {
    if (!conn.profileId) return;
    setPicking(true);
    try {
      const path = await commands.pickExportPath(p.entity, p.view, format);
      if (!path) return;
      await start.mutateAsync({
        profileId: conn.profileId,
        request: {
          entity: p.entity, view: p.view, format, path, overwrite: true, total_hint: scope === "all" ? p.total : null,
          scope: scope === "selected" ? { kind: "selected", sequences: p.selected } : { kind: "all", filter: p.filter },
        },
      });
      closeSheet();
      dispatch({ type: "layout", patch: { drawerOpen: true, drawerTab: "jobs" } });
    } finally {
      setPicking(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <Sheet
      open
      onOpenChange={(o) => !o && closeSheet()}
      title="Export messages"
      description="Peeked again and written to a file on this computer. Nothing is removed from Service Bus."
      footer={
        <>
          <Button onClick={closeSheet}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" disabled={picking || start.isPending} onClick={() => void go()}>Choose file and export</Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Messages</span>
          <Segmented
            label="Messages to export"
            value={scope}
            onChange={setScope}
            options={[{ value: "all", label: "All in this view" }, ...(p.selected.length ? [{ value: "selected" as const, label: `Selected (${formatNumber(p.selected.length)})` }] : [])]}
          />
          <p className="m-0 text-12 text-muted">{what}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Format</span>
          <Segmented label="Format" value={format} onChange={setFormat} options={[{ value: "json", label: "JSON" }, { value: "ndjson", label: "NDJSON" }]} />
          <p className="m-0 text-12 text-muted">
            {format === "json" ? "One JSON array, pretty-printed. Each message has its body and every property." : "One JSON object per line, for streaming tools such as jq."}
          </p>
        </div>
        {start.error && <Problem role="alert" title="Couldn't start the export" body="Choose another file or check the folder's permissions, then try again." detail={start.error.message} />}
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

`MessagesView.tsx`: the toolbar's `exportMenu` is a `DropdownMenu` with trigger `<Button variant="quiet"><Download className="size-3.5 text-icon" aria-hidden />Export</Button>` and items "Export all…" and, when rows are selected, `Export {n} selected…`; each opens `openSheet({ kind: "export", entity: tab.id, view, filter: vs.filter, selected: [...sel.ids].map(Number), total: <the view's count from counts or null> })`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { deadLetterUrl, entityBase, entityUrl } from "./entityUrls";

describe("entity URLs", () => {
  it("builds sb:// URLs like the engine's entity paths", () => {
    expect(entityUrl("ns.servicebus.windows.net", "orders", "queue")).toBe("sb://ns.servicebus.windows.net/orders");
    expect(entityBase("orders/eu/subscriptions/audit", "subscription")).toBe("orders/eu/Subscriptions/audit");
    expect(deadLetterUrl("ns.servicebus.windows.net", "events/subscriptions/all", "subscription")).toBe("sb://ns.servicebus.windows.net/events/Subscriptions/all/$DeadLetterQueue");
    expect(deadLetterUrl("ns", "events", "topic")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formatTimestamp } from "../time";
import { formatIsoDuration, formatProperty, labelOf } from "./labels";

describe("property labels and values", () => {
  it("labels known names and splits unknown ones", () => {
    expect(labelOf("LockDuration")).toBe("Lock duration");
    expect(labelOf("SomeNewServiceFlag")).toBe("Some new service flag");
  });
  it("formats ISO 8601 durations, with the maximum as never", () => {
    expect(formatIsoDuration("PT1M")).toBe("1 min");
    expect(formatIsoDuration("PT30S")).toBe("30 s");
    expect(formatIsoDuration("P14D")).toBe("14 days");
    expect(formatIsoDuration("P1DT2H")).toBe("1 day 2 h");
    expect(formatIsoDuration("PT1.5S")).toBe("1.5 s");
    expect(formatIsoDuration("P10675199DT2H48M5.4775807S")).toBe("Never");
    expect(formatIsoDuration("nonsense")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("formats flags, sizes and times", () => {
    expect(formatProperty("RequiresSession", "true")).toBe("Yes");
    expect(formatProperty("MaxSizeInMegabytes", "1024")).toBe("1,024 MB");
    expect(formatProperty("SizeInBytes", "81920")).toBe("80.0 KiB");
    // Local time: compare with the same formatter, so the test passes in every time zone.
    expect(formatProperty("CreatedAt", "2026-09-01T08:00:00Z")).toBe(formatTimestamp("2026-09-01T08:00:00Z"));
    expect(formatProperty("CreatedAt", "2026-09-01T08:00:00Z")).toMatch(/^2026-0[89]-\d\d \d\d:\d\d:00$/);
    expect(formatProperty("UserMetadata", "team=ops")).toBe("team=ops");
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { activityStore } from "../drawer/activity";
import { renderShell } from "../test-utils";

const seed = (id: string, kind: string, profile = "p-logistics") => ({
  last_profile: profile,
  [`shell:${profile}`]: { tabs: [{ id, kind, subTab: "overview" }], activeTab: id, expanded: [], selected: null, layout: { treeSize: 18, drawerOpen: false, drawerSize: 18, drawerTab: "activity" } },
});

describe("overview", () => {
  it("shows counts with the dead-letter number in amber, and the properties", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seed("invoice-generate", "queue"));
    const counts = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Counts" });
    const dlq = within(counts).getByText("37");
    expect(dlq.className).toContain("text-accent");
    expect(within(counts).getByText("1,284")).toBeInTheDocument();
    const props = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Properties" });
    expect(within(props).getByText("Lock duration")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(props).getByText("30 s")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(props).getByText("Never")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("copies the URL and the dead-letter URL", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seed("invoice-generate", "queue"));
    const addresses = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Addresses" });
    await userEvent.click(within(addresses).getByRole("button", { name: "Copy URL" }));
    expect(await navigator.clipboard.readText()).toBe("sb://sb-logistics-prod.servicebus.windows.net/invoice-generate");
    await userEvent.click(within(addresses).getByRole("button", { name: "Copy dead-letter URL" }));
    expect(await navigator.clipboard.readText()).toBe("sb://sb-logistics-prod.servicebus.windows.net/invoice-generate/$DeadLetterQueue");
  });

  it("asks the engine to copy the connection string and never shows it", async () => {
    const { mock } = renderShell("default", seed("invoice-generate", "queue"));
    const addresses = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Addresses" });
    await userEvent.click(within(addresses).getByRole("button", { name: "Copy connection string" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(mock.calls.some((c) => c.cmd === "copy_entity_connection_string")).toBe(true));
    expect(screen.queryByText(/SharedAccessKey/)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reading the details logs nothing unless it fails", async () => {
    // Overview polls every 10 s; Rust and the mock log entity_details only on failure.
    renderShell("default", seed("invoice-generate", "queue"));
    await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Counts" });
    expect(activityStore.all().some((e) => /details/i.test(e.action))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("Entra connections explain why there is no connection string", async () => {
    renderShell("default", seed("invoice-generate", "queue", "p-logistics-dev"));
    const addresses = await screen.findByRole("region", { name: "Addresses" });
    expect(within(addresses).getByRole("button", { name: "Copy connection string" })).toBeDisabled();
    expect(within(addresses).getByText(/Entra ID connections have no connection string/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

(`p-logistics-dev` is an Entra profile in the fixtures; the mock connects it without a sign-in check. If it does not, sign in first through `mock.invoke("sign_in", { tenant: null, deviceCode: false })`.)

`app/src/shell/entityUrls.ts`:

```ts
import { splitSubscriptionId } from "./entityId";
import type { EntityKind } from "./state";

export function entityBase(id: string, kind: EntityKind): string {
  if (kind !== "subscription") return id;
  const { topic, name } = splitSubscriptionId(id);
  return `${topic}/Subscriptions/${name}`;
}
export const entityUrl = (host: string, id: string, kind: EntityKind) => `sb://${host}/${entityBase(id, kind)}`;
export const deadLetterUrl = (host: string, id: string, kind: EntityKind) => (kind === "topic" ? null : `${entityUrl(host, id, kind)}/$DeadLetterQueue`);
```

`app/src/shell/overview/labels.ts`:

```ts
import { formatBytes, formatNumber } from "@/ui";
import { formatTimestamp } from "../time";

const LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  LockDuration: "Lock duration", MaxSizeInMegabytes: "Max size", RequiresDuplicateDetection: "Duplicate detection",
  RequiresSession: "Sessions", DefaultMessageTimeToLive: "Message time to live", DeadLetteringOnMessageExpiration: "Dead-letter expired messages",
  DeadLetteringOnFilterEvaluationExceptions: "Dead-letter filter errors", DuplicateDetectionHistoryTimeWindow: "Duplicate detection window",
  MaxDeliveryCount: "Max delivery count", EnableBatchedOperations: "Batched operations", EnablePartitioning: "Partitioning",
  EnableExpress: "Express", ForwardTo: "Forwards to", ForwardDeadLetteredMessagesTo: "Forwards dead letters to",
  AutoDeleteOnIdle: "Delete when idle", UserMetadata: "User metadata", CreatedAt: "Created", UpdatedAt: "Updated",
  AccessedAt: "Last accessed", MessageCount: "Messages", MaxMessageSizeInKilobytes: "Max message size", SupportOrdering: "Ordering",
  Status: "Status", SizeInBytes: "Size", SubscriptionCount: "Subscriptions", EntityAvailabilityStatus: "Availability",
};

export function labelOf(name: string): string {
  if (LABEL[name]) return LABEL[name];
  const words = name.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, "$1 $2").toLowerCase();
  return words[0].toUpperCase() + words.slice(1);
}

const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;

/** `PT1M` → `1 min`, `P14D` → `14 days`; .NET's TimeSpan.MaxValue (`P10675199D…`) is "Never". */
export function formatIsoDuration(v: string): string | null {
  const m = /^P(?:(\d+)D)?(?:T(?:(\d+)H)?(?:(\d+)M)?(?:(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)S)?)?$/.exec(v);
  if (!m || v === "P" || v === "PT") return null;
  const [d, h, min, s] = [m[1], m[2], m[3], m[4]].map((x) => (x ? Number(x) : 0));
  if (d >= 10_675_199) return "Never";
  const parts = [d && plural(d, "day", "days"), h && `${h} h`, min && `${min} min`, s && `${Number(s.toFixed(3))} s`].filter(Boolean);
  return parts.length ? parts.join(" ") : "0 s";
}

export function formatProperty(name: string, value: string): string {
  if (value === "true") return "Yes";
  if (value === "false") return "No";
  if (/At$/.test(name)) return formatTimestamp(value) || value;
  if (name === "MaxSizeInMegabytes") return `${formatNumber(Number(value))} MB`;
  if (name === "MaxMessageSizeInKilobytes") return `${formatNumber(Number(value))} KB`;
  if (name === "SizeInBytes") return formatBytes(Number(value));
  if (/^P/.test(value)) return formatIsoDuration(value) ?? value;
  return value;
}
```

`app/src/shell/overview/OverviewView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Copy, KeyRound } from "lucide-react";
import { commands, useEntityDetails, useProfiles, type EntityCounts } from "@/engine";
import { Button, CountBadge, EmptyState, Hint, IconButton, Problem, SkeletonRows, formatBytes, formatClock } from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import { deadLetterUrl, entityUrl } from "../entityUrls";
import type { EntityTab } from "../state";
import { formatProperty, labelOf } from "./labels";

const REFRESH_MS = 10_000;
const HIDDEN = new Set(["Status", "SizeInBytes"]);

function CountRow({ label, value, c, attention }: { label: string; value: number | undefined; c: EntityCounts | null; attention?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 py-0.5">
      <dt className="text-12 text-muted">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="m-0"><CountBadge value={value} quality={c ?? undefined} attention={attention} className="text-13" /></dd>
    </div>
  );
}

function Address({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-0.5">
      <span className="w-[112px] shrink-0 text-12 text-muted">{label}</span>
      <span className="min-w-0 truncate font-mono text-11" data-selectable title={value}>{value}</span>
      <Hint label={`Copy ${label === "URL" ? "URL" : "dead-letter URL"}`}>
        <IconButton label={`Copy ${label === "URL" ? "URL" : "dead-letter URL"}`} className="ml-auto size-5" onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(value).catch(() => {})}><Copy /></IconButton>
      </Hint>
    </div>
  );
}

export function OverviewView({ tab }: { tab: EntityTab }) {
  const { conn, dispatch } = useShell();
  const kind = tab.kind;
  const q = useEntityDetails(conn.profileId, kind, tab.id, REFRESH_MS);
  const profile = useProfiles().data?.find((p) => p.id === conn.profileId);
  const host = profile?.host ?? conn.view?.host ?? "";
  const entra = profile?.auth.kind === "entra";
  if (q.isPending) return <SkeletonRows rows={10} columns={2} />;
  if (q.isError && !q.data)
    return q.error.kind === "not_found" ? (
      <EmptyState tone="error" title="This entity wasn't found" body="It may have been deleted or renamed. Refresh the tree to see what is there now." action={<Button onClick={() => dispatch({ type: "close", id: tab.id })}>Close tab</Button>} />
    ) : (
      <EmptyState tone="error" title="Couldn't read this entity" body="Try again. If it keeps failing, the detail below says why." detail={q.error.message} action={<Button onClick={() => void q.refetch()}>Try again</Button>} />
    );
  const d = q.data!;
  const c = d.counts;
  const dlqUrl = deadLetterUrl(host, tab.id, kind);
  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 grid-cols-[minmax(240px,320px)_minmax(320px,1fr)] gap-x-8 overflow-y-auto px-3.5 py-3" data-shot-scroller>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        {kind !== "topic" && (
          <section aria-label="Counts">
            <h2 className="m-0 mb-1 text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Counts</h2>
            <dl className="m-0">
              <CountRow label="Active" value={c?.active} c={c} />
              <CountRow label="Dead-letter" value={c?.dead_letter} c={c} attention />
              <CountRow label="Scheduled" value={c?.scheduled} c={c} />
              <CountRow label="Transfer" value={c?.transfer} c={c} />
              <CountRow label="Transfer DLQ" value={c?.transfer_dead_letter} c={c} attention />
            </dl>
            <p className="m-0 mt-1 text-11 text-muted">
              {d.size_in_bytes != null && `${formatBytes(d.size_in_bytes)} · `}
              {c?.approximate ? "Counted by peeking · " : ""}Updated {formatClock(new Date(q.dataUpdatedAt).toISOString())}, every 10 s
            </p>
            {q.isError && <Problem title="Couldn't refresh the counts" body="Showing the last counts read." detail={q.error.message} />}
          </section>
        )}
        <section aria-label="Addresses">
          <h2 className="m-0 mb-1 text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Addresses</h2>
          <Address label="URL" value={entityUrl(host, tab.id, kind)} />
          {dlqUrl && <Address label="Dead-letter URL" value={dlqUrl} />}
          {kind !== "topic" && (
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
              <Button className="self-start" disabled={entra} onClick={() => conn.profileId && void commands.copyEntityConnectionString(conn.profileId, tab.id).catch(() => {})}>
                <KeyRound aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5 text-icon" />Copy connection string
              </Button>
              <span className="text-11 text-muted">
                {entra ? "Entra ID connections have no connection string. Copy the URL instead." : "Scoped to this entity. It goes to the clipboard and is never shown."}
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
      <section aria-label="Properties">
        <h2 className="m-0 mb-1 text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Properties</h2>
        <dl className="m-0 grid grid-cols-[max-content_1fr] gap-x-6 gap-y-0.5">
          {d.status && (<><dt className="text-12 text-muted">Status</dt><dd className="m-0 text-12">{d.status}</dd></>)}
          {d.properties.filter((p) => !HIDDEN.has(p.name)).map((p) => (
            <div key={p.name} className="contents">
              <dt className="text-12 text-muted" title={p.name}>{labelOf(p.name)}</dt>
              <dd className="m-0 break-all text-12" data-selectable>{formatProperty(p.name, p.value)}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`EntityView.tsx`: the `overview` sub-tab renders `<OverviewView tab={tab} />` (topics included). Copying the connection string records "Copied the connection string for …" in Activity; a failure shows there too (the engine logs it).

`app/src/shell/sessions/SessionsView.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useSessions, type SessionSummary } from "@/engine";
import { Button, DataGrid, EmptyState, Input, formatNumber, rowHeight, type GridSort } from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import { setViewState, viewKey } from "../messages/viewStore";
import type { EntityTab } from "../state";
import { formatTimestamp } from "../time";

export function SessionsView({ tab, requiresSession }: { tab: EntityTab; requiresSession: boolean | null }) {
  const { conn, prefs, dispatch } = useShell();
  const q = useSessions(conn.profileId, tab.id, requiresSession !== false);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("");
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<GridSort | null>(null);
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<{ ids: Set<string>; focus: string | null }>({ ids: new Set(), focus: null });
  const truncated = q.data?.truncated ?? false;
  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<SessionSummary, unknown>[]>(() => [
    { id: "id", header: "Session", accessorFn: (s) => s.id, meta: { mono: true, width: "minmax(160px, 1fr)" } },
    { id: "messages", header: "Messages", accessorFn: (s) => s.messages ?? -1, meta: { align: "end", width: "96px" },
      cell: (c) => { const n = c.row.original.messages; return n == null ? "?" : truncated ? `~${formatNumber(n)}+` : formatNumber(n); } },
    { id: "first", header: "First enqueued", accessorFn: (s) => s.first_enqueued ?? "", meta: { mono: true, width: "150px" }, cell: (c) => formatTimestamp(c.row.original.first_enqueued) },
    { id: "last", header: "Last enqueued", accessorFn: (s) => s.last_enqueued ?? "", meta: { mono: true, width: "150px" }, cell: (c) => formatTimestamp(c.row.original.last_enqueued) },
  ], [truncated]);
  const rows = useMemo(() => (q.data?.sessions ?? []).filter((s) => s.id.toLowerCase().includes(filter.trim().toLowerCase())), [q.data, filter]);
  const noun = tab.kind === "subscription" ? "subscription" : "queue";
  if (requiresSession === false)
    return <EmptyState title={`This ${noun} doesn't use sessions`} body="Sessions appear here for queues and subscriptions created with sessions enabled." />;
  const peek = (id: string) => {
    setViewState(viewKey(conn.profileId, tab.id, "active"), { from: { kind: "start" }, topN: prefs.peekSize, filter: {}, filterOpen: true, sorting: null, detailOpen: false }, { filter: { session_id: id }, filterOpen: true });
    dispatch({ type: "subTab", id: tab.id, subTab: "messages" });
  };
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div role="toolbar" aria-label="Sessions" className="flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line px-3">
        <Input type="search" aria-label="Filter sessions" placeholder="Filter sessions" className="w-[220px]" value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)} />
        <Button disabled={!sel.focus} onClick={() => sel.focus && peek(sel.focus)}>Peek messages</Button>
        <span className="ml-auto text-11 text-muted">
          {q.data && (q.data.from_broker ? "Listed by Service Bus" : "Found by peeking: the service didn't list sessions")}
          {truncated && ` · counts from the first ${formatNumber(q.data!.scanned)} messages`}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <DataGrid<SessionSummary>
          label="Sessions"
          columns={columns}
          rows={rows}
          getRowId={(s) => s.id}
          selectedIds={sel.ids}
          focusId={sel.focus}
          onSelectionChange={(ids, focus) => setSel({ ids, focus })}
          onActivate={peek}
          rowHeight={rowHeight[prefs.density]}
          sorting={sorting}
          onSortingChange={setSorting}
          loading={q.isPending}
          error={q.isError ? <EmptyState tone="error" title="Couldn't list sessions" body="Try again. If it keeps failing, the detail below says why." detail={q.error.message} action={<Button onClick={() => void q.refetch()}>Try again</Button>} /> : undefined}
          empty={<EmptyState title="No sessions" body="No session has messages or state right now." />}
          initialRect={{ width: 900, height: 600 }}
        />
      </div>
      <p className="m-0 border-t border-line px-3 py-1 text-11 text-muted">Session state needs a session lock, so it is read and edited with the operate features.</p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

(DataGrid rows need an accessible name for the test's `getByRole("row", { name: /RMA-1003/ })`: give each row `aria-label` from its first cell text, via a new optional `rowLabel?(row: R): string` prop on DataGrid (add it in this task with a one-line test in `DataGrid.test.tsx`).)

`app/src/shell/rules/RulesView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { CircleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { useRules, type RuleFilter, type RuleInfo } from "@/engine";
import { Button, EmptyState, RuleGlyph, SkeletonRows } from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import type { EntityTab } from "../state";

const norm = (s: string) => s.replace(/\s+/g, "");

export function ruleKind(f: RuleFilter): string {
  if (f.kind === "correlation") return "Correlation filter";
  if (norm(f.expression) === "1=1") return "Matches every message";
  if (norm(f.expression) === "1=0") return "Matches no message";
  return "SQL filter";
}

const FIELDS: [keyof Extract<RuleFilter, { kind: "correlation" }>, string][] = [
  ["correlation_id", "Correlation ID"], ["message_id", "Message ID"], ["to", "To"], ["reply_to", "Reply to"], ["subject", "Subject"],
  ["session_id", "Session ID"], ["reply_to_session_id", "Reply to session ID"], ["content_type", "Content type"],
];

function Rule({ r }: { r: RuleInfo }) {
  const f = r.filter;
  const pairs = f.kind === "correlation" ? [...FIELDS.filter(([k]) => f[k]).map(([k, l]) => [l, String(f[k])] as const), ...f.properties.map((p) => [p.name, p.value] as const)] : [];
  return (
    <li aria-label={r.name} className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-b border-line px-3.5 py-2.5">
      <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
        <RuleGlyph aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5 self-center text-icon" />
        <span className="text-12 font-semibold">{r.name}</span>
        <span className="text-11 text-muted">{ruleKind(f)}</span>
      </div>
      {f.kind === "sql" ? (
        <code className="block whitespace-pre-wrap break-all font-mono text-11" data-selectable>{f.expression}</code>
      ) : pairs.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="m-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-12"><CircleAlert aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5 text-accent" />This rule matches nothing: its correlation filter sets no fields.</p>
      ) : (
        <dl className="m-0 grid grid-cols-[max-content_1fr] gap-x-4">
          {pairs.map(([k, v]) => (
            <div key={k} className="contents">
              <dt className="font-mono text-11 text-muted">{k}</dt>
              <dd className="m-0 font-mono text-11" data-selectable>{v}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
      )}
      {r.action && (
        <p className="m-0 text-11 text-muted">Action <code className="font-mono text-fg" data-selectable>{r.action}</code></p>
      )}
    </li>
  );
}

export function RulesView({ tab }: { tab: EntityTab }) {
  const { conn } = useShell();
  const q = useRules(conn.profileId, tab.id);
  if (q.isPending) return <SkeletonRows rows={6} />;
  if (q.isError) return <EmptyState tone="error" title="Couldn't list the rules" body="Listing rules needs Manage or Listen on this subscription." detail={q.error.message} action={<Button onClick={() => void q.refetch()}>Try again</Button>} />;
  if (q.data.length === 0) return <EmptyState title="No rules" body="A subscription without rules receives no messages." />;
  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto" data-shot-scroller>
      <ul aria-label="Rules" className="m-0 list-none p-0">{q.data.map((r) => <Rule key={r.name} r={r} />)}</ul>
      <p className="m-0 px-3.5 py-2 text-11 text-muted">Rules are read-only here. Adding and editing them comes with entity management.</p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`EntityView.tsx`: `sessions` → `<SessionsView tab={tab} requiresSession={info.status === "found" ? info.facts.includes("sessions") : null} />`; `rules` → `<RulesView tab={tab} />`.
