`state.ts`: `export type TabKind = EntityKind | "dashboard"; export const DASHBOARD_ID = "::dashboard";` `OpenTab.kind: TabKind`; `parseProfileShell` accepts `kind === "dashboard"` only when `id === DASHBOARD_ID`; `Prefs.dashboardRefresh` parsed with `oneOf(o.dashboardRefresh, [0, 30, 60, 300] as const, 0)`. Places that switch on `tab.kind` handle `"dashboard"`: `Workspace` narrows before rendering, so `EntityView`, `useEntityInfo`, `OverviewView`, `MessagesView`, `SessionsView` and `RulesView`, which take `tab: EntityTab` since Task 14 (`type EntityTab = OpenTab & { kind: EntityKind }`), never see a dashboard and need no change; `EntityTabs`, `treeRowOf` (typed `EntityTab`; `CommandPalette` skips the dashboard before calling it) and the palette handle it explicitly (the tree does not reveal it; the tab strip shows "Dashboard" without a kind word; `Workspace` renders `<DashboardView />` for it instead of `EntityView`).

`app/src/shell/dashboard/tsv.ts`:

```ts
import type { DashboardRow } from "@/engine";

const cell = (v: string | number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? "" : String(v).replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g, " "));

/** Header plus one line per row; numbers raw, unknown counts empty. */
export function toTsv(rows: DashboardRow[]): string {
  const head = ["Entity", "Topic", "Kind", "Status", "Active", "Dead-letter", "Scheduled", "Transfer", "Transfer DLQ"];
  const lines = rows.map((r) => {
    const c = r.counts;
    return [r.name, r.topic, r.kind, r.status, c?.active, c?.dead_letter, c?.scheduled, c?.transfer, c?.transfer_dead_letter].map(cell).join("\t");
  });
  return [head.join("\t"), ...lines].join("\n");
}
```

`app/src/shell/dashboard/DashboardView.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ColumnDef } from "@tanstack/react-table";
import { ClipboardCopy, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useDashboard, type DashboardRow } from "@/engine";
import { Button, CountBadge, DataGrid, EmptyState, EntityGlyph, IconButton, Input, Problem, Segmented, formatClock, rowHeight, type GridSort } from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import { toTsv } from "./tsv";

const count = (pick: (r: DashboardRow) => number | undefined, label: string, id: string, attention = false): ColumnDef<DashboardRow, unknown> => ({
  id, header: label, accessorFn: (r) => pick(r) ?? -1, meta: { align: "end", width: "96px" },
  cell: (c) => <CountBadge value={pick(c.row.original)} quality={c.row.original.counts ?? undefined} attention={attention} className="text-12" />,
});

export function DashboardView() {
  const { conn, prefs, setPrefs, dispatch } = useShell();
  const q = useDashboard(conn.profileId, prefs.dashboardRefresh * 1000);
  const [sorting, setSorting] = useState<GridSort | null>({ id: "dlq", desc: true });
  const [onlyDlq, setOnlyDlq] = useState(false);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("");
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<{ ids: Set<string>; focus: string | null }>({ ids: new Set(), focus: null });
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const columns = useMemo<ColumnDef<DashboardRow, unknown>[]>(() => [
    {
      id: "entity", header: "Entity", accessorFn: (r) => `${r.topic ?? ""}/${r.name}`, meta: { width: "minmax(220px, 2fr)" },
      cell: (c) => (
        <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
          <EntityGlyph kind={c.row.original.kind} className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-icon" />
          <span className="truncate">{c.row.original.name}</span>
          {c.row.original.topic && <span className="truncate text-muted">{c.row.original.topic}</span>}
        </span>
      ),
    },
    { id: "status", header: "Status", accessorFn: (r) => r.status ?? "", meta: { width: "110px" } },
    count((r) => r.counts?.active, "Active", "active"),
    count((r) => r.counts?.dead_letter, "Dead-letter", "dlq", true),
    count((r) => r.counts?.scheduled, "Scheduled", "scheduled"),
    count((r) => r.counts?.transfer, "Transfer", "transfer"),
    count((r) => r.counts?.transfer_dead_letter, "Transfer DLQ", "tdlq", true),
  ], []);
  const rows = useMemo(() => {
    const term = filter.trim().toLowerCase();
    return (q.data?.rows ?? []).filter((r) => (!onlyDlq || (r.counts?.dead_letter ?? 0) > 0) && (!term || `${r.topic ?? ""}/${r.name}`.toLowerCase().includes(term)));
  }, [q.data, onlyDlq, filter]);
  const open = (id: string) => {
    const r = rows.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (r) dispatch({ type: "open", tab: { id: r.id, kind: r.kind } });
  };
  const copy = () => {
    void navigator.clipboard?.writeText(toTsv(rows)).then(() => {
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
    }).catch(() => {});
  };
  const failures = q.data?.failures ?? [];
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-4 gap-y-1 px-3.5 pt-2.5">
        <h1 className="m-0 text-14 font-semibold">Dashboard</h1>
        <span className="text-muted">Every queue and subscription with its counts</span>
      </div>
      <div role="toolbar" aria-label="Dashboard options" className="mt-1.5 flex h-8 shrink-0 items-center gap-2 border-y border-line px-3">
        <Input type="search" aria-label="Filter entities" placeholder="Filter" className="w-[200px]" value={filter} onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)} />
        <Button variant="quiet" aria-pressed={onlyDlq} onClick={() => setOnlyDlq(!onlyDlq)}>Only with dead letters</Button>
        <span className="ml-2 text-12 text-muted">Auto-refresh</span>
        <Segmented
          label="Auto-refresh"
          value={String(prefs.dashboardRefresh)}
          onChange={(v) => setPrefs({ dashboardRefresh: Number(v) as 0 | 30 | 60 | 300 })}
          options={[{ value: "0", label: "Off" }, { value: "30", label: "30 s" }, { value: "60", label: "1 min" }, { value: "300", label: "5 min" }]}
        />
        <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
          {q.dataUpdatedAt > 0 && <span className="text-11 tabular-nums text-muted">Refreshed {formatClock(new Date(q.dataUpdatedAt).toISOString())}</span>}
          <Button variant="quiet" onClick={copy}><ClipboardCopy aria-hidden="true" className="size-3.5 text-icon" />{copied ? "Copied" : "Copy as TSV"}</Button>
          <IconButton label="Refresh the dashboard" onClick={() => void q.refetch()}><RefreshCw /></IconButton>
        </div>
      </div>
      {q.data?.limited && <div className="border-b border-line px-3 py-2.5"><Problem title="Showing pinned entities only" body={q.data.limited} /></div>}
      {failures.length > 0 && (
        <div className="border-b border-line px-3 py-2.5">
          <Problem
            title={`Couldn't list the subscriptions of ${failures.length} ${failures.length === 1 ? "topic" : "topics"}`}
            body={failures.map((f) => f.topic).join(", ")}
            detail={failures[0].message}
          />
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
        <DataGrid<DashboardRow>
          label="Dashboard"
          columns={columns}
          rows={rows}
          getRowId={(r) => r.id}
          selectedIds={sel.ids}
          focusId={sel.focus}
          onSelectionChange={(ids, focus) => setSel({ ids, focus })}
          onActivate={open}
          rowHeight={rowHeight[prefs.density]}
          sorting={sorting}
          onSortingChange={setSorting}
          loading={q.isPending}
          error={q.isError && !q.data ? <EmptyState tone="error" title="Couldn't load the dashboard" body="Try again. If it keeps failing, the detail below says why." detail={q.error.message} action={<Button onClick={() => void q.refetch()}>Try again</Button>} /> : undefined}
          empty={<EmptyState title={onlyDlq ? "No dead letters anywhere" : "No queues or subscriptions"} />}
          initialRect={{ width: 1000, height: 600 }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`palette/commands.ts`: `{ id: "app:dashboard", label: "Open dashboard", shortcut: "mod+shift+d", run: () => c.open({ id: DASHBOARD_ID, kind: "dashboard" }) }` in the App group (connected only); `Hotkeys.tsx`: `{ combo: "mod+shift+d", run: () => free && dispatch({ type: "open", tab: { id: DASHBOARD_ID, kind: "dashboard" } }) }`. `NamespaceTree.activate`: a `namespace` node opens the dashboard; its context menu gains "Open dashboard".

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { compileRules, highlightOf } from "./highlight";

const c = (active: number, dead_letter: number) => ({ active, dead_letter, scheduled: 0, transfer: 0, transfer_dead_letter: 0, approximate: false, truncated: false });

describe("highlight rules", () => {
  it("applies the first matching rule", () => {
    const rules = compileRules([
      { id: "1", color: "rose", match: { kind: "count", count: "dead-letter", atLeast: 100 } },
      { id: "2", color: "blue", match: { kind: "name", pattern: "^invoice-" } },
    ]);
    expect(highlightOf("invoice-generate", c(1, 300), rules)).toBe("rose");
    expect(highlightOf("invoice-generate", c(1, 3), rules)).toBe("blue");
    expect(highlightOf("label-print", c(1, 3), rules)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("skips bad patterns and unknown counts", () => {
    const rules = compileRules([
      { id: "1", color: "green", match: { kind: "name", pattern: "(" } },
      { id: "2", color: "slate", match: { kind: "count", count: "active", atLeast: 1 } },
    ]);
    expect(rules).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(highlightOf("x", null, rules)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("matches names case-insensitively", () => {
    expect(highlightOf("Orders-EU", c(0, 0), compileRules([{ id: "1", color: "violet", match: { kind: "name", pattern: "orders" } }]))).toBe("violet");
  });
});

import { screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { renderShell } from "../test-utils";

describe("explorer options", () => {
  it("adds a name rule, rejects a bad pattern, and saves", async () => {
    const { mock } = renderShell("default", { last_profile: "p-logistics" });
    await screen.findByRole("tree", { name: "Namespace" });
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Explorer options" }));

});

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`app/src/shell/tree/highlight.ts`:

```ts
import type { EntityCounts } from "@/engine";
import type { LabelColor } from "@/ui";
import type { CountKind, HighlightRule } from "../state";

export type Compiled = { color: LabelColor; test(name: string, c: EntityCounts | null | undefined): boolean };

const value = (c: EntityCounts, k: CountKind) =>
  k === "active" ? c.active : k === "dead-letter" ? c.dead_letter : k === "scheduled" ? c.scheduled : c.transfer_dead_letter;

export function compileRules(rules: HighlightRule[]): Compiled[] {
  const out: Compiled[] = [];
  for (const r of rules) {
    if (r.match.kind === "name") {
      try {
        const re = new RegExp(r.match.pattern, "i");
        out.push({ color: r.color, test: (name) => re.test(name) });
      } catch {
        // a bad pattern highlights nothing
      }
    } else {
      const { count, atLeast } = r.match;
      out.push({ color: r.color, test: (_n, c) => !!c && value(c, count) >= atLeast });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

export function highlightOf(name: string, counts: EntityCounts | null | undefined, rules: Compiled[]): LabelColor | null {
  return rules.find((r) => r.test(name, counts))?.color ?? null;
}
```

`state.ts`: add the types from Interfaces, `emptyShell.explorer = { counts: ["active", "dead-letter"], rules: [], openOn: "overview" }`, `emptyShell.treeFilter = ""`; `parseProfileShell` validates (`counts` filtered to the four kinds, default when empty; each rule needs a known `color` from `labelColors`, a `name` match with a string pattern up to 200 characters or a `count` match with a known kind and a finite `atLeast >= 0`; at most 20 rules; `openOn` via `oneOf`; `treeFilter` a string up to 200 characters). Reducer: `explorer` replaces `s.explorer`; `treeFilter` sets it; `open` uses `a.tab.subTab ?? (a.tab.kind === "topic" || a.tab.kind === "dashboard" ? "overview" : s.explorer.openOn)`.

`NamespaceTree.tsx`:
- The filter input reads and writes `shell.treeFilter` through `dispatch({ type: "treeFilter", value })` (Escape clears it).
- `Counts` renders, in order, the chosen counts among active, scheduled and transfer DLQ (muted `CountBadge`s; scheduled and transfer DLQ hidden when zero, like dead letters) and dead-letter last (amber above zero); before them, when a rule matches the row (`highlightOf(n.label, n.counts, compiled)` for queue, subscription and known rows; name rules also for topics), `<LabelSwatch color={color} />` plus `<span className="sr-only">highlighted</span>`.
- The header gets an `IconButton` (`SlidersHorizontal`, label "Explorer options") that opens `{ kind: "explorer-options" }`.
- Context menu items as in Interfaces: "Copy URL" → `entityUrl(host, entity.id, entity.kind)`; "Copy dead-letter URL" (not on topics) → `deadLetterUrl(...)`; "Copy connection string" → `commands.copyEntityConnectionString(profileId, entity.id)`, disabled with the Entra hint as its `title` when the profile is Entra; "Open dashboard" on the namespace row. Clipboard writes use `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, as M1b's "Copy name".

`palette/commands.ts`: "Explorer options" (opens the sheet) and, when an entity tab is active, "Copy URL of {name}" and "Copy connection string of {name}" (connection-string profiles only). `PaletteContext` gains `activeTab` and `copy(text)`, `copyConnectionString(entityId)`.

`app/src/shell/sheets/ExplorerOptionsSheet.tsx`:

```tsx
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useProfiles } from "@/engine";
import {
  Button, Field, IconButton, Input, LabelSwatch, Segmented, Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue, Sheet, ToggleGroup, labelColors, type LabelColor,
} from "@/ui";
import { useShell } from "../context";
import type { CountKind, ExplorerOptions, HighlightRule } from "../state";

type MatchKey = "name" | CountKind;
const MATCH: { value: MatchKey; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "name", label: "Name matches" }, { value: "active", label: "Active at least" }, { value: "dead-letter", label: "Dead-letter at least" },
  { value: "scheduled", label: "Scheduled at least" }, { value: "transfer-dlq", label: "Transfer DLQ at least" },
];
const COLORS = Object.keys(labelColors) as LabelColor[];

const keyOf = (r: HighlightRule): MatchKey => (r.match.kind === "name" ? "name" : r.match.count);
const textOf = (r: HighlightRule) => (r.match.kind === "name" ? r.match.pattern : String(r.match.atLeast));
const problem = (r: HighlightRule): string | undefined => {
  if (r.match.kind === "count") return Number.isFinite(r.match.atLeast) && r.match.atLeast >= 0 ? undefined : "Enter a whole number, 0 or more.";
  if (!r.match.pattern.trim()) return "Enter a pattern, such as ^orders- or -dlq$.";
  try { new RegExp(r.match.pattern); return undefined; } catch (e) { return `Not a valid pattern: ${(e as Error).message}`; }
};

export function ExplorerOptionsSheet() {
  const { shell, dispatch, closeSheet, conn } = useShell();
  const name = useProfiles().data?.find((p) => p.id === conn.profileId)?.name ?? "this connection";
  const [o, setO] = useState<ExplorerOptions>(shell.explorer);
  const setRule = (i: number, r: HighlightRule) => setO({ ...o, rules: o.rules.map((x, j) => (j === i ? r : x)) });
  const blocked = o.rules.some((r) => problem(r));
  return (
    <Sheet
      open
      onOpenChange={(v) => !v && closeSheet()}
      title={`Explorer options for ${name}`}
      description="Saved for this connection only."
      initialFocus="field"
      footer={
        <>
          <Button onClick={closeSheet}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" disabled={blocked} onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: "explorer", options: o }); closeSheet(); }}>Save</Button>
        </>
      }
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Counts in the tree</span>
          <ToggleGroup
            label="Counts in the tree"
            values={o.counts}
            onChange={(counts) => setO({ ...o, counts: counts.length ? counts : ["active"] })}
            options={[{ value: "active", label: "Active" }, { value: "scheduled", label: "Scheduled" }, { value: "transfer-dlq", label: "Transfer DLQ" }, { value: "dead-letter", label: "Dead-letter" }]}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Open entities on</span>
          <Segmented label="Open entities on" value={o.openOn} onChange={(openOn) => setO({ ...o, openOn })} options={[{ value: "overview", label: "Overview" }, { value: "messages", label: "Messages" }, { value: "dead-letter", label: "Dead-letter" }]} />
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <span className="text-12 font-semibold text-muted">Highlight rules</span>
          <p className="m-0 text-12 text-muted">Mark tree rows with a colour. The first rule that matches a row wins.</p>
          {o.rules.map((r, i) => (
            <div key={r.id} className="flex items-start gap-2">
              <Select value={r.color} onValueChange={(color) => setRule(i, { ...r, color: color as LabelColor })}>
                <SelectTrigger aria-label={`Rule ${i + 1} colour`} className="w-[56px]"><SelectValue>{<LabelSwatch color={r.color} />}</SelectValue></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>{COLORS.map((c) => <SelectItem key={c} value={c}><span className="flex items-center gap-2"><LabelSwatch color={c} />{c[0].toUpperCase() + c.slice(1)}</span></SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
              </Select>
              <Select
                value={keyOf(r)}
                onValueChange={(k) => setRule(i, { ...r, match: k === "name" ? { kind: "name", pattern: "" } : { kind: "count", count: k as CountKind, atLeast: 1 } })}
              >
                <SelectTrigger aria-label={`Rule ${i + 1} match`} className="w-[150px]"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>{MATCH.map((m) => <SelectItem key={m.value} value={m.value}>{m.label}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
              </Select>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Field label={`Rule ${i + 1} value`} hideLabel error={problem(r)}>
                  {(ids) => (
                    <Input
                      {...ids}
                      mono={r.match.kind === "name"}
                      inputMode={r.match.kind === "count" ? "numeric" : undefined}
                      value={textOf(r)}
                      onChange={(e) => setRule(i, r.match.kind === "name" ? { ...r, match: { kind: "name", pattern: e.target.value } } : { ...r, match: { ...r.match, atLeast: e.target.value === "" ? NaN : Number(e.target.value) } })}
                    />
                  )}
                </Field>
              </div>
              <IconButton label={`Remove rule ${i + 1}`} onClick={() => setO({ ...o, rules: o.rules.filter((_, j) => j !== i) })}><X /></IconButton>
            </div>
          ))}
          <Button
            className="self-start"
            disabled={o.rules.length >= 20}
            onClick={() => setO({ ...o, rules: [...o.rules, { id: Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10), color: COLORS[o.rules.length % COLORS.length], match: { kind: "name", pattern: "" } }] })}
          >
            Add rule
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}
```

(M1b's `Field` has no `hideLabel` (as of 63b66c3): add it in `ui/components/field.tsx`. The label stays in the DOM as `sr-only`, so the input keeps its accessible name "Rule 1 value".)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 11 (`useTestDraft`, `commands.openSignInPage`, `useSignIns`, `useSignOut`, `StoredSignIn`), M1b `useSignInFlow` (`start(deviceCode, tenant)`), M1b `TenantSignInAction` (unchanged).
- Produces:
  - Profile sheet: **Test connection** tests what the form says now (new and edited profiles, without saving); the result line reads "Tested with these settings."; the M1b hint "Save to test these changes." goes away. An edited connection-string profile with an empty field tests with the stored string.
  - Device-code sign-in shows **Open sign-in page** (calls `open_sign_in_page`) beside **Copy code**.
  - Settings gains **Accounts**: every stored sign-in (`user_principal`, tenant, "Current" badge, "Signed out" when its token is gone, "Another account" when `same_account` is false, "Used by …" profiles that connect with it now), **Sign in again** for a signed-out one (signs in to its own tenant) and **Sign out** with an in-place confirmation row (no modal).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`ProfileSheet.tsx`:
- There is no `toDraft` helper yet: M1b builds the draft inline in `onSave`. Extract it into `function toDraft(): ProfileDraft` inside the component (the same fields as now: `id`, trimmed `name`, `auth` with `connection_string: f.replaceSecret || !editing ? f.connectionString.trim() : null`, `production`, `color`, `transport`, `proxy`, `pinned_entities`), and have `onSave` call it, so Save and Test send the same draft.
- Replace `useTestProfile` with `useTestDraft`. The Test button calls `test.mutate(toDraft())` (when editing a connection-string profile without a new string, `connection_string` stays `null` so the engine uses the stored one). It is enabled whenever the form has what a draft needs (a name and, for new connection-string profiles, a string; for Entra, a namespace).
- Keep the result in local state and reset the mutation once it settles, so the draft (with a typed connection string) does not stay in the mutation's `variables` (as `save.reset()` does after saving):

```tsx
const test = useTestDraft();
const [tested, setTested] = useState<{ report: ConnectionReport } | { error: EngineError } | null>(null);
const runTest = () =>
  test.mutate(toDraft(), {
    onSuccess: (report) => setTested({ report }),
    onError: (error) => setTested({ error }),
    onSettled: () => test.reset(),
  });
```

  The result block reads `tested` instead of `test.data` / `test.error`; "Testing…" still follows `test.isPending` (import the `ConnectionReport` and `EngineError` types from `@/engine`).
- Remove the `dirty`-based disabling, the "Save to test these changes." hint and its `aria-describedby`; the result block's first line becomes "Tested with these settings.".
- Changing any field clears the previous result (`setTested(null)`), so a result never describes settings the form no longer has.
- `SignInPanel` and M1b's `TenantSignInAction` in the edit sheet stay as they are (no `tenant` prop).

`SettingsSheet.tsx`, a new section after Version:

```tsx
function Accounts() {
  const q = useSignIns();
  const signOut = useSignOut();
  const flow = useSignInFlow();
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState<string | null>(null);
  if (!q.data?.length) return <p className="m-0 text-12 text-muted">No Entra ID sign-ins. Add an Entra ID connection to sign in.</p>;
  return (
    <ul aria-label="Accounts" className="m-0 flex list-none flex-col gap-2 p-0">
      {q.data.map((s) => (
        <li key={s.id} aria-label={s.user_principal} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="truncate text-12">{s.user_principal}</span>
            {s.current && <Badge>Current</Badge>}
            {!s.same_account && <span className="text-11 text-muted">Another account</span>}
            {!s.signed_in && <span className="text-11 text-muted">Signed out</span>}
            <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
              {!s.signed_in && <Button size="sm" disabled={flow.pending} onClick={() => flow.start(false, s.tenant_id)}>Sign in again</Button>}
              {confirm !== s.id && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setConfirm(s.id)}>Sign out</Button>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <span className="font-mono text-11 text-muted">{s.tenant_id}</span>
          {s.profiles.length > 0 && <span className="text-11 text-muted">Used by {s.profiles.join(", ")}</span>}
          {confirm === s.id && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border border-line px-2 py-1.5">
              <span className="text-12">{s.profiles.length ? `${s.profiles.join(", ")} will need a new sign-in.` : "This sign-in will be forgotten."}</span>
              <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
                <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setConfirm(null)}>Cancel</Button>
                <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => signOut.mutate(s.id, { onSettled: () => setConfirm(null) })}>Sign out {s.user_principal}</Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

rendered as `<Row label="Accounts" hint="Entra ID sign-ins on this computer, one per tenant you signed in to.">` with the list below the label (full width). While `flow.pending`, show `<SignInProgress flow={flow} />` under the list. Import `Badge`, `useSignIns`, `useSignOut`, `useSignInFlow`, `SignInProgress`, `useState`.

`app/e2e/browse.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

const tab = (id: string, kind: string, subTab: string) =>
  encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({ [`shell:p-logistics`]: { tabs: [{ id, kind, subTab }], activeTab: id, expanded: ["group:queues", "group:topics"], selected: null, layout: { treeSize: 18, drawerOpen: true, drawerSize: 22, drawerTab: "activity" } } }));
const open = (page: Page, q: string) => page.goto(`/?${q}`);
const status = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("status", { name: "Peek status" });
const activity = (page: Page) => page.getByRole("region", { name: "Jobs and activity" });

test("dead letters: rows, detail, filter, read-only note", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter")}`);
  const grid = page.getByRole("grid", { name: "Dead-letter queue" });
  await expect(grid.getByText("MaxDeliveryCountExceeded").first()).toBeVisible();
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("37 messages");
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("Peek only, nothing locked");
  await grid.getByRole("row").nth(1).click();
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  const detail = page.getByRole("region", { name: /^Message \d+$/ });
  await expect(detail).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Filter/ }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Body contains").fill("SHP-1000");
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(status(page)).not.toContainText("37 messages");
});

test("12,000 messages stay navigable from the keyboard", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=many-messages&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("customs-docs", "queue", "messages")}`);
  await page.getByRole("combobox", { name: "Messages to read" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: "50,000" }).click();
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("12,000 messages", { timeout: 30_000 });
  const grid = page.getByRole("grid", { name: "Active messages" });
  await grid.getByRole("row").nth(1).click();
  await page.keyboard.press("End");
  await expect(grid.getByRole("row").last()).toContainText("12000");
  expect(await grid.getByRole("row").count()).toBeLessThan(120);
});

test("from the end, then back to paging with Peek more", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync", "subscription", "dead-letter")}`);
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("100 messages");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "End" }).click();
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("Last 100 messages");
  await expect(status(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Peek more" })).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Start" }).click();
  await status(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Peek more" }).click();
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("200 messages");
});

test("export all to a file and see the job finish; cancel a slow one", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter")}`);
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("37 messages");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Export" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Export all…" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Export messages" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Choose file and export" }).click();
  await expect(activity(page).getByText(/Exported 37 messages to invoice-generate-dead-letter-\d{8}-\d{4}\.json/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(activity(page).getByRole("button", { name: "Show in folder" })).toBeVisible();

  await open(page, `scenario=export-slow&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter")}`);
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("37 messages");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Export" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("menuitem", { name: "Export all…" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Export messages" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Choose file and export" }).click();
  await activity(page).getByRole("button", { name: /^Cancel Export/ }).click();
  await expect(activity(page).getByText("Cancelled")).toBeVisible();
});

test("sessions: list, peek one session", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("returns-intake", "queue", "sessions")}`);
  const grid = page.getByRole("grid", { name: "Sessions" });
  await grid.getByRole("row", { name: /RMA-1003/ }).dblclick();
  await expect(page.getByRole("tab", { name: /Messages/, selected: true })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Remove session RMA-1003" })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("4 messages");
});

test("rules are read-only", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("shipment-events/subscriptions/tracking-sync", "subscription", "rules")}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole("listitem", { name: "eu-only" })).toContainText("SET priority = 'high'");
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: /Edit|Delete|Add rule/ })).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("overview counts and copying a connection string shows no secret", async ({ page, browserName }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("invoice-generate", "queue", "overview")}`);
  const counts = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Counts" });
  await expect(counts.getByText("37")).toHaveClass(/text-accent/);
  await page.getByRole("region", { name: "Addresses" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Copy connection string" }).click();
  await expect(activity(page).getByText("Copied the connection string for invoice-generate")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText(/SharedAccessKey=/)).toHaveCount(0);
  if (browserName === "chromium") {
    await page.context().grantPermissions(["clipboard-read", "clipboard-write"]);
    await page.getByRole("region", { name: "Addresses" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Copy URL" }).click();
    expect(await page.evaluate(() => navigator.clipboard.readText())).toBe("sb://sb-logistics-prod.servicebus.windows.net/invoice-generate");
  }
});

test("dashboard: dead letters first, opens an entity", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, "scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics");
  await expect(page.getByRole("tree", { name: "Namespace" })).toBeVisible();
  await page.keyboard.press("ControlOrMeta+Shift+d");
  const grid = page.getByRole("grid", { name: "Dashboard" });
  await expect(grid.getByRole("row").nth(1)).toContainText("tracking-sync");
  await grid.getByText("label-print").dblclick();
  await expect(page.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: "label-print" })).toBeVisible();
});

test("a highlight rule marks tree rows", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, "scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Explorer options" }).click();
  const sheet = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Explorer options for Logistics" });
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Add rule" }).click();
  await sheet.getByLabel("Rule 1 value").fill("^invoice");
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Save" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("treeitem", { name: /invoice-generate/ }).locator('[style*="--label-"]')).toHaveCount(1);
});

test("an unsaved connection can be tested", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, "scenario=empty");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add connection string" }).click();
  const sheet = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Add connection" });
  await sheet.getByLabel("Name").fill("Orders");
  await sheet.getByLabel("Connection string").fill("Endpoint=sb://orders.servicebus.windows.net/;SharedAccessKeyName=reader;SharedAccessKey=c2VjcmV0");
  await sheet.getByRole("button", { name: "Test connection" }).click();
  await expect(sheet.getByText("Tested with these settings.")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText("c2VjcmV0")).toHaveCount(0);
});

test("F5 peeks again without reloading the page", async ({ page }) => {
  await open(page, `scenario=default&autoconnect=p-logistics&seed=${tab("invoice-generate", "queue", "dead-letter")}`);
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("37 messages");
  await page.evaluate(() => ((window as unknown as { marker: number }).marker = 1));
  await page.keyboard.press("F5");
  await expect(status(page)).toContainText("37 messages");
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { marker?: number }).marker)).toBe(1);
});
```

## Self-review notes (for the executor)

- **Spec §4.2 Explorer:** counts choice and colouring by thresholds and name regex → Task 21; dashboard (counts per queue and subscription, sortable, auto-refresh off/30 s/1 min/5 min, DLQ > 0 marked, copy as TSV) → Task 20; copy URL, DLQ URL, entity connection string → Tasks 10, 18, 21; F5 → Tasks 14 (`refresh` resets peeks and the peek round), 23. Deviations recorded as Decisions 13–14: no transfer count kind in the tree, Rules as a sub-tab instead of tree nodes.
- **Spec §4.4 Message browsing:** five views → Task 14; Top N, from a sequence number, from the end, per session, peek more → Tasks 2, 7, 11, 14, 19; property (SQL-like) and body (substring, regex, JSON path) filters → Tasks 1, 15; virtualised grid at 10k+ rows with configurable sortable columns and row numbers → Tasks 12, 14, 23, 24; detail pane (auto-detect, pretty JSON/XML/text, hex, all system and typed custom properties, copy body, copy as JSON) → Tasks 13, 16; export selected/all to JSON or NDJSON as a job → Tasks 3, 9, 17. Receive-and-delete, delete and import stay M2 (read-only rule).
- **Spec §4.6 (read-only part):** list sessions including empty ones where the broker allows → Task 5; peek within one session → Tasks 2, 19. Session state viewing and editing → M2 (needs a session lock).
- **Spec §6:** Overview (properties + live counts) → Task 18; sub-tabs Messages · Dead-letter · Transfer DLQ · Scheduled · Deferred · Sessions · Rules → Tasks 14, 19; Dashboard tab → Task 20; keyboard j/k, F5 → Tasks 12, 14, 23; Delete never deletes (no delete in M1c).
- **Spec §5 safety:** everything read-only; peeks via `peek-message` only; per-session peeks take no lock; verified live in Task 24.
- **Spec §8 errors:** every new view maps engine kinds to plain words with the raw detail (Tasks 14, 16, 18, 19, 20); jobs report `cancelled` without an error (Task 9); selected exports report missing messages (Task 9).
- **Spec §9 testing:** unit tests with fakes and ATOM fixtures (Tasks 1–10), emulator gate `emulator_m1c` in CI (Task 5), Playwright browse flows (Task 23), live checklist (Tasks 24–25).
- **M1b "Left for M1c":** message grid with peek, detail pane, filters, export, Overview, Sessions, Rules, Dashboard → Tasks 14–20; concurrent Entra sign-ins → Task 22 (plus M1b's R35 work); deferred items: `profile_test_draft` → Tasks 10, 22; "Open sign-in page" → Tasks 10, 22 (through the existing `open` crate, not an opener plugin); DataGrid minors (id anchor, activedescendant, O(1) focus index, Shift+J/K, aria-busy and row wrappers) → Task 12; peek TS wrapper → Task 11.
- **Concurrent work on `m1b`:** the branch is still receiving fixes while this plan is written. Before each task, read the files it modifies as they are now; where a snippet's surrounding code has moved on (for example `Profile.bound_sign_in`), keep the plan's behaviour and adapt to the current code.
- **Type names that cross tasks:** `FilterSpec`, `FilterIssue`, `MessageSummary`, `MessageDetail`, `PeekFrom`, `PeekRequest`, `PeekPageView`, `EntityKind` (core) / `EntityKindName` (TS), `EntityDetails`, `RuleInfo`, `RuleFilter`, `SessionListing`, `DashboardView`, `ExportRequest`, `ExportScope`, `JobStarted`, `StoredSignIn` (the Settings list item, Rust and TS; M1b's `SignInView { account, tenant_sign_in }` stays what `sign_in` returns), `ViewState`, `ColumnId`, `GridSort`, `TabKind`, `EntityTab`, `ExplorerOptions`, `HighlightRule`, `CountKind`. Rust command argument names map to camelCase in TS (`profile_id` → `profileId`); payload fields stay snake_case.

## Pre-flight rulings applied

From `.superpowers/sdd/m1c-preflight.md` (checked against `m1b` at 63b66c3):

- H1 → The Settings list item is `StoredSignIn` in Rust and TS (Tasks 10, 11, 22, self-review). M1b's `SignInView` is unchanged.
- H2 → The mock's `sign_ins_list`/`sign_out` use `records`, `live`, `current()` and `boundSignIn` (Task 11 Step 5).
- H3 → The two kept CLI export tests now use `check_output` + core `FileExport`, and the core test `a_fifo_is_written_directly` replaces the FIFO test (Task 3).
- H4 → The anchored-range test presses Shift+K and asserts both selected ids (Task 12).
- H5 → Added `resetViewStates()`, called in `renderShell` and the global `afterEach`, with a fresh-state test (Task 14 files and code).
- M1 → `ProfileStore::sign_ins()` skips unreadable rows and has a damaged-row test (Task 10).
- M2 → `draft_sign_in` uses `usable_sign_in` for edits and `entra_sign_in` for new profiles; `namespace_for_draft` goes through a transient `Profile` + `namespace_for` with a normalised tenant; one test per path (Task 10).
- M3 → Added `same_account`; `profiles` follow `bound_sign_in`; `useSignOut` also invalidates profiles, tenants and discovery; Settings shows "Another account" (Tasks 10, 11, 22).
- M4 → Dropped the SignInPanel `tenant` change. M1b's `TenantSignInAction` meets Decision 11 (Task 22, Decision 11).
- M5 → Rust remembers the dialog's path (`Jobs::set_picked`), and `authorize_overwrite` honours `overwrite` only for that path, once; with a test (Task 9, notes in Task 17 and Review Focus 5).
- M6 → `peek_tail`, `collect_sequences` and the session tally run over `PerBatch`, which takes the AMQP lock per batch (Tasks 2, 5).
- M7 → A session peek's non-2xx except 401/403/404/429/503 is `Unsupported`; the test covers 410/423 (Task 2, Decision 1).
- M8 → The FilterBar draft is `useMemo`'d over its primitive fields (Task 15).
- M9 → `cargo +1.88 check -p dispatched-app --locked` after each `cargo add`; no npm plugin or capability (Tasks 9, 10, Global Constraints).
- M10 → `peek_page`, `entity_details` and `dashboard` use `tracked_failures`, the mock matches, and Overview/Dashboard tests check that nothing is logged (Tasks 7, 8, 11, 18, 20, Global Constraints).
- M11 → The order line now says 9 after 8, 10 after 9, 18 after 14, 19 after 12+14, 20 after 12+14, 21 after 13+18. Task 9 also consumes Task 8.
- L1 → `seqs`/`seq_numbers` are already in `scan.rs` tests at 63b66c3. Task 2 reuses them and adds them only if they are missing.
- L2 → `ViewArgs.session` goes in `commands/browse.rs`, and the `main.rs` test destructures with `..` (Task 6).
- L3 → Files lists fixed: Task 14 adds dropdown-menu.tsx and format.test.ts, Task 16 drops columns.tsx, Task 19 adds the DataGrid files, Task 21 adds field.tsx. `EntityTab` arrives in Task 14, so Task 20 does not retype the views.
- L4 → Task 22 extracts `toDraft()` from `onSave`.
- L5 → The mock invoice-generate generates 1,288 messages (1,284 active). `useFilterCheck` makes no call for an empty filter (Task 11).
- L6 → The "stop keeps the rows" test uses a 20 ms mock (Task 11).
- L7 → Removed `render(<span />)` (Task 19). `list_fails_only_when_both_sources_fail` moved to `sessions.rs` (Task 5).
- L8 → The CLI uses core `is_special` (Task 3). `walk_messages` is shared by `count_messages`/`tally_sessions` (Task 5). The draft namespace goes through `namespace_for` (Task 10). Task 16 uses one `propText` and one `bodyText` decode.

- L11 → Recorded as M1c Decisions 13 (no transfer count kind) and 14 (Rules as a sub-tab); Task 25 writes fourteen decisions.
